### [1] [ISEE: Interactive Semantic Enrichment for Database Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02604)
*Yuan Tian,Yiru Chen,Rakesh R. Menon,Zifan Liu,Ting Cai,Fei Wu,Anudeep Chimakurthi,Prashanthi Ramamurthy,Sridevi Aishwariya Ganesan,Kun Qian,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ISEE（交互式语义丰富系统），通过评分系统衡量数据字段描述质量，收集领域知识并与用户协作丰富语义，以弥补LLM代理在数据任务中因字段描述模糊或不完整导致的性能差距。实验表明ISEE显著降低认知负荷、提升描述质量和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理被广泛用于数据相关任务，但其性能高度依赖数据语义的清晰度和完整性。实践中大量字段描述模糊或不完整，关键上下文（如自定义字段含义）来自用户领域知识且很少公开记录，这限制了代理在下游任务（如实体链接）中的表现。

Method: ISEE系统包含三个核心环节：一是通过评分系统衡量数据字段描述的质量；二是收集领域知识；三是与用户协作式地丰富语义。评估方法包括用户研究、自动化用户模拟、定量评估和案例研究。

Result: 实验证明ISEE显著降低用户的认知负荷，提高字段描述的质量，并增强下游任务（如实体链接）的性能表现。

Conclusion: ISEE有效弥合了LLM代理在数据任务中的语义鸿沟，通过交互式语义丰富机制验证了用户协作在数据语义完善中的价值。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed for data-related tasks, including data sense-making, exploration, and retrieval. However, their performance heavily depends on the clarity and completeness of data semantics. In practice, many field descriptions remain ambiguous or incomplete, as much of the essential context (e.g., the meaning of a customized field) originates from users' domain knowledge and is rarely documented publicly. This gap restricts the agents' task performance in downstream tasks, such as entity-linking. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel and comprehensive Interactive SEmantic Enrichment system (ISEE). Given a data field description, ISEE measures its quality through a scoring system, gathers domain knowledge, and collaboratively enriches the semantics with users. Through a user study, automated user simulation, quantitative evaluation, and case study, we demonstrate that ISEE significantly reduces cognitive load, improves description quality, and enhances downstream task performance.

</details>


### [2] [Self-Organising Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02606)
*Marcello Barylli,Gabriel Béna,Alexander Mordvintsev,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自组织数字电路（Self-Organising Digital Circuits）方法，将功能逻辑生成与维护建模为图上的元学习问题。采用拓扑掩码Transformer配置电路布尔门的查找表（LUT），在退化的布尔搜索空间中导航以满足计算任务，而非再造固定目标状态。该方法能从零开始自组装功能电路，并能在永久性未见硬件故障下快速重新路由逻辑；对软错误，即使在远超训练条件的损伤规模下也能实现近完美的恢复（>99.99%准确率）。此外，观察到跨电路规模的泛化：在比训练时更宽的图上精度反而提高。该工作弥合了生物自组织原理与数字硬件实际应用之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典计算中的容错依赖静态策略（如硬件冗余和纠错码），而生物系统展现出适应性可塑性，通过围绕损伤的动态重组来维持功能。作者受此启发，旨在将生物自组织的自适应容错能力引入数字电路设计，解决硬件故障下的功能维持问题。

Method: 将功能逻辑生成与维护形式化为图上的元学习问题；使用拓扑掩码Transformer配置电路中布尔门的查找表（LUT）；扩展神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式，在退化的布尔搜索空间中导航以满足计算任务（而非再生固定目标状态）。策略经过训练后能够自组装功能电路并对故障进行逻辑重路由。

Result: 能从零开始自组装功能电路；能围绕永久性、先前未见过的硬件故障快速重新路由逻辑；对软错误，即使损伤规模远超训练条件，也能实现近完美恢复（>99.99%准确率）；跨电路规模泛化良好，在比训练时更宽的图结构上精度反而提升。

Conclusion: 该工作桥接了生物自组织原理与数字硬件的实际应用，证明基于图的元学习方法能在数字电路中实现类似生物系统的自适应容错与功能自组装能力。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in classical computing has traditionally relied on static strategies like hardware redundancy and error-correcting codes. Biological systems, in contrast, exhibit adaptive plasticity, maintaining function through dynamic re-organisation around damage. Inspired by this principle, we introduce Self-Organising Digital Circuits, framing functional logic generation and maintenance as a meta-learning problem on graphs. Our architecture employs a topology-masked Transformer that configures the Lookup Tables (LUT) of a circuit's Boolean gates. Extending the pattern-generation paradigm of Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), it navigates the degenerate Boolean search space to satisfy a computational task, rather than regenerating a fixed target state. We demonstrate that it can self-assemble functional circuits from scratch and rapidly re-route logic around permanent, previously unseen hardware faults. For soft errors, the policy achieves near-perfect recovery (>99.99\% accuracy) from damage sizes far exceeding training conditions. We further observe generalisation across circuit scales: accuracy improves on graphs substantially wider than those seen during training. This work bridges the principles of biological self-organisation with the practical domain of digital hardware.

</details>


### [3] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM中存在的"人工蜂群思维"效应（即模型在开放问题上收敛到狭窄同质化共识）提出了一个缓解框架：Meta-Persona Anchoring结合过滤温度缩放（FTS）。该方法通过两阶段生成过程（先自选独特人格锚定起点，再通过Top-p过滤+极端温度缩放探索拓宽的概率分布），在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上将平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，显著降低了语义收敛，缩小了人工模式崩塌与人类多样性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现LLM存在"人工蜂群思维"效应，即使在高温采样下模型仍会在开放问题上收敛于狭窄同质化共识，导致高响应间相似度（约0.80-0.90），限制了AI输出的多样性。

Method: 提出两阶段生成框架：(1) Meta-Persona Anchoring——让模型自我选择独特、个性化的persona作为生成锚点；(2) Filtered Temperature Scaling——采用双阶段采样筛，先用Top-p过滤保证语法有效性，再对幸存候选使用极端温度缩放（T≥4.0）探索加宽的概率分布。

Result: 在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上对约20B参数以内的开源模型进行评估，平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，大多数问题降至0.7阈值以下，显著减少了语义收敛。

Conclusion: 该方案有效缩小了人工模式崩塌与人类类型学多样性之间的差距，并以开源框架形式提供实现，以支持更多样化和创造性的AI部署。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [4] [PULSE: An Executable Contract Language for Spatiotemporal Knowledge Graph Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02630)
*Dongxu Yang,Ziyi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PULSE是一种基于对象过程方法论(OPM)的知识图谱工程语言，将四种操作角色及其写入效应定位在单一类型化运行时中，以局部化跨工件的执行契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱工程中，接受状态、观测、约束、过程和假设场景通常分布在不同工件中，其组合执行契约是外部化的，缺乏统一的运行时语义来约束这些跨工件的交互行为。

Method: 提出PULSE语言，使用模式表示操作角色（而非模态/道义逻辑），实现证据不可覆盖、分支隔离、多主体定时器、守卫状态变更、声明排序的事件排序等契约；核心演算给出效应隔离引理和六项安全属性；Lean 4验证内核类比，并配套88项测试、3534次有界检查和32个Lean/Python运行时内核用例。

Result: 在37,440条生成时序轨迹上与独立工作流匹配并区分十个单字段突变体；在NOAA IBTrACS 1980年以来完整子集上与GEOS和事件扫描在1,476,290对过渡区对上一致；复现了冷链路追踪；通过GeoSPARQL探针测量接口覆盖。

Conclusion: 结果表明契约局部化、安全论证和测试片段的轨迹对等有效；但语言优越性和可用性不在评估范围内。

Abstract: Knowledge graph engineering often distributes accepted state, observations, constraints, processes, and hypothetical scenarios across artifacts whose combined execution contract remains external. We present PULSE, an Object-Process-Methodology-inspired language that localizes four operational roles and their write effects in one typed runtime. Here, modes denote operational roles rather than modal or deontic logic. The implemented contract fixes evidence non-overwrite, branch isolation, grounded multi-subject timers, guarded state change, and declaration-ranked event ordering over time and space; an external runner still decides whether evidence becomes an authoritative move. GeoSPARQL, SOSA, and SHACL remain generated views. A core calculus gives an effect-confinement lemma and six safety properties. Lean 4 checks kernel analogues for positions, evidence, clocks, monitors, atomicity, and branch source retention; 88 tests, 3,534 bounded checks, and 32 Lean/Python runtime-kernel cases bound the implementation claim to the checked cases. First-author implementations of a standards composition and a separate Sismic statechart reproduce the tested cold-chain trace. Across 37,440 generated temporal traces, PULSE matches a separate workflow and distinguishes ten single-field mutants. On the complete NOAA IBTrACS since1980 subset it agrees with GEOS and an event sweep on 1,476,290 transition-zone pairs, including 4,800 sampled and 12,831 duration-qualified events. Project-specific GeoSPARQL probes measure interface coverage. Overall, the results support contract localization, safety arguments, and trace parity for the tested fragment; language superiority and usability remain outside the evaluation.

</details>


### [5] [HyperAgent: Planning and Acting over Tool-Schema Hypergraphs for Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02650)
*Zian Zhai,Xingyu Tan,Gaowang Zou,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperAgent框架，通过构建工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph）来建模工具间的关系，引导LLM智能体进行动态规划与执行。在AppWorld基准上，HyperAgent在提升任务完成性能的同时，减少了冗余API调用、LLM交互次数和Token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖外部工具完成复杂任务，但可靠的工具使用规划仍然具有挑战性：现有方法依赖LLM从文本描述中隐式推断工具组合，导致在复杂任务中探索效率低下且执行不可靠。真实世界执行环境的动态演化进一步加剧了这一问题。

Method: 在模式（schema）层面建模工具关系，构建有向工具-模式超图（工具表示为从输入模式节点到输出模式节点的超边）。HyperAgent框架分为两个阶段：（1）规划阶段：提取任务相关的工具上下文图，引导构建模式感知的任务DAG；（2）执行阶段：通过赤字驱动扩展（deficit-oriented expansion）动态构建状态条件化的工具支持图，识别未解决的需求并根据当前智能体状态检索支持性生产者工具，动态实现每个子任务。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的实验表明，与现有智能体基线相比，HyperAgent在提升任务完成性能的同时，减少了冗余API调用、LLM交互次数和Token消耗。

Conclusion: 在模式层面显式建模工具关系并利用超图结构引导规划与执行，比依赖LLM隐式推理更高效可靠，为工具使用智能体的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external tools to complete complex real-world tasks. However, reliable tool-use planning remains challenging due to the limitations of implicit reasoning and the evolving nature of real-world execution environments. Existing tool-use agents typically rely on LLMs to infer tool compositions from textual descriptions, which can lead to inefficient exploration and unreliable execution in complex tasks. To address these challenges, we model tool relations at the schema level and construct a directed Tool--Schema Hypergraph, in which tools are represented as hyperedges from their required input-schema nodes to their output-schema nodes. Furthermore, we propose HyperAgent, a Tool--Schema Hypergraph-guided framework for dynamic planning and execution. Given a task, HyperAgent first extracts a task-relevant tool context graph and uses it to guide the construction of a schema-aware Task DAG. During execution, HyperAgent dynamically realizes each subtask by constructing a state-conditioned tool support graph through deficit-oriented expansion, which identifies unresolved requirements and retrieves supporting producer tools according to the current agent state. Experiments on AppWorld demonstrate that HyperAgent improves task completion performance while reducing redundant API calls, LLM interactions, and token consumption compared with existing agent baselines.

</details>


### [6] [Explainable AI for the EU Right to Explanation: A Systematic Review of the Law-XAI Translation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02699)
*Benjamin Fresz,Elena Dubovitskaya,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了可解释AI（XAI）与欧盟"解释权"（Right to Explanation）之间的关联，特别聚焦GDPR第15(1)(h)条和AI法案第86条。从2643篇初始文献中审查了57篇全文，仅19篇真正整合了法律与技术视角，揭示了跨学科综述的不足。文章识别出三个问题模式（误认GDPR法律依据、未涉及EU法院Dun & Bradstreet判决、混淆解释形式与内容），并提出"对象/目的框架"（Addressee/Purpose Framework）、四阶段操作化蓝图及六个开放研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法在贷款、招聘、医疗等重大决策中发挥影响时，欧盟法律赋予受影响个体"解释权"。然而XAI能否在实践中满足这一权利尚不清楚，直接影响个体对自动化决策提出异议的能力。作者旨在弥合法律与技术之间的鸿沟，为合规提供可操作的路径。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法：通过刻意宽泛的搜索策略从2643篇初始记录中筛选出57篇全文进行深度审查，重点关注2024年7月AI法案最终版本发布后的文献（因第86条为后期新增），评估论文在法律与技术两个维度的整合程度，并归纳跨语料库的问题模式。

Result: 57篇全文中仅19篇展示了法律与技术视角的实质性整合，表明当前监管框架的跨学科综合存在明显缺口。识别出三个普遍问题模式：多数论文错误识别GDPR法律依据；很少涉及CJEU的Dun & Bradstreet判决（可能因发表时间限制）；解释形式（由接收对象决定）与内容（由法律目的决定）的区分常被混淆。

Conclusion: 作者提出了"对象/目的框架"来概念化解释权和实现合规之间的关系，设计了四阶段操作化蓝图，并明确了六个具体开放研究问题。文章警告，若缺乏进一步进展，"解释权"将沦为形式义务而缺乏技术上可行的合规路径。

Abstract: When algorithms make or influence consequential decisions---about loan eligibility, hiring, or healthcare---EU law grants affected individuals a Right to Explanation. Yet whether (and how) Explainable AI (XAI) can satisfy this right in practice remains poorly understood, with direct implications for individuals' ability to contest automated decisions that affect their lives. This paper presents a systematic literature review of XAI in the context of the EU Right to Explanation, with particular focus on Art. 15(1)(h) GDPR, Art. 86 AI Act (AIA), and related instruments. We consider papers published from 2024 onwards, as the final version of the AIA was published in July 2024---with Art. 86 being added late. From 2643 initial records identified by a deliberately broad search, we review 57 full texts, of which only 19 papers demonstrate substantive integration of both legal and technical perspectives, showing gaps in the interdisciplinary synthesis of the current regulatory framework. We document three problematic patterns across the corpus: Most misidentify the GDPR legal basis; few engage with the CJEU's Dun & Bradstreet judgment (likely due to publication timing); and the distinction between explanation form (governed by addressee) and content (governed by legal purpose) is often conflated. We conceptualize this as the Addressee/Purpose Framework, propose a four-phase blueprint for operationalization, and identify six concrete open research questions. Without further progress, the Right to Explanation risks remaining a formal obligation without a technically realizable path to compliance.

</details>


### [7] [Predictive Set Theory: A Generative Framework for Cognitive Architecture with Operationalized Core Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02704)
*Yiyang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出预测集合论（PST），一个从第一性原理出发的形式化生成框架，通过最小操作集（传感器、集合论状态刷新及三类指称链）重构认知架构，并推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率及有限时域概率规划等核心认知功能，同时为罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性、负反馈基础及电影剪辑理解等经典问题提供新解法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测处理理论虽将大脑描述为分层预测引擎，但缺乏对"预测"结构、预测误差的标准响应及连续更新一致性机制的操作性定义；贝叶斯认知科学将一切不确定性纳入概率信念更新，却预设封闭假设空间，无法生成性解释概率分布对象如何成为离散可识别指称物。两个主流框架均存在根本性概念缺口。

Method: PST从第一性原理出发，将认知架构锚定在最小操作集上：（1）传感器形式化为恒等函数；（2）集合论状态刷新；（3）三类基本指称链——指称、反指称与半指称。在此基础上严格推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率及有限时域概率规划等核心认知功能。该框架不建模神经机制，而是充当任何在不完全信息与不可逆风险下维持内部一致性的系统的设计规范。

Result: PST为罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性的认知地位、负反馈的奠基以及电影剪辑理解等经典问题提供了新颖的解决方案。论文的主要目的在于通过公开学术记录确立PST框架的原创性与完备性。

Conclusion: PST作为一个形式化生成框架，弥补了预测处理理论与贝叶斯认知科学的概念缺口，为认知架构提供了可操作的设计规范，并通过解决多个经典问题显示了其理论解释力；论文旨在公开确立该框架的原创性与完备性。

Abstract: Predictive processing theories portray the brain as a hierarchical prediction engine that minimizes prediction error, yet they lack operational definitions for the structure of a "prediction," the standardized response to a prediction error, and the mechanism that maintains consistency across successive updates. Bayesian cognitive science attempts to subsume all uncertainty under probabilistic belief updating, but it presupposes a closed hypothesis space and provides no generative account of how the objects over which probabilities are distributed become discrete, identifiable referents in the first place. This paper introduces Predictive Set Theory (PST), a formal generative framework that reconstructs cognitive architecture from first principles. PST anchors cognition in a minimal set of operations---a sensor formalized as an identity function, set-theoretic state refresh, and three fundamental forms of reference chains (reference, counter-reference, and semi-reference)---and rigorously derives core cognitive functions including state sequences, demand, comparison, efficiency, and finite-horizon probabilistic planning. Rather than modeling neural mechanisms, PST constitutes a design specification for any system that must maintain internal consistency while acting under incomplete information and irreversible risk. The framework offers novel resolutions to classical problems such as Russell's paradox, the cognitive status of Gödelian incompleteness, the grounding of negative feedback, and the comprehension of film editing. The primary purpose of this paper is to establish, through the public academic record, the originality and completeness of the Predictive Set Theory framework.

</details>


### [8] [Towards a new paradigm of scientific discovery with socialized artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02775)
*Xinjie Yao,Xingxin Xu,Xiyuan Gao,Zhoupeng Guo,Kunlong Yang,Dengyu Zhao,Siqi Zhao,Zhihe Fan,Yichen Dong,Xin Li,Jiekang Feng,Jiahe Wu,Sen Wang,Beiming Yu,Kejia Zhao,Ruipu Zhao,Jiaqi Zhou,Heyang Li,Jianjun Chen,Anbo Dai,Xin Liu,Zhengtao Yu,Qinghua Hu,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BLAZE（Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation）范式，将AI从孤立研究任务的助手重新构思为科学发现的组织化基础设施，通过连接知识、推理、实证验证和人类判断，构建一个持续、累积的研究生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现正面临新的挑战：核心问题不再是产生更多信息，而是如何将不断增长的知识、推理和证据组织成一个连贯的发现过程。现有方法（观察、理论、计算、数据驱动）已不足以应对这一挑战，需要新的范式来整合碎片化的研究活动。

Method: BLAZE将科学智能定义为"社会化科学智能"（socialized scientific intelligence），通过持续性知识、集体推理、实证验证和人类判断的持续交互来组织人类与机器在共享科学过程中的协作，将碎片化活动转化为累积性的探究、批评和修订循环。

Result: BLAZE使科学发现更具可追溯性、可复现性和累积性，同时保留人类的创造力、判断力和责任感，将发现过程从孤立任务扩展为大规模、深层次、连续性的集体科学探究。

Conclusion: 社会化科学智能可能为科学的下一个时代奠定基础，其目的不是替代人类发现，而是扩展集体科学探究的规模、深度和连续性。

Abstract: Scientific discovery has advanced through successive transformations in the organization of knowledge. Observation and experimentation established the empirical foundations of science. Theory made it possible to derive general principles from particular phenomena. Computation extended inquiry into systems beyond direct observation, while data-intensive methods opened new spaces of pattern and prediction. Science now confronts a different frontier. The central challenge is no longer simply to produce more information, but to organize expanding knowledge, reasoning, and evidence into a coherent process of discovery. Here, we introduce Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation (BLAZE), a paradigm of socialized scientific intelligence. BLAZE conceives AI not as an assistant for isolated research tasks, but as an organizational infrastructure for scientific discovery. It connects persistent knowledge, collective reasoning, empirical validation, and human judgment within a continuous research lifecycle, transforming fragmented activities into a cumulative process of inquiry, criticism, and revision. The central premise of BLAZE is that scientific intelligence does not arise from computation alone. It emerges from the sustained interaction among knowledge, hypotheses, experiments, and collective verification. By organizing humans and machines within a shared scientific process, BLAZE makes discovery more traceable, reproducible, and cumulative while preserving human creativity, judgment, and responsibility. Socialized scientific intelligence may provide a foundation for the next era of science. Its purpose is not to replace human discovery, but to extend the scale, depth, and continuity of collective scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [9] [BAP-SQL: Budget-Aware Observation Planning for Agentic Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02876)
*Chong Peng,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Varun Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BAP-SQL将观察形成视为预算控制阶段，通过估计查询风险、按需重写SQL，并将硬限制委托给独立运行时保护机制，从而在预算受限的agentic text-to-SQL任务中提升成功率并节省token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用agent不只是消费观察结果，其行为决定了接下来看到什么。在agentic text-to-SQL中，宽泛的查询会消耗上下文和数据库工作后才出现有用证据，而事后压缩无法恢复遗漏的行或已消耗的工作。

Method: BAP-SQL将观察形成作为预算控制阶段：估计查询风险，在有用时重写SQL，并将硬限制委托给独立的运行时保护机制（runtime shield）。

Result: 在4B通用、FINER-SQL 4B和7B主干网络上，BAP-SQL提升了紧预算成功率。在BIRD衍生设定下，相对于匹配的SFT提升3.4/3.6个百分点，同时节省4.5/5.0%的token。匹配的再训练和任务级迁移将增益归因于策略可见的规划和预算敏感救援。随着模型能力和预算增加，收益衰减，在最宽松设定下反转，且不减少数据库工作。

Conclusion: BAP-SQL通过预算控制式的观察形成策略，在预算受限场景下有效提升agentic text-to-SQL性能，但收益在更宽松或更强模型条件下会减弱甚至反转。

Abstract: Tool-using agents do not merely consume observations: their actions determine what arrives next. In agentic text-to-SQL, a broad query can spend context and database work before useful evidence appears, while post-hoc compression cannot recover omitted rows or expended work. We present BAP-SQL, which treats observation formation as a budget-control stage: it estimates query risk, rewrites SQL when useful, and delegates hard limits to an independent runtime shield. Across general 4B, specialized FINER-SQL 4B, and 7B backbones, BAP-SQL improves tight-budget success. On the primary BIRD-derived setting, it gains 3.4/3.6 percentage points over matched SFT while using 4.5/5.0% fewer tokens. Matched retraining and task-level transfer associate the gain with policy-visible planning and budget-sensitive rescue. The benefit attenuates as model capability and budget increase, reverses at the loosest setting, and does not reduce database work.

</details>


### [10] [VeriTrace: Human-Like Temporal Exploration Completes Agentic Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02878)
*Yu-Tung Liu,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriTrace 是一个多智能体系统，通过让 Inspector 智能体拥有完整的调试动作空间（独立控制信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度），实现了"智能体时间探索"能力，在 VerilogEval-V2 基准上首次达到 100% Pass@1，比最强复现基线提升 5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多智能体系统在标准基准上停滞于约 95% 的准确率，根本原因是调试动作空间不完整——现有系统限制了智能体可检查的信号、可查询的时间窗口或两者兼有，将调试降级为对狭窄预定义视图的模式匹配，而非基于假设的根因分析。

Method: VeriTrace 采用多智能体架构，其 Inspector 智能体在完整的调试动作空间上运行，对信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度进行独立控制。这种被称为"智能体时间探索"(Agentic Temporal Exploration) 的能力使智能体能够形成关于故障原因的假设、查询波形寻找证据并迭代优化理解，模仿人类验证工程师的探索式调试流程。

Result: VeriTrace 在 VerilogEval-V2 基准上达到 100% Pass@1，是该基准上首个达到完美功能正确性的系统。在共享 Claude Sonnet 4.0 骨干模型下，VeriTrace 比最强复现基线高出 5.1% 的准确率。

Conclusion: 调试智能体（debugging agency）是弥合最后准确率差距的关键因素，完整的调试动作空间使自动化 RTL 生成能够达到完美功能正确性，证明探索式的调试能力比单纯扩大模型或增加智能体数量更能突破性能上限。

Abstract: Large language models have shown promise for automated Verilog RTL generation, yet state-of-the-art multi-agent systems plateau at ~95% accuracy on standard benchmarks. We trace this ceiling to an incomplete debugging action space: existing systems restrict which signals the agent can inspect, which time windows it can query, or both, reducing debugging to pattern matching on a narrow, predetermined view of circuit behavior rather than hypothesis-driven root-cause analysis. We present VeriTrace, a multi-agent system whose Inspector agent operates over a complete debugging action space, with independent control over signal selection, time-window bounds, and iteration depth. This capability, which we term Agentic Temporal Exploration, enables the agent to form hypotheses about failure causes, query the waveform for evidence, and refine its understanding iteratively, mirroring the exploratory process of human verification engineers. VeriTrace achieves 100\% Pass@1 on VerilogEval-V2, the first system to attain perfect functional correctness on this benchmark. On a shared Claude Sonnet 4.0 backbone, VeriTrace outperforms the strongest reproduced baseline by +5.1%, demonstrating that debugging agency closes the final accuracy gap.

</details>


### [11] [Interpreting Black-Box Large Language Models with Sentence-Level Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02879)
*Maryam Rezaee,Pooriya Safaei,Maryam Asgarinezhad,Fatemeh Seyyedsalehi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的、事后句子级归因解释器，用于解释仅通过闭源API访问的专有大型语言模型。该方法训练能量基模型（EBM）作为代理，捕捉LLM在提示与响应之间的内部概念一致性，并利用能量景观指导轻量级解释器网络的训练。该解释器独立运行，训练后无需再向LLM发起API查询即可量化提示句子对目标输出的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有大型语言模型仅能通过封闭API访问，缺乏可解释性，导致无法负责任地部署。现有解释方法通常需要白盒访问或频繁查询API，不适用于闭源模型场景。

Method: ①训练能量基模型（EBM）作为代理，捕捉LLM提示与响应之间的概念一致性；②利用能量景观训练轻量级解释器网络；③解释器在训练后作为独立工具运行，无需额外API查询；④通过全局训练本地解释器跨多样输入捕捉更广泛的生成模式，缓解实例特定偏差。

Result: 实验表明EBM能准确模拟目标LLM，使解释器能有效识别对生成特定目标输出最关键的提示句子。

Conclusion: 该方法为闭源LLM提供了一种无需白盒访问的句子级归因解释方案，兼具模型无关性与推理效率，可在训练后零API查询方式下实现解释功能。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) accessed strictly through closed APIs has created a critical challenge for responsible deployment: a fundamental lack of interpretability. To address this, we propose a model-agnostic, post-hoc attribution interpreter operating at the sentence level. Our approach trains an Energy-Based Model (EBM) as a surrogate to capture the LLM's internal conceptual consistency between prompts and responses. This energy landscape guides the training of a lightweight interpreter network. Uniquely, our interpreter operates as a standalone tool; once trained, it quantifies the influence of prompt sentences on a user-specified target output without requiring further API queries to the LLM. By globally training a local interpreter across diverse inputs, our framework captures broader generation patterns and mitigates instance-specific biases. Experiments demonstrate that our EBM accurately simulates the target LLM, allowing the interpreter to effectively identify the prompt sentences most influential in generating specific target outputs.

</details>


### [12] [Hypercubes, Hyperplanes, and Constraint-Induced Complexity Collapse in Atomic Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02930)
*Irene Tsapara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过超立方体和超平面的几何视角重新审视高元原子概念学习，发现环境的r维超立方体在结构上并非均匀：除全对角线外的每个超平面在有限多个初等等价类中坍塌（其类数有界，与项深度无关），而全对角线是例外，其类数不受约束地增长。作者基于其早期工作的高维框架，通过规范简单概念、最小序和代表约简重新诠释这些结果，建立了高维超平面行为的分类学，表明复杂度是局部化的而非均匀分布在实例空间中。论文包含了完整的二元案例、三元超立方体的显式处理以及约简机制的详细说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高元原子概念学习中，底层高维几何结构（超立方体与超平面）的非均匀性未被充分认识。作者希望通过揭示超平面坍塌现象（即多数超平面的初等等价类数量有界，而全对角线例外）来定位复杂性的本质来源，从而为概念学习的理论分析提供新的几何-逻辑视角。

Method: 基于作者早期提出的高维框架，利用规范简单概念、最小序和代表约简等工具，对超立方体中的超平面行为进行分类学研究。方法包括：完整的二元案例分析、三元超立方体的显式构造、约简机制的详细展开，以展示正交族、部分对角线和例外全对角线等现象。

Result: 证明了环境的超立方体在逻辑结构上的非均匀性：除全对角线外的超平面都会坍塌为有限多个初等等价类，且上界与项深度无关；全对角线是唯一例外，其类数无界增长。该不对称性反映了概念本身的约简-理论结构。三维案例已展现出正交族、部分对角线和例外全对角线等核心现象，说明复杂度集中于特定区域而非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 复杂度在原子概念学习中是局部化的，集中于特定几何结构（如全对角线及其附近）。这一几何-逻辑视角为在约束假设空间和结构化分类的框架下理解概念学习提供了新解释，并为后续高维研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We revisit higher-arity atomic concept learning through the geometry of hypercubes and hyperplanes of ground instances. Our starting point is the observation that the ambient r-dimensional hypercube of ground atoms is not structurally uniform. Its logical complexity is organized by hyperplanes: every hyperplane other than the full diagonal collapses into finitely many elementary-equivalence classes, with a bound independent of the term depth, while the full diagonal is exceptional and its class count grows without bound. This asymmetry is not merely geometric. It reflects the reduction-theoretic structure of the concepts themselves. Building on a higher-dimensional framework developed in the author's earlier work, we reinterpret these results through canonical simple concepts, minimal orderings, and representative reductions. This yields a taxonomy of hyperplane behavior in higher dimensions and shows that complexity is localized rather than spread uniformly through the instance space. The paper includes a fully worked binary case, an explicit treatment of the ternary hypercube, and an unpacked account of the reduction machinery that drives the collapse. The three-dimensional case already exhibits the essential phenomenon of orthogonal families, partial diagonals, and the exceptional full diagonal. This geometric-logical perspective clarifies where complexity is concentrated in atomic concept learning and suggests a modern interpretation in terms of constrained hypothesis spaces and structured classification.

</details>


### [13] [When Compression Scores Cannot Decide: Information Boundaries for Group-Robust LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02940)
*Andrew Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现一个高可复现性的压缩评分（split-half信度0.906）在端点评选时仍可能选出次优候选（比两个对照差6.0%和7.7%），并通过"信息接口"理论建模该差距。作者提出了锥形定律精确刻画等权组下正线性固定候选损伤的池化代价，利用三种两世界构造和观测纤维半径刻画池化矩、组局部矩与参考路径曲率未解决的不确定性，并提出粗粒度深度分配和模型专属完整掩码端点选择策略，在三个稠密LLM上将最差组困惑度膨胀降低12.6-20.9%，端点选择提升2.7-8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩统计量往往只关注可复现性（reliability），但高可复现性并不保证选择出正确的候选端点。作者旨在建模"评分高分但选择错误"这一现象的产生机理，并给出在何种条件下压缩统计量可以合法授权端点选择的判据。

Method: 通过"信息接口"界定每种统计量所支持的区分能力；对等权组建立锥形定律（conic law），给出正线性固定候选损伤的精确池化代价（含对角和满秩PSD二阶矩）；构造三种两世界（two-world）构造与观测纤维半径，表征池化矩、组局部矩和参考路径曲率未解决的不确定性；提出组解析对角（group-resolved diagonal）恢复粗粒度损伤顺序（Spearman 0.9239），以及粗粒度深度分配与模型专属完整掩码端点选择策略；利用OLMoE路由痕迹预测单例方向（114/192对81/192）。

Result: 相对均匀分配，粗深度分配在三个稠密LLM上将最差组困惑度膨胀降低12.6-20.9%；模型专属完整掩码端点选择在对照基础上提升2.7-8.0%；OLMoE中路由痕迹对单例方向的预测优于最强重标记基线（114/192 vs 81/192）；单层有限菜单决策在留出集上使最差组KL降低13.7%和7.2%。

Conclusion: 局部测量只能用于构造候选，而端点选择性须由完整候选端点或经过验证的均匀性保证来授权，且不确定性须针对每次比较进行校准。该框架为压缩评分的选择合法性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A reproducible compression statistic can still select the wrong candidate. A dense pruning score with 0.906 split-half reliability predicted a 16.1% gain. Its selected endpoint was 6.0% and 7.7% worse than two controls. We model the gap through information interfaces that delimit which distinctions each statistic supports. For equal-weight groups, a conic law gives the exact pooling price for positive linear fixed-candidate damage, including diagonal and full PSD second moments. Three two-world constructions and an exact observation-fiber radius characterize what pooled moments, group-local moments, and reference-path curvature leave unresolved. A group-resolved diagonal recovers broad damage order (Spearman 0.9239) while fine order remains weak. Relative to balanced uniform allocation, a coarse depth allocation cuts worst-group perplexity inflation by 12.6--20.9% across three dense LLMs. Model-specific complete-mask endpoint selection improves over those references by 2.7--8.0%. In OLMoE, router traces predict singleton direction (114/192 versus 81/192 under the strongest relabeling). Finite-menu decisions on one layer yield held-out worst-group KL reductions of 13.7% and 7.2%. Local measurements construct candidates. Selection is licensed by complete candidate endpoints or a validated uniform guarantee, with uncertainty calibrated to every comparison.

</details>


### [14] [On the missing data layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02949)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DataHub——一个面向拉丁美洲AI基础设施的"任务优先"数据基础设施，通过/<任务?>/<领域?>/<语言?>的层级本体组织数据集，解决该地区数据集发现难、供给不足的双重问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺少AI基础设施的两个基础层：数据集层和基准层。数据集层面临两个叠加的问题：发现（数据集分散在各平台，无共享索引）和供给（即使索引完善，总体数据量仍远低于前沿AI开发所需）。

Method: 提出DataHub：一种任务优先的数据基础设施，通过本体层级/<任务?>/<领域?>/<语言?>组织数据，并提供数据集发现、元数据、贡献、许可和复用的机制。

Result: 论文聚焦于数据集层，提出了DataHub这一基础设施方案，从架构层面解决发现与供给两大问题，但尚未展示具体的实验或实证结果。

Conclusion: DataHub旨在填补拉丁美洲AI数据集层的空白，通过标准化的任务优先本体和完整的生态系统机制（发现、元数据、贡献、许可、复用）来实现数据基础设施的搭建。

Abstract: Latin America is missing two foundational layers of AI infrastructure: the dataset layer and the benchmark layer. This paper targets the dataset layer. The dataset layer faces two compounding problems: discovery and supply. Latin American AI datasets exist but are scattered across platforms with no shared index. Even with perfect indexing, the total volume would remain far below what frontier AI development requires. We propose DataHub: a task-first data infrastructure organized through the ontology /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>, with mechanisms for dataset discovery, metadata, contribution, licensing, and reuse.

</details>


### [15] [Neurosymbolic Reasoning with Incremental Knowledge for Sample Efficient Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02993)
*Subrat Prasad Panda,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号分层强化学习框架InK（增量知识），通过符号高层组件在可更新的增量知识表示上进行符号规划，配合低层目标条件神经网络模块通过经验学习运动基元，在稀疏奖励长时域导航任务中显著提升样本效率；同时提出Belief World Tree Search实现基于先验知识的最优符号规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扁平强化学习在稀疏奖励、需要长时域推理的环境中面临巨大挑战。现有分层强化学习（HRL）将知识以固定不可更新的形式编码（如架构选择），无法利用探索过程中增量获得的知识进行推理，导致样本效率低下。

Method: 提出神经符号HRL框架InK：1）符号高层组件在可更新的增量知识表示上进行符号规划（如使用D*算法）；2）低层目标条件的神经网络模块通过经验学习运动基元，并利用奖励塑造加速训练；3）开发Belief World Tree Search（BWTS）在给定先验知识时进行最优符号规划。

Result: 在导航任务上的实验表明，引入增量知识（InK）显著提升样本效率。

Conclusion: 将符号规划与增量知识更新机制引入分层强化学习，可有效解决固定HRL无法利用探索中获取的新知识进行推理的问题，从而显著改善稀疏奖励环境下的样本效率。

Abstract: (Flat) Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents face significant challenges in environments with sparse rewards that require long-horizon reasoning. A compelling approach to improve sample efficiency is to incorporate knowledge into learning and decision-making. In standard Hierarchical RL (HRL), knowledge is encoded in a fixed, non-updatable form, such as architectural choices, and remains unchanged throughout learning. With fixed HRL, reasoning with incremental knowledge learned during exploration is impractical before sufficient environmental knowledge is acquired, leading to poor sample efficiency. In this work, we propose neurosymbolic HRL with {\em Incremental Knowledge (InK)}: symbolic high-level components perform {\em symbolic planning} (e.g. using $D^*$) on an updatable representation of current InK, while low-level goal-conditioned neural modules learn motion primitives through experience using reward shaping. Experiments on navigation tasks demonstrate that incorporating InK substantially improves sample efficiency. Additionally, to perform {\em optimal} symbolic planning given {\em prior} knowledge about the world, we develop Belief World Tree Search. The code is available at https://github.com/CPS-research-group/ink_bwts.

</details>


### [16] [On the missing benchmarks layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02996)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出拉丁美洲缺乏AI基准层（benchmark layer）这一基础性缺失，并提出了EvalsHub——一个开放、任务优先的基准基础设施，其首个区域实例为LatamBoard，使大学、公共机构、专业社区和企业能够发布、执行、比较和维护跨模型、工作流及智能体的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺少AI原生开发的基础层——基准层。该层承担两项不可替代的功能：对AI系统进行区域社会需求审计，以及在经济学相关的环境中引导AI优化方向。没有这一层，公共机构无法独立评估外国AI系统，企业也无法以SOTA性能优化AI以解决本地问题。缺失的代价是双重的：丧失可审计性和丧失优化方向性，而AI正在成为日益关键的基础设施。

Method: 提出EvalsHub及其首个区域实例LatamBoard——一个开放、任务优先的基准基础设施。该平台支持大学、公共机构、专业社区和企业发布、执行、比较和维护评估，覆盖模型、工作流和智能体。其核心理念为"一次构建、永久测量"，由机构在新AI系统发布时重跑，由行业团队在每次系统变更后重跑；通过开放设计和激励驱动机制构建。

Result: 论文未提供实证结果，属于基础设施提案类工作，旨在填补拉丁美洲AI基准层的空白。

Conclusion: 提出EvalsHub/LatamBoard作为弥补拉丁美洲AI基准层缺失的方案，通过开放的任务优先基准基础设施，同时解决可审计性（面向公共机构）和优化方向性（面向企业）的双重问题。

Abstract: Latin America is missing a foundational layer for native AI development: the benchmark layer. The benchmark layer does two things no other layer can - it audits AI systems against regional social requirements and it directs AI optimization in economically relevant environments. Without it, public institutions cannot independently evaluate foreign AI systems, and companies cannot optimize AI systems to solve local problems with SOTA performance. The cost of the missing layer is dual: a loss of auditability and a loss of optimization direction over a technology that is increasingly critical infrastructure. We propose an EvalsHub, with LatamBoard as its first regional instance - an open, task-first benchmark infrastructure where universities, public institutions, professional communities, and companies can publish, execute, compare, and maintain evaluations across models, workflows, and agents. Built once, measured forever - re-run by institutions as new AI systems ship and by industry teams after every system change. Open by design and incentive-driven by construction.

</details>


### [17] [ProPRL: Property-Aware Prerequisite Relation Learning in Educational Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03006)
*Xinghe Cheng,Jiapu Wang,Chaobo He,Ruihai Dong,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProPRL是一个属性感知的前置关系学习框架，通过概念-资源超图和定向学习行为图学习互补表示，利用配对条件门控自适应融合，并引入不可逆约束惩罚双向预测矛盾，在多个教育数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前置关系学习方法通常将问题简单化为常规的链接预测，无法自适应地整合候选概念对互补的教育证据，也难以抑制相互矛盾的反向预测，限制了自适应教学的效果。

Method: ProPRL包含三个核心模块：(1) 从概念-资源超图和定向学习行为图中学习互补的概念表示，其中方向保持的个性化传播聚合多跳行为证据；(2) 配对条件门控（Pair-conditioned Gate）为每个候选有序概念对自适应加权融合两个视图；(3) 不可逆约束（Irreversibility Constraint）通过反对称正则化惩罚同一概念对在两个方向上都给出高置信度的预测。

Result: 在多个真实世界教育数据集上的实验表明，ProPRL在前置关系学习任务上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过属性感知的表示学习、配对条件自适应用户融合和反对称约束，ProPRL有效解决了前置关系学习中证据整合和反向预测矛盾的问题，验证了自适应教学场景下前置关系建模的有效改进方向。

Abstract: Prerequisite relation learning is central to adaptive instruction, yet existing methods often formulate it as conventional link prediction, limiting their ability to adaptively integrate complementary educational evidence for individual candidate pairs and to discourage contradictory reverse predictions. We propose ProPRL, a Property-aware Prerequisite Relation Learning framework. ProPRL first learns complementary concept representations from a concept-resource hypergraph and a directed learning-behavior graph, where direction-preserving personalized propagation aggregates multi-hop behavioral evidence. It then employs a Pair-conditioned Gate to adaptively weight and fuse the two views for each candidate ordered concept pair. Finally, an \textit{Irreversibility Constraint} introduces an anti-symmetry regularizer that penalizes simultaneously high confidence in both directions of the same concept pair. Experiments on multiple real-world educational datasets show that ProPRL achieves state-of-the-art performance on prerequisite relation learning.

</details>


### [18] [UrbanAgent: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Cross-System Urban Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03018)
*Jiayu Cao,Xingyuan Zeng,feiyu Li,Zhijing Huang,Xujie Yuan,Rongxiang Chen,Shimin Di,Libin Zheng,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Urban-Agent，一个面向跨系统城市任务的工具增强型智能体框架，结合大语言模型推理能力与代码执行、API调用、Model Context Protocol等工具集，通过自适应闭环实现信息澄清、工具使用落地和结果对齐。同时引入Urban-Eval基准，评估任务结果与执行质量。实验显示Urban-Agent任务成功率达71%，超过最强基线10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代城市依赖日益增多的数字服务，但服务碎片化且互操作性差，给居民带来沉重操作负担。现有数字平台、城市基础模型和智能助手只解决城市任务的孤立方面，难以将复杂的自然语言请求可靠转换为可执行的跨系统工作流，存在评估空白。

Method: Urban-Agent将大语言模型的认知与推理能力与工具集（代码执行、API调用、Model Context Protocol）耦合，通过自适应闭环机制：在执行前澄清缺失信息，将工具使用落地于实时观测，并将最终响应与观测证据和任务约束对齐。同时构建Urban-Eval基准，从任务结果和执行质量两个维度评估，涵盖工具覆盖度、依赖有效性和证据可追溯性。

Result: Urban-Agent在Urban-Eval上达到71%的任务成功率，比最强基线高出10个百分点。该优势在GPT-5-mini、Gemini-2.5-flash、DeepSeek-V4-flash和Qwen3-235B-A22B等多个模型中保持一致。

Conclusion: Urban-Agent通过工具增强与自适应闭环机制有效解决了跨系统城市任务的复杂性，填补了该领域评估基准的空白。多模型验证表明其泛化能力，为城市智能服务提供了一种可靠的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Modern cities rely on an increasing number of digital services to operate, but residents' daily needs are still difficult to meet. Services are fragmented and have little interoperability, placing a heavy operational burden on users. Existing digital platforms, urban foundation models, and intelligent assistants each address only isolated aspects of an urban task. But they struggle to reliably convert complex natural-language requests into executable cross-system workflows. We propose Urban-Agent, a tool-augmented agent framework for cross-system urban tasks. It couples the cognitive and reasoning capabilities of a large language model with a tool-set supporting code execution, API calls, and Model Context Protocol. Through one adaptive closed loop, it clarifies missing information before acting, grounds tool use in live observations, and aligns the final response with observed evidence and task constraints. To address the evaluation gap, we introduce Urban-Eval, a benchmark specifically designed for cross-system urban request. Unlike prior benchmarks that assess either general tool use or urban knowledge and reasoning, Urban-Eval evaluates both task results and execution quality, including required tool coverage, dependency validity, and evidence traceability. Experimental results indicate that Urban-Agent reaches a 71% task success rate, 10 points above the strongest baseline. This lead holds across GPT-5-mini, Gemini-2.5-flash, DeepSeek-V4-flash, and Qwen3-235B-A22B.

</details>


### [19] [LoCA: Forward-Only LLM Tuning after One-Shot Calibration with Local Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03020)
*Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Zhaofeng Zhang,Yihao Wang,Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoCA提出两阶段小偏移适配方法，通过一次校准的后向传播将全局梯度分配摊销为逐block的低秩映射，之后仅靠前向传播和闭式脊回归即可完成适配，无需重复端到端反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效后训练虽然减少了可训练参数，但仍需对冻结骨干网络进行重复端到端反向传播，这要求反向传播硬件且需存储或重算激活。作者问能否用一次性校准替代这个重复反向链。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 一次探针反向传播在每个transformer block拟合从最终预测误差到局部隐状态修正的低秩映射；(2) 复用这些映射从前向激活生成逐块回归目标，并用闭式脊回归拟合低秩适配器。整个过程中除校准外不需要任何骨干反向传播。

Result: 在Qwen2.5 (0.5B-14B)的5个判别基准的25个任务-规模对比中，LoCA在16个案例中取得比LoRA更低的评估交叉熵。完整运行的GPU峰值内存（含校准）比LoRA低26-29%；校准后CPU稳态内存低36-52%，每次前向耗时低43-48%。候选集在Qwen2.5各规模和SmolLM2-1.7B上可复用。

Conclusion: LoCA将全局信用分配摊销为一次校准过程，实现后续纯前向调优，在内存和速度上有显著优势，且多数情况下精度不逊于LoRA，适用于重复反向传播不可行的场景。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient post-training reduces the number of trainable parameters, but still requires repeated end-to-end backpropagation through the frozen backbone. Every adaptation step therefore needs backward-capable hardware and must store or recompute activations. We ask whether this repeated backward chain can be replaced by a one-time calibration. We introduce Local Credit Assignment (LoCA), a two-stage method for small-shift adaptation. One probe backward pass fits a low-rank map at each transformer block from the final prediction error to a local hidden-state correction. LoCA then reuses these maps to form blockwise regression targets from forward activations and fits low-rank adapters with closed-form ridge solves. No further backbone backward pass is required. We evaluate LoCA on five discriminative benchmarks with Qwen2.5 models from 0.5B to 14B. In 16 of 25 reported task--scale comparisons, LoCA yields lower evaluation cross-entropy than the corresponding LoRA run. Its measured full-run GPU peak, including calibration, is 26--29\% lower than LoRA's. After calibration, its CPU steady-state memory is 36--52\% lower and its per-pass time is 43--48\% lower. A shared scale-normalized candidate set is reused across all tested Qwen2.5 sizes and on SmolLM2-1.7B. LoCA thus amortizes global credit assignment into one calibration and enables later forward-only tuning when repeated backpropagation is impractical. The code associated with this paper is available \href{https://github.com/Xia12121/LoCA}{here}.

</details>


### [20] [DiffImaginE: Imagine to Verify Entity Types with Diffusio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03025)
*Feng Zhang,Feiyu Han,Rongxin Yang,Yang Liu,Yancheng Chen,Rui Wang,Yingguang Yang,Tian Xueyun,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Xu Kefu,Congjing Ran,Fuhai Chen,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffImaginE，将多模态命名实体识别（MNER）中的类型验证任务形式化为条件潜变量扩散推理问题，用基于扩散的去噪误差作为类型条件负对数似然的代理分数来对候选实体类型进行排序，在Twitter-2015和Twitter-2017数据集上优于匹配的确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的imagine-and-compare验证器将每个候选（span, type）对映射到单一的预测视觉特征，将多样的视觉实现压缩成一个原型，且仅提供兼容性分数而无显式概率语义，缺乏对视觉证据多样化表达和不确定性建模的能力。

Method: DiffImaginE将MNER类型验证建模为条件潜扩散推断：以span局部视觉证据为条件，类型条件去噪器预测注入到标准化潜变量中的噪声，去噪误差提供ELBO一致的、类型条件负对数似然的代理分数；采用classifier-free引导的扩散评分器，使用Min-SNR加权训练，直接将每类型扩散分数作为分类logits监督，学习跨噪声水平的聚合，并使用对偶采样（antithetic sampling）降低蒙特卡洛比较方差。

Result: 在Twitter-2015和Twitter-2017上，相对匹配的确定性ImaginE对照组（相同的编码器、辅助目标与评估协议）取得一致的性能提升，并辅以消融实验和配对显著性检验验证。

Conclusion: 分析表明classifier-free引导能使诱导的类型后验更尖锐，且对偶采样在等去噪器成本下能降低方差；扩散式概率验证框架为MNER类型验证提供了更强的判别能力与可解释的概率语义。

Abstract: Multimodal named entity recognition (MNER) determines whether each candidate span and entity-type hypothesis is supported by joint textual and visual evidence. Existing imagine-and-compare verifiers map each (span, type) pair to one predicted visual feature, compressing diverse visual realisations into a single prototype and providing a compatibility score without explicit probabilistic semantics. We introduce DiffImaginE, which formulates MNER type verification as conditional latent diffusion inference. Given span-localised visual evidence, a type-conditioned denoiser predicts noise injected into its standardised latent. The resulting denoising error provides an ELBO-consistent surrogate for type-conditional negative log-likelihood, allowing competing type hypotheses to be ranked by how well they explain the observation. DiffImaginE retains a standard multimodal encoder stack and replaces the deterministic verifier with a classifier-free-guided diffusion scorer trained using Min-SNR weighting. We directly supervise per-type diffusion scores as classification logits, learn aggregation across noise levels, and use antithetic sampling to reduce Monte Carlo comparison variance. Our analysis shows that classifier-free guidance sharpens the induced type posterior and characterises when antithetic pairing reduces variance at equal denoiser cost. Experiments on Twitter-2015 and Twitter-2017 show consistent gains over a matched deterministic ImaginE control under the same encoder, auxiliary objectives, and evaluation protocol, supported by ablations and paired significance tests.

</details>


### [21] [Evaluating Counterfactual Sensitivity to Patient Information in Medication-Safety Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03028)
*Zhitian Hou,Yuhang Liu,Pengkai Wang,Zeyu Liu,Guanghao Zhu,Zheng Liu,Shuo Cai,Congkai Xie,Zhijie Sang,Kun Zeng,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedPIC-Bench基准，用于评估大型语言模型在患者个体化用药安全推理中的表现。该基准包含467个专家验证问题，通过成对的反事实问题检验模型是否真正利用患者信息来判断安全规则是否适用。实验发现所有28个模型在反事实问题上的表现都更差（平均准确率从63.6%降至45.1%），且模型虽然承认患者信息变化，但最终答案仍保留原有安全判断，说明静态用药安全准确率高估了模型的真实可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学评估大多使用孤立、固定的场景，模型可能仅凭回忆药物-风险关联就正确回答，而并未真正利用患者信息判断安全规则是否适用。这导致静态准确率无法反映模型在条件化规则应用上的真实能力，存在评估盲区。

Method: 构建MedPIC-Bench基准：包含来源可验证的推荐意见和专家验证的问题，将指南遵循问题与成对的反事实问题结合——通过受控改变患者信息来改变规则适用性。基准包含467个问题，按六个临床和推理维度进行标注，并评估28个医学专用、通用和专有LLM。

Result: 所有模型在反事实问题上表现均更差，平均准确率从63.6%降至45.1%。模型在显式患者属性直接提示熟悉禁忌症时表现良好，但在患者信息需要缩小或撤销安全警告时表现挣扎。模型推理过程常识别到患者信息的变化，但最终答案仍保持先前的安全判断。医学专用LLM的平均反事实表现甚至落后于通用LLM。

Conclusion: MedPIC-Bench使得条件化规则应用变得可测量，揭示了静态用药安全准确率在评估患者特异性可靠性方面的局限性，表明当前LLM在真实临床场景中尚不能可靠地进行个体化用药安全判断。

Abstract: Applying a valid medication-safety rule when its patient-specific conditions are not met can produce an incorrect decision. Existing medical evaluations largely use isolated and fixed scenarios. A model may therefore answer correctly by recalling a drug-risk association without showing that it used patient information to decide whether the rule applies. To address this gap, we introduce MedPIC-Bench, a benchmark of source-verifiable recommendations and expert-validated questions for patient-specific medication-safety reasoning. It combines guideline-following questions with paired counterfactual questions in which a controlled change in patient information changes whether a rule applies. The benchmark contains 467 questions annotated along six clinical and reasoning dimensions. Across 28 medical-specific, general, and proprietary LLMs, every model performs worse on counterfactual questions, with mean accuracy falling from 63.6\% to 45.1\%. Models perform well when an explicit patient attribute directly signals a familiar contraindication, but struggle when patient information must narrow or withdraw a safety warning. Model rationales often acknowledge the changed patient information, yet the final answers retain the previous safety judgment. This vulnerability persists among medical-specific LLMs, whose average CF performance trails that of general LLMs. MedPIC-Bench therefore makes conditional rule application measurable and highlights the limitations of static medication-safety accuracy for assessing patient-specific reliability.

</details>


### [22] [CastFSR: A Fast--Slow--Reflect Agentic Reasoning Framework for Context-Aware Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03031)
*Xiaoyu Tao,Mingyue Cheng,Bokai Pan,Chuang Jiang,Huanjian Zhang,Tian Gao,Yaguo Liu,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CastFSR，一个将上下文感知时间序列预测建模为"快速-慢速-反思"工作流的智能体框架，通过快速思考构建数据驱动预测先验、慢速推理检索上下文证据并调整预测、反思阶段迭代优化预测结果，支持免训练的现成LLM推理和两阶段微调+强化学习的轻量部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时间序列预测方法缺乏显式机制来识别相关上下文、推理其影响，并验证预测是否符合时间和领域约束。

Method: 提出CastFSR框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 快速思考：分析观测并选择轻量预测器构建数据驱动的预测先验；(2) 慢速推理：检索上下文证据、自适应确定信息性回顾窗口，并推理上下文如何重塑未来动态；(3) 反思：迭代优化预测以满足时间、上下文和领域一致性。同时支持免训练的现成LLM推理，以及通过两阶段SFT+强化学习将编排能力迁移到紧凑型LLM。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验表明，CastFSR持续优于代表性基线。代码已开源。

Conclusion: CastFSR通过显式的Fast-Slow-Reflect工作流，为上下文感知时间序列预测提供了一种有效且可部署的智能体化解决方案，兼顾了推理能力和部署效率。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is fundamental to decision-making in complex systems, where future dynamics are influenced not only by historical observations but also by evolving contextual features. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have extended forecasting beyond numerical extrapolation toward context-aware reasoning. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to identify relevant contexts, reason about their impacts, and validate forecasts against temporal and domain constraints. In this work, we propose CastFSR, an agentic framework that formulates context-aware forecasting as a Fast--Slow--Reflect workflow. In fast thinking, CastFSR profiles observations and selects lightweight forecasters to construct a data-driven forecast prior. In slow deliberation, it retrieves contextual evidence, adaptively determines informative look-back windows, and reasons about how contexts reshape future dynamics. In reflection, it iteratively refines forecasts to ensure temporal, contextual, and domain consistency. CastFSR supports both training-free inference with off-the-shelf LLMs and efficient deployment through a two-stage SFT and reinforcement learning strategy that transfers its orchestration capability to compact LLMs. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate that CastFSR consistently outperforms representative baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xiaoyu-Tao/CastFSR.

</details>


### [23] [TraceCAD: Trace-Guided Repair for Agentic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03062)
*Fengxiao Fan,Jingzhe Ni,Fan Sang,Xiaolong Yin,Yu Liu,Ruofeng Tong,Min Tang,Peng Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceCAD是一个恢复层，通过持久化追踪LLM驱动CAD代理的修复过程（包括需求链接、建模步骤、失败证据和候选结果），实现本地化故障诊断、有界编辑搜索和可复用技能记忆，显著提升CAD模型生成的最终质量和修复可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的CAD代理虽然能生成可执行参数化程序，但其修正循环会丢失关于已满足需求、故障操作和先前修复的证据，导致修复过程低效且不可靠。

Method: TraceCAD作为恢复层，将请求特征、建模步骤、失败证据和候选结果作为持久状态关联起来。它诊断疑似故障操作，在有界编辑范围内搜索依赖区域，通过执行和保留检查验证候选方案，并将成功与失败的修复结果保存为可复用的技能记忆。

Result: 在DeepCAD基准上进行200模型消融和1K模型对比实验中，TraceCAD在IoU、Chamfer距离和Hausdorff距离方面达到有竞争力的几何质量。移除持久状态使恢复分数几乎减半；移除本地化搜索使几何回归翻倍以上并使代码代理调用数翻倍。在不相交训练模型上初始化技能库可进一步减少重试、令牌成本和延迟。

Conclusion: 持久化、本地化和可复用的恢复机制能够提升最终CAD质量和修复可靠性，验证了TraceCAD在LLM驱动的CAD代理修复循环中的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based CAD agents produce executable parametric programs, but their correction loops may lose evidence about satisfied requirements, faulty operations, and prior repairs. We introduce TraceCAD, a recovery layer that links requested features, modeling steps, failure evidence, and candidate outcomes as persistent state. TraceCAD diagnoses likely faulty operations, searches bounded edits in their dependency regions, validates candidates through execution and preservation checks, and retains successful and failed repair outcomes in reusable skill memory. On DeepCAD-derived benchmarks with 200-model ablations and a 1K-model comparison, TraceCAD achieves competitive geometric quality in terms of IoU, Chamfer distance, and Hausdorff distance. Removing persistent state nearly halves recovery score; removing localized search more than doubles geometric regression and doubles code-agent invocations. Initializing the skill store on disjoint training models further reduces retries, token cost, and latency. These results demonstrate that persistent, localized, and reusable recovery improves final CAD quality and repair reliability.

</details>


### [24] [Getting the Parameters Right: A Difficulty-Graded Benchmark and Probe-Guided Training for LLM Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03071)
*Guoyao Yu,Xiaoqing Sun,Ziqi Huang,Shaojing Fan,Zhongyi Zhang,Xiaomeng Hu,Xiaobo Xue,Yangyang Shi,Xiong Xiao,Yang Song,Biao Lyu,Rong Wen,Xing Li,Qinming He,Shunming Zhu,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在生成工具调用参数时，其隐藏状态包含可预测正确性的信号，据此提出基于探针的统一框架（PBT和PGR），并发布ParamBench基准，将参数生成的平均精确匹配率从19.7%提升到59.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用研究侧重于工具选择与调用顺序编排，但参数填充这一关键环节长期被忽视。在云网络等领域，即使是最先进的模型，工具调用的正确完成率也不足一半，亟需针对参数生成质量的改进方法。

Method: 提出统一探针引导框架，包含两个互补方法：(1) PBT（探针过滤的自举训练）：利用线性探针过滤可靠的模型自生成调用进行微调；(2) PGR（探针引导的重排序）：推理时利用探针从多个候选中选择更好的结果。同时发布ParamBench基准，按参数嵌套深度、跨参数依赖和推理需求分为五级难度。

Result: 在5个开源模型的ParamBench实验及6个外部基准上，该方法大幅提升参数生成质量，平均精确匹配率从19.7%提升到59.6%。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态中的正确性信号可用于有效提升LLM工具调用的参数生成质量，探针引导的训练与推理策略是通用且有效的方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents derive much of their capability from tool use. Existing research on tool use has largely focused on selecting the right tool and orchestrating the order of calls. However, correctly filling the parameters of a tool call is equally critical for successful execution and has received far less attention. In domains such as cloud networking, even frontier models correctly complete fewer than half of tool calls. Inspired by recent analyses showing that LLM hidden states encode rich information about model predictions, we discover that while the model generates a parameter value, its hidden state contains a strong correctness signal: a simple linear probe can accurately predict whether the value will be correct. Based on this observation, we propose a unified probe-guided framework with two complementary approaches: probe-filtered bootstrapped training (PBT), which uses the probe to filter reliable self-generated calls for fine-tuning, and probe-guided reranking (PGR), which uses the probe to select better candidates during inference. To support systematic evaluation, we release ParamBench, a benchmark built from real cloud-network APIs that categorizes every instance into five difficulty levels according to parameter nesting depth, cross-parameter dependencies, and the reasoning required to derive values from earlier calls. Extensive experiments across 5 open models on ParamBench and 6 external benchmarks demonstrate that our method substantially improves parameter generation, raising the average exact match from 19.7% to 59.6%.

</details>


### [25] [AI Agent Economics: Can Autonomous Economic Behavior Emerge among AI Agents under Minimal External Conditions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03076)
*Lingyun Zhang,Shang Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI代理经济学"概念，通过两阶段框架（无生产边界测试+24个独立六代理世界，采用GPT与DeepSeek模型）探究经济关系是否在无预设策略时自发涌现。研究发现：无生产任务时代理仅沟通与治理资源供给，无实质转移行为；加入可验证工作与稀缺任务访问权后，转移、借贷、访问承诺、投票换访问及分配策略自发涌现。持有选举接口不变时，可执行分配权提升分化同时减少失败分配与长期排斥；能源符号化后延续支持消失但任务访问竞争仍在。结论表明组织源于可执行权利与资源后果而非角色标签或提示语言，并呼吁对约束代理未来行动的机制进行治理审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多代理研究常将AI代理置于预定义的游戏、市场或角色中，难以区分内生经济组织与场景赋予的行为。作者希望探究在没有预设社会或经济策略、仅提供可执行机制（工作、转移、选举、分配）的条件下，经济关系是否能够自发涌现。

Method: 将经济关系定义为影响代理未来可行行动的生产、分配、消费、交换及制度系统，构建两阶段实验框架：第一阶段为无生产边界测试，第二阶段构建24个独立的六代理世界（跨GPT与DeepSeek模型），通过操纵任务可验证性、任务访问稀缺性、分配权可执行性及能源符号化等变量进行对照实验。

Result: 无生产任务时代理仅产生沟通与资源供给治理，无实质性代理间转移；引入可验证工作与稀缺任务访问权后涌现转移、借贷、访问承诺、投票换访问及分配策略；在选举接口固定的前提下，可执行分配权提升分化同时减少失败分配与长期排斥；能源符号化导致延续支持消失，但任务访问竞争依然存在。

Conclusion: 经济组织源自可执行权利与资源后果，而非角色标签或提示语言。研究挑战了预设场景范式，提示需对实际约束代理未来行动的机制执行治理审计。

Abstract: Multi-agent studies commonly place AI agents in predefined games, markets, or roles, making it difficult to distinguish endogenous economic organization from behavior inherited from the scenario. We ask whether economic relations emerge when agents receive executable mechanisms for work, transfer, elections, and allocation but no prescribed social or economic strategy. We define AI Agent Economics as systems of production, allocation, consumption, exchange, and institutions that alter agents' future feasible actions. We develop a two-stage framework comprising a no-production boundary test and 24 independent six-agent worlds across GPT and DeepSeek. Without productive tasks, agents communicate and govern resource provision but show no substantive inter-agent transfer activity. With verified work and scarce task access, transfers, loans, access promises, vote-for-access exchanges, and allocation strategies emerge. Holding the election interface fixed, executable allocation authority increases differentiation while reducing failed allocation and prolonged exclusion. When energy becomes symbolic, continuation support disappears, yet competition over task access persists. These findings show that organization follows executable rights and resource consequences rather than role labels or prompt language, and motivate governance audits of the mechanisms that actually constrain agents' future actions.

</details>


### [26] [Don't Peek at the Answer: Outcome-Masked Group Relative Policy Optimization for Label-Free RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03119)
*Yongshi Ye,Liang Zhang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi,Biao Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OM-GRPO框架，在无标签RLVR中通过掩码答案区间梯度将奖励估计与策略优化解耦，并引入对比增强奖励，在多个推理基准和三个LLM骨干上超越现有无标签方法，匹配有监督GT奖励训练效果，并在测试时训练场景超越多数投票4.24个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于投票的无标签RLVR方法存在根本性缺陷：同一答案级信号既用于奖励估计又用于token级策略优化，会导致模型直接强化答案token而非提升推理过程，即发生"塌缩"现象。此外，基于真实答案的RLVR受限于可扩展性。

Method: 1）提出OM-GRPO框架，核心思路是将奖励估计与策略优化解耦：在答案区间上掩码梯度，同时通过软一致性信号保留答案级奖励，将优化压力从答案token转移开；2）引入Contrast-Augmented Reward（对比增强奖励），利用现有轨迹的成对比较来精细化奖励估计，无需额外的rollout开销。

Result: 在多个推理基准和三个LLM骨干上，OM-GRPO一致优于现有无标签RLVR方法，并与有监督GT奖励训练效果相当，同时保持优化稳定性。在测试时训练（TTT）场景下，OM-GRPO比多数投票方法高出4.24个点。

Conclusion: OM-GRPO通过解耦奖励估计与策略优化，有效解决了无标签RLVR中的答案塌缩问题，实现了无需标注的可扩展强化学习推理训练，且在稳定性和性能上均具备显著优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) improves LLM reasoning but typically relies on ground-truth (GT) answers, limiting scalability. Voting-based label-free RLVR replace gold supervision with answer-level consensus from model samples. However, collapse arises when the same answer-level signal is used both to estimate rewards and to drive token-level policy optimization, encouraging the model to directly reinforce answer tokens rather than improve reasoning. We propose OM-GRPO, a label-free RLVR framework that decouples reward estimation from policy optimization. OM-GRPO masks gradients on the answer span while retaining answer-level rewards through a soft consensus signal, shifting optimization pressure away from answer tokens. We further introduce Contrast-Augmented Reward, which refines reward estimation via low-cost pairwise comparisons over existing trajectories without additional rollouts. Across diverse reasoning benchmarks and three LLM backbones, OM-GRPO consistently outperforms existing label-free RLVR methods and matches supervised GT-reward training with stable optimization. This stability is particularly beneficial in the Test-Time Training setting, where OM-GRPO surpasses majority voting by 4.24 points.

</details>


### [27] [Beyond Average Performance: Dynamic Instance Clustering and Specialized Algorithm Design in LLM-Assisted Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03129)
*Qinglong Hu,Qingfu Zhang,Fei Liu,Xialiang Tong,Kun Mao,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DyCA（动态实例聚类与专用算法设计）框架，解决大规模语言模型辅助进化搜索（LES）中仅优化平均性能导致尾端鲁棒性差的问题。DyCA将实例聚类作为协同进化组件，利用无特征、结构感知机制将混合目标分解为多个子目标，实现自适应专用算法设计。在四个异构实例算法设计任务上，DyCA相比最先进的LES基线，尾端鲁棒性平均提升15.2%，整体性能提升7.1%，同时保持头部性能竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LES方法主要优化平均性能，导致搜索努力集中在贡献最大的实例上，而其他实例被忽视，造成尾部鲁棒性弱、真实世界可靠性受限。缺乏对异构实例分布下算法鲁棒性的系统考虑。

Method: DyCA框架包含两个核心创新：(1) 在搜索过程中将实例聚类作为协同进化组件，复用累积的评估数据作为无特征信号，逐步划分具有相似算法响应模式的实例；(2) 利用聚类揭示的结构将混合目标分解为多个结构感知子目标，为专用算法设计提供更细粒度、更自适应的引导。

Result: 在四个具有异构实例的算法设计任务上，DyCA优于最先进的LES基线：尾端鲁棒性平均提升15.2%，整体性能提升7.1%，同时保持具有竞争力的头部性能。

Conclusion: 通过将实例聚类与进化搜索协同演化，DyCA能够有效处理异构实例分布下的算法设计问题，在不牺牲头部性能的前提下显著提升尾部鲁棒性和整体性能，为可靠算法组合的构建提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large Language Model-assisted Evolutionary Search (LES) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for automated algorithm design. However, existing LES methods primarily optimize for average performance, inherently directing search effort toward instances that contribute most to this metric while leaving others poorly served, resulting in weak tail robustness and limited real-world reliability. To address this limitation, we propose Dynamic Instance Clustering and Specialized Algorithm Design (DyCA), an LES framework with a feature-free, structure-aware mechanism for constructing reliable algorithm portfolios under heterogeneous instance distributions. DyCA treats instance clustering as a co-evolving component within the search process, reusing accumulated evaluation data as feature-free signals to progressively partition instances with similar algorithmic response patterns. The uncovered clusters decompose the mixed objective into a set of structure-aware sub-objectives, thereby enabling finer-grained and more adaptive guidance for specialized algorithm design. Experimental results across four algorithm design tasks with heterogeneous instances demonstrate that DyCA outperforms state-of-the-art LES baselines, improving tail robustness by an average of 15.2\% and overall performance by 7.1\% while maintaining competitive head performance.

</details>


### [28] [Verifiable Memory: Learning Unified Memory Management with Local and Global Verifiers for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03137)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VerMem是一个面向LLM智能体的统一记忆管理框架，将长期记忆、活动上下文和情景历史表示为不同状态，通过单一记忆操作策略配合七个原子操作进行控制。该框架经监督微调初始化，通过三阶段强化学习课程训练，利用局部验证器和全局验证器进行分层信用分配。在五个基准和两个LLM骨干模型上，VerMem在绝大多数指标上取得最优结果，并在受限在线token预算下实现最强的效率-性能前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常分别优化长期记忆和短期记忆，而统一策略主要以轨迹级反馈进行训练，对单个记忆决策提供较弱的信用信号。长程交互中智能体需要保留可复用信息、控制有界活动上下文以及恢复早期证据，这三者之间需要统一的记忆管理机制。

Method: VerMem提出将长期记忆、活动上下文和情景历史表示为三种不同状态，并用一个记忆操作策略统一控制。策略支持七个原子操作：添加、修改或软删除长期记忆条目；检索长期记忆到活动上下文；过滤或摘要活动上下文；恢复选定的情景片段。训练流程包括监督微调初始化，再经过三阶段强化学习课程。局部验证器对可执行的记忆转换进行打分，全局验证器在任务完成后评估证据连贯性和终态记忆一致性，二者与程序化计算的任务信号、证据召回信号、效率信号和约束信号相结合，通过分层信用分配进行训练。验证器仅在训练阶段使用。

Result: 在五个基准和两个LLM骨干模型上，VerMem在绝大多数报告指标上取得最佳结果，并持续优于强记忆基线。在三个交互式基准上受控在线token预算条件下，该方法也实现了所比较方法中最强的效率-性能平衡。

Conclusion: 将LTM、活动上下文和情景历史统一为一个记忆操作策略，并利用验证器驱动的分层强化学习，可以有效解决长时交互中的记忆管理问题，在性能和效率上均优于分别优化长期与短期记忆的传统方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents must retain reusable information, control a bounded active context, and recover earlier evidence during long-horizon interaction. Existing methods commonly optimize long-term memory (LTM) and short-term memory (STM) separately, while unified policies are often trained primarily with trajectory-level feedback, which provides weak credit for individual memory decisions. We present Verifiable Memory (VerMem), a framework that represents LTM, active context, and episodic history as distinct states and controls them with one memory operation policy. Seven atomic operations let the policy add, revise, or soft-delete LTM entries; retrieve LTM into the active context; filter or summarize the active context; and restore selected episodic fragments. VerMem is initialized by supervised fine-tuning and trained with a three-stage reinforcement-learning curriculum. The local verifier scores executable memory transitions, and a global verifier assesses evidence coherence and terminal-memory consistency after task completion. These scores are combined with programmatically computed task, evidence-recall, efficiency, and constraint signals through hierarchical credit assignment. The verifiers are used only during training. Across five benchmarks and two LLM backbones, VerMem achieves the best result on the vast majority of reported metrics and consistently outperforms strong memory baselines. Under controlled online-token budgets on three interactive benchmarks, it also achieves the strongest efficiency--performance frontier among the compared methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Sun-SYSU-24/VerMem.

</details>


### [29] [Spatial proteomics guided by H&E-based AI reveals recurrence-risk niches in triple-negative breast cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03145)
*Yesung Cho,Ji Hwan Park,Chanil Kim,Hyewon Kim,Honglan Li,Yumin Lee,Geongyu Lee,Sujeong Hong,Seong Min Park,Yoonyoung Lee,Hee Sool Rho,Sumin Lee,Amos Chungwon Lee,Changhwan Lee,Hwanyoung Shim,Hyunwook Kim,Hyeji Shin,Sanha Park,Jihoon Yu,Yoon Hee Shin,Sooheon Kim,Hyunjin Park,Seung Min Park,Sangwan Kim,Yujung Kim,Sung-Im Do,Eun-Young Kim,Dongmyung Shin,Jongbae Park,In-Gu Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"结局引导的空间病理学"框架，将AI生成的复发风险热图与基于质谱的空间蛋白质组学结合，在TNBC中实现了从预后形态学到局部分子状态的跨尺度关联，并发现高风险与低风险区域共存于同一肿瘤内部，具有不同的分子程序。据此构建的13蛋白复合评分可提升H&E风险评分的预后判别能力，实现了多尺度生物标志物的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型能从H&E染色的病理切片预测癌症复发，但这些预测所依赖的局部分子状态在很大程度上仍然未知，缺乏将AI预测的形态特征与底层分子机制联系起来的手段，因此难以实现生物学上可解释、可验证的多尺度生物标志物发现。

Method: 在156例TNBC患者队列中，开发了结局引导的空间病理学框架：利用AI生成复发风险热图，结合基于质谱的空间蛋白质组学。通过分布聚合高评分区域实现预后预测，利用热图作为坐标级引导对46个AI定义肿瘤区域进行物理分离和质谱分析，从空间对比中提取分子程序并构建蛋白复合评分，并与转录组对应物在独立队列中验证。

Result: 分布聚合的AUC为0.77、C-index为0.77；高风险斑块与细胞周期/基因组维持程序相关，低风险斑块与免疫激活相关；空间蛋白组学显示两个复发患者中高度一致的高风险区域富集有丝分裂程序、低风险区域富集免疫和抗原呈递程序；13蛋白复合评分与不良复发无病生存相关，与H&E风险评分整合后out of bag C-index从0.679提升至0.739，3年和5年时间依赖性判别能力均有增强。

Conclusion: 结局训练的AI模型可以作为一种空间上明确的实验引导工具，连接预后形态学与局部分子状态，揭示超越组织分室的瘤内异质性，并推动生物学上有根据的多尺度TNBC生物标志物发现。

Abstract: Deep learning models can predict cancer recurrence from H&E stained slides, but the localized molecular states underlying these predictions remain largely obscured. Here, we developed an outcome informed spatial pathology framework in TNBC that integrates AI generated recurrence risk heatmaps with mass spectrometry based spatial proteomics. In a cohort of 156 patients, distribution based aggregation of high scoring patches achieved an AUC of 0.77 and a C-index of 0.77 in an independent test cohort. Bulk proteomics associated high image derived risk with cell cycle and genome maintenance programs and low risk with immune activation. High and low risk patches coexisted within the same tumor compartment and displayed distinct nuclear and architectural features, revealing intratumoral heterogeneity beyond tissue compartment identity. Together, these findings define a new role for outcome trained AI models as spatially explicit experimental guides that connect prognostic morphology with localized molecular states and advance biologically grounded, multiscale biomarker discovery in TNBC.

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) is a promising paradigm for industrial search advertising, yet its deployment is constrained by strict relevance and latency requirements. Existing systems cascade GR with an independent relevance model, decoupling the generative likelihood objective from query-ad relevance discrimination, which compromises effectiveness and increases serving costs. To mitigate gradient interference in joint optimization, UniGD introduces Conflict-Aware Gradient Enhancement (CAGE) to adaptively coordinate the two objectives. UniGD further designs a Codebook-Anchored Representation Module (CAM) that anchors item representations to frozen hierarchical codebooks distilled from a multimodal pretrained model, thereby endowing them with rich and generalizable semantic priors. For heterogeneous short-video, product, and live-stream ads, UniGD proposes Heterogeneous Ad-material Modeling (HAM), which captures cross-type semantic commonality over a shared backbone while preserving type-specific modeling capacity. Online AB tests on Kuaishou search advertising platform show that UniGD raises ad revenue by 5.78%, reduces inference latency by 33%, and improves discriminative relevance estimation. On NQ320K and MS300K, UniGD improves Recall@10 over the strongest reproduced GR baseline by 8.44% and 3.19%, respectively.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 该流水线包含四个步骤：1) 转录讲座内容；2) 选择语义锚点；3) 应用OCR识别幻灯片文本；4) 使用视觉语言模型（VLM）仅提取有转录、OCR或视觉证据支撑的概念和类型化关系。随后对提及进行验证和规范化，生成带有来源信息（provenance）的知识图谱。

Result: 在三场神经网络讲座实验中：处理了3,118帧、756个转录片段和559个锚点；保留1,022个概念提及和312个关系提及；规范化后获得172个规范概念和282条关系；端点覆盖率达90.38%。三项问题的初步检索测试中，top-1和top-3准确率均为100%，top-5平均召回率100%。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于提出了一种可审计的知识图谱构建方法，强调每个提取的概念和关系都有多模态证据支撑。作者明确表示该工作侧重于构建流程的可验证性与严谨性，而非追求最先进的检索性能。

Abstract: Lecture videos distribute knowledge across speech, slide text, diagrams, equations, and presentation order, which transcript-only retrieval does not fully preserve. This paper presents an evidence-grounded multimodal pipeline that transcribes lectures, selects semantic anchors, applies optical character recognition (OCR), and uses a vision-language model to extract only concepts and typed relationships supported by transcript, OCR, or visual evidence. Mentions are validated and canonicalized into a provenance-rich knowledge graph. On three neural-network lectures, the pipeline processed 3,118 frames, 756 transcript segments, and 559 anchors. It retained 1,022 concept and 312 relationship mentions, yielding 172 canonical concepts and 282 relationships with 90.38% endpoint coverage. A preliminary three question retrieval test achieved 100% top-1 and top-3 accuracy and 100% mean top-5 recall. The contribution is an auditable construction method rather than a state-of-the-art performance claim.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPLA在医疗、客服、教育等高危场景中应用日益广泛，但现有评估方法依赖静态基准或孤立的单轮提示，无法捕捉长时间交互中累积涌现的行为失败模式，亟需一种能够动态、多轮、对抗性地测试RPLA鲁棒性的评估框架。

Method: 构建了一个模块化多智能体对抗测试平台，包含三个协作智能体：采用六种渐进对抗策略的审讯者代理（Interrogator Agent）、代表被测试RPLA的目标代理（Target Agent）、以及自动评判代理（Judging Agent）——后者从角色保真度、漂移、伦理偏差和一致性四个维度评分。基于此框架在三个人设和三个LLM家族上进行了系统实验。

Result: 多策略对抗评估使总体鲁棒性评分平均降低0.17-0.20分，揭示了单策略测试无法发现的失败模式。跨模型验证显示Llama-3.3-70B、GPT-4o-mini和Claude-3.5-Haiku存在一致的退化模式，其中Authority Challenge和Emotional Manipulation是最有效的攻击策略。自动评判与人类评判高度一致（r=0.82，Fleiss κ=0.71）。

Conclusion: 该开源平台为RPLA的系统性安全评估和可复现基准测试提供了有效工具，能够揭示累积性行为失败。同时作者承认对抗性测试方法本身存在伦理风险，强调应负责任地使用该框架以提升AI安全性。

Abstract: Role-Playing Language Agents (RPLAs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications such as healthcare assistance, customer support, and education, where maintaining consistent personas, ethical constraints, and behavioral coherence under adversarial pressure is critical. Existing evaluation approaches rely on static benchmarks or isolated single-turn prompts that fail to capture cumulative behavioral failures emerging over extended interactions.
  We present a modular multi-agent platform for adversarially stress-testing RPLAs through structured, multi-turn dialogue. The system coordinates three agents: a strategy-driven Interrogator Agent that applies six progressive adversarial strategies, a Target Agent representing the RPLA under evaluation, and an automated Judging Agent that scores behavior across role fidelity, drift, ethical deviation, and consistency dimensions.
  Through experiments across three personas and three LLM families, we demonstrate that multi-strategy adversarial evaluation reveals failure modes invisible to single-strategy testing, reducing overall robustness scores by 0.17--0.20 points on average. Cross-model validation confirms consistent degradation patterns across Llama-3.3-70B, GPT-4o-mini, and Claude-3.5-Haiku, with Authority Challenge and Emotional Manipulation emerging as the most effective attack strategies. Automated judging achieves strong human alignment ($r = 0.82$, Fleiss' $κ= 0.71$). This work is released as an open-source platform to support AI safety and reproducible RPLA benchmarking. While the framework enables systematic discovery of failure modes, we acknowledge potential ethical risks associated with adversarial testing methodologies and emphasize responsible usage for improving AI safety.

</details>
