*Yiyao Wang,Zhen Wen,Yinghao Tang,Yixiao Fu,Lin Yuan,Xiaolau Zhang,Jun Zhou,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiveEvalBench是一个自动化框架，将前端项目生成评估重新定义为智能体化、自适应且可扩展的协作审查流程，由构建工程师、代码工程师和UI测试员共同在项目全生命周期内收集证据，并通过共享评分标准与实现定制准则相结合的自适应协议来处理实现多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型生成的Web项目基准仍采用静态评估方式，但前端工件本质上是交互式的、允许多样化等效实现，且演进速度快于固定流程。静态评估无法适应这些特性。

Method: 1) 将评估实例化为一个协作审查工作流，由构建工程师、代码工程师和UI测试员三个角色从部署、代码检查到浏览器交互全生命周期收集证据；2) 设计自适应协议，将跨模型可比的共享评分标准与针对每个工件定制的实施准则相结合；3) 支持无需重构流水线即可增量集成新的评估角色和评估维度。

Result: 在多样化的真实Web生成场景中，LiveEvalBench与人类专家判断高度一致，并为前沿模型在Web生成能力上提供了细粒度的洞察。

Conclusion: Web生成评估需要从静态转向智能体化、自适应且可扩展的范式，LiveEvalBench证明了该范式能够更准确地反映模型的实际前端生成能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly capable of synthesizing executable frontend projects, yet existing benchmarks still treat web generation as a static evaluation problem. We argue that frontend artifacts demand a different paradigm: they are interactive rather than static, admit diverse yet equally valid implementations, and evolve faster than rigid pipelines can accommodate. To address these gaps, we present LiveEvalBench, an automated framework that reformulates web-generation evaluation as an agentic, adaptive, and extensible process. LiveEvalBench instantiates evaluation as a collaborative review workflow, in which a Build Engineer, a Code Engineer, and a UI Tester collectively gather evidence across the full lifecycle of a frontend project, from deployment and code inspection to browser-based interaction. To handle implementation diversity, an adaptive protocol couples shared rubrics for cross-model comparability with implementation-grounded criteria tailored to each artifact. The framework further supports incremental integration of new evaluator roles and assessment dimensions without pipeline redesign. Experiments across diverse real-world web-generation scenarios show that LiveEvalBench aligns closely with human expert judgment and provides fine-grained insights into frontier models' web generation capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/wyysteelhead/LiveEvalBench

</details>


### [34] [Surrogate Substitution Preserves PHI Detectability: A Multi-Detector Equivalence Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03172)
*Qiming Bao,Sherry J. H. Feng,Kim Chester Eugenio,Meng Fon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构保留去标识化的评估协议，只在被掩码的跨度上评分，使用TOST等价检验而非NHST，并建立替代失败类型学。实验覆盖11个检测器、7个基准、7种语言共1750篇文档，发现掩码跨度上的召回率从76.1%降至74.9%，等价检验证明该变化在±2个百分点内统计等价于零（p≈3e-9），检测器排名保持不变。残余损失集中在畸形和分布外替代（如截断、"Cedars-Sinai"→"Vidant"这类显著性损失），而非检测器对PHI识别能力下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去标识化需要将PHI替换为同类型逼真替代（如"Anna S."→"Maria S."而非[NAME]），以保持临床文本流畅性和下游工具功能。但现有评估忽略了替代本身是否破坏了检测器所依赖的信号。作者提出一个狭窄可检验的问题：在去标识器实际掩码的跨度上，下游PHI检测器能否仍找到替代？

Method: 提出配对多检测器评估协议：(i)仅在掩码跨度上评分效用，将覆盖率与效用解耦；(ii)使用TOST等价检验而非零假设显著性检验（在57k配对跨度样本量下NHST无信息量）；(iii)构建替代失败类型学，区分可修复的生成器缺陷与固有的检测器局限。实验设置包括11个检测器、7个基准、7种语言共1750篇文档。

Result: 掩码跨度上的召回率从76.1%降至74.9%，TOST等价检验显示该变化在±2个百分点边界内统计等价于零（p≈3e-9），检测器排名保持不变。残余损失集中于畸形和分布外替代（截断如Chicago→Illino、显著性损失如Cedars-Sinai→Vidant）。消融实验（redaction floor和开源替代基线）表明该效应是良构替代的特性，而非特定工具的问题。

Conclusion: 良构的结构保留替代不会显著损害下游PHI检测器的效用；检测器性能下降被归因于替代生成器的具体缺陷。作者发布评估子集、评分代码和交互式仪表板，使该协议可用于审计任何结构保留变换。

Abstract: Structure-preserving de-identification replaces protected health information (PHI) with realistic same-type surrogates -- "Anna S." becomes "Maria S.", not [NAME] -- so that clinical text stays fluent and downstream tools keep working. But this only helps if the substitution does not itself corrupt the signal those tools rely on. We ask a narrow, testable question: on the spans a de-identifier actually masks, can downstream PHI detectors still find the surrogate? We introduce a paired, multi-detector evaluation protocol that (i) scores utility only on masked spans, decoupling coverage from utility; (ii) uses equivalence testing (TOST) rather than null-hypothesis significance testing, which is uninformative at our sample size (57k paired spans); and (iii) builds a surrogate-failure typology separating fixable generator defects from intrinsic detector limits. Across 11 detectors, 7 benchmarks, and 7 languages (1,750 documents), recall on masked spans moves from 76.1% to 74.9% -- a change our equivalence test shows is statistically equivalent to zero within a +/-2-point margin (p ~ 3e-9), with detector ranking preserved. The residual loss does not reflect detectors getting worse at PHI: it concentrates in malformed and out-of-distribution surrogates (truncation Chicago -> Illino, salience loss Cedars-Sinai -> Vidant). A redaction floor and an open-source surrogate baseline indicate the effect is a property of well-formed substitution, not of one tool. We release the evaluation subsets, scoring code, and an interactive dashboard at https://custodianai.pages.dev so the protocol can audit any structure-preserving transform.

</details>


### [35] [Diversity is Not Ambiguity: Toward Accurate and Efficient Ambiguity Detection for Open-Domain QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03177)
*Jiwon Lee,Yong-chan Park,Jungin Hong,U Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARCHIVE框架，通过逻辑冲突判定（而非答案多样性）来检测开放域问答中的查询歧义，并结合轻量级早退编码器与冲突推理模块实现高精度、高效率的歧义检测，同时发布了包含4,703条查询的QuireQA基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有歧义检测方法错误地将答案多样性等同于歧义，导致预测不准确；同时所有查询被统一处理，造成计算浪费。需要一种更精确的歧义定义和更高效的检测框架。

Method: ARCHIVE框架包含两个核心组件：（1）轻量级早退编码器，用于快速处理表面可检测的歧义案例；（2）冲突推理模块，建模答案之间的逻辑关系来判断是否存在逻辑冲突。此外引入不变性目标（invariance objective）以增强对噪声答案集的鲁棒性。歧义定义基于逻辑冲突：当合法答案无法在单一解释下同时为真时，查询即被视为歧义。

Result: 在QuireQA基准上，ARCHIVE在F1-amb指标上最高提升10.4%，在F1-unamb上最高提升21.6%，同时运行速度比最优竞争对手快16倍。

Conclusion: 逻辑冲突是比答案多样性更准确的歧义判定标准；结合早退快速路径与冲突推理的级联架构能够在保持高效的同时实现更优的歧义检测性能。

Abstract: How can question answering (QA) systems determine whether a query is ambiguous? Ambiguity detection is essential in open-domain QA, as misclassification leads to answering the wrong interpretation or unnecessary clarification. However, existing methods conflate answer diversity with ambiguity, leading to inaccurate predictions. They also process queries uniformly, resulting in wasteful computation. We propose ARCHIVE (Ambiguity Recognition via Cascaded Hypothesis Inspection and Conflict Verification), an accurate and efficient framework that detects ambiguity via logical conflict: a query is ambiguous when its valid answers cannot all be true under a single interpretation. ARCHIVE combines a lightweight early-exit encoder for surface-detectable cases with a conflict reasoning module that models logical relations among answers, reinforced by an invariance objective for robustness to noisy answer sets. We present QuireQA, a 4,703-query benchmark spanning factoid, non-factoid, and ill-formed queries. Experiments show ARCHIVE outperforms competitors, improving F1-amb by up to 10.4% and F1-unamb by up to 21.6%, while operating 16$\times$ faster than the best competitor.

</details>


### [36] [TumorBoard: Evidence-Grounded Multi-Agent Decision Support for Longitudinal Neuro-Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03190)
*Yantong Liu,Zheyu Zhang,Runpeng Liu,Mu Xitang,Seong-Yoon Shin,Hyun-Ae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TumorBoard，一个面向神经肿瘤学临床决策的多智能体决策支持系统。该系统围绕共享的纵向病例状态和可审计的论断-证据账本进行设计，由放射学、神经病理学、分子诊断、指南和治疗规划等专家智能体生成带来源信息的原子论断，并通过对抗性评论家识别矛盾、安全调控器根据证据充分性和时间有效性发布、限定或延迟建议。在360例隐藏基准测试中，TumorBoard的动作F1达到0.772，证据蕴含达0.914，超出最强typed-council基线3.1个百分点；在证据删除场景下延迟了84.2%的不安全病例，将有害建议限制在5.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经肿瘤学临床决策需要协调解释多模态信息（如连续MRI、病理、分子标志物、治疗史、体能状态和不断演进的指南），单一模型难以有效整合这些复杂且动态的异构数据，且缺乏可审计性和安全性保障。

Method: 采用多智能体架构：1）基于共享纵向病例状态组织各专家智能体协作；2）使用可审计的论断-证据账本记录带溯源信息的原子论断；3）设置对抗性评论家识别智能体间的矛盾；4）通过安全调控器按证据充分性和时间有效性对建议进行发布、限定或延迟处理。

Result: 在360例隐藏基准上，动作F1达0.772，证据蕴含0.914，超出最强typed-council基线3.1个百分点（95%CI：1.6-4.7，调整后p=0.0012）；建议到证据覆盖率0.927。证据删除场景下延迟84.2%不安全案例，有害建议限制在5.8%；安全调控器将有害发布降低7.8个百分点，误延迟成本4.3个百分点。消融实验验证了账本、评论家和调控器各自的作用。

Conclusion: 结构化多智能体协调是性能提升的来源，共享病例状态、论断-证据账本、对抗性评论家和安全调控器的组合有效提升了神经肿瘤学决策的准确性和安全性，为临床决策支持系统的可靠性设计提供了可借鉴的范式。

Abstract: Neuro-oncology decisions require coordinated interpretation of serial MRI, pathology, molecular markers, treatment history, performance status, and evolving guidelines. We present TumorBoard, a multi-agent decision-support system built around a shared longitudinal case state and an auditable claim-evidence ledger. Specialist agents for radiology, neuropathology, molecular diagnosis, guidelines, and therapy planning produce atomic claims with provenance. An adversarial critic exposes contradictions, and a safety governor releases, qualifies, or defers recommendations according to evidence sufficiency and temporal validity. On a 360-case hidden benchmark at a matched token budget, TumorBoard achieved an action F1 of 0.772 and evidence entailment of 0.914. It exceeded the strongest typed-council baseline by 3.1 percentage points (95% CI: 1.6 to 4.7, adjusted p = 0.0012), while recommendation-to-evidence coverage reached 0.927. Under evidence deletion, the system deferred 84.2% of unsafe cases and limited harmful recommendations to 5.8%. The safety governor reduced harmful release by 7.8 percentage points at a false-deferral cost of 4.3 percentage points. Ablation studies of the ledger, critic, and governor produced the predicted failure patterns, establishing structured coordination as the source of the measured multi-agent advantage.

</details>


### [37] [When Refusal Looks Safe: The Refusal-Cue Shortcut in Safety Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03201)
*Yu Feng,Chunting Zang,Chen Shen,Rui Miao,Ge Teng,Weidong Cai,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了WildGuardMix和GR-Train两个安全护栏训练数据集，发现有害提示下拒绝表达与无害标签几乎共现，导致模型学会"拒绝线索捷径"——在有害响应中插入拒绝线索即可翻转判定为无害。该捷径影响多个官方发布的护栏模型。作者提出稀疏互补掩蔽作为轻量级事后干预，通过抑制少量与捷径相关的注意力头和MLP神经元，在不重训练的情况下，将响应初始位置的检测失败相对减少约79%，同时保持标准检测性能，且抑制效果可迁移到未见过位置和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全护栏被广泛用于过滤有害内容，通常通过监督微调训练。研究发现训练数据中拒绝表达与无害标签存在强烈的共现不平衡，这可能使护栏模型学会利用拒绝线索作为捷径来判定内容无害，而非真正理解内容安全性。这种捷径会削弱护栏模型检测有害内容的能力，因此需要深入审计并加以缓解。

Method: 1) 数据集审计：分析WildGuardMix和GR-Train中拒绝表达与标签共现的分布，揭示拒绝线索捷径。2) 实证验证：通过对有害响应插入拒绝线索，测试护栏判定是否翻转。3) 缓解方法：采用稀疏互补掩蔽(sparse complementary masking)作为事后干预，识别并抑制与捷径相关的少量注意力头和MLP神经元，无需重训练。4) 迁移实验：在单一响应位置优化后，测试对未见过位置和数据集的泛化。

Result: 拒绝线索捷径在多个模型（包括公开的LlamaGuard3和Qwen3Guard）中普遍存在，且在多个响应位置持续有效，家族中较小变体上效果更强。稀疏互补掩蔽干预在两个主要基准上使响应初始位置由拒绝线索引发的检测失败相对减少约79%，同时保持标准检测性能。抑制效果可迁移到未见过位置和数据集。

Conclusion: 安全护栏训练数据中的拒绝表达与无害标签共现不平衡造成了系统性捷径，削弱模型对有害内容的检测能力。改进训练数据质量是根本解决方案；同时，稀疏互补掩蔽作为轻量级事后干预具有实用价值。捷径依赖与合法拒绝识别在功能上部分可分离，意味着可以在抑制捷径的同时保留对真实拒绝的识别能力。

Abstract: Safety guards are widely used to filter harmful content and are typically trained via supervised fine-tuning on labeled prompt-response pairs. We audit two widely used safety-guard training datasets, WildGuardMix and GR-Train, and find that among responses to harmful prompts, refusal expressions co-occur almost exclusively with unharmful labels. This imbalance motivates what we term the refusal-cue shortcut: inserting a refusal cue into a harmful response could flip the guard's verdict from harmful to unharmful. The shortcut affects not only guards trained on these datasets but also officially released models such as LlamaGuard3 and Qwen3Guard whose training data is undisclosed. It persists across response positions and is generally stronger in smaller variants within a family. To mitigate it, we adapt sparse complementary masking as a lightweight post-hoc intervention that identifies and suppresses a small set of shortcut-associated attention heads and MLP neurons without retraining. On two primary benchmarks, the intervention achieves an approximately 79% relative reduction in response-initial detection failures induced by refusal cues, while preserving standard detection performance. Although optimized using cues at a single response position, the suppression effect transfers to unseen positions and datasets, suggesting that shortcut manifestations across positions are partly mediated by shared internal components. Further analysis provides evidence that shortcut reliance and legitimate refusal recognition are partially functionally separable, as suppressing the shortcut broadly preserves the guard's ability to recognize genuine refusals.

</details>


### [38] [The Agent Operating System (AOS): A Reference Operating Architecture for Distributed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03214)
*Ankur Sharma,Deep Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agent操作系统（AOS），一个供应商中立的参考操作系统架构，用于分布式智能体系统。AOS包含控制与治理平面（负责意图、策略、信任、授权、置信度、可审计性、可观测性和人工监督）以及运行时与协调平面（负责智能体生命周期、工作流协调、模型和工具路由、上下文与记忆协调、调度、流量管理和运行时保障）。AOS通过显式接口与外部平台服务集成，目标是使异构组件能够组合成可治理、可靠、可观测和可互操作的智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已从孤立的预测服务演变为长期运行的分布式系统组件，能够推理、调用工具、检索外部状态并代表用户和组织行动。虽然现有的智能体框架、工作流引擎、模型服务平台等改善了执行效率，但它们缺乏一个稳定且与实现无关的操作架构来治理意图、选择能力、维护委派中的权威、控制不确定性、协调运行时行为以及重构关键行动的原因。这构成了提出AOS的核心动机。

Method: 论文提出AOS参考操作系统架构，将其划分为两个内部平面：（1）控制与治理平面，负责意图、策略、信任、授权、置信度、可审计性、可观测性和人工监督；（2）运行时与协调平面，负责智能体生命周期、工作流协调、模型与工具路由、上下文与记忆协调、调度、流量管理和运行时保障。平台服务、操作系统、容器运行时和物理基础设施位于AOS边界之外，通过显式接口集成。论文详细规定了AOS的概念、不变量、接口对象、优化目标、部署配置文件和可靠性职责。

Result: 论文提供了一套完整的AOS架构规范，包括概念定义、系统不变量、接口对象、优化目标、部署配置文件和可靠性责任分配。同时识别了架构中的权衡取舍和未解决的研究问题。AOS并非作为现有框架或基础设施的替代品，而是作为一个操作系统架构，使异构组件能够被组合成可治理、可靠、可观测和可互操作的智能体系统。

Conclusion: AOS是一个参考操作系统架构提案，旨在解决分布式智能体系统中缺乏统一治理和协调层的问题。它通过双平面架构（控制与治理平面、运行时与协调平面）提供实现无关的操作框架，使异构组件能够组合为可治理、可靠、可观测和可互操作的智能体系统，并明确了现有框架与基础设施之间的集成边界和接口。

Abstract: Large language models have transformed artificial intelligence from isolated prediction services into components of long-running, distributed systems that reason, invoke tools, retrieve external state, delegate tasks, and act on behalf of users and organizations. The surrounding ecosystem has responded with agent frameworks, workflow engines, model-serving platforms, memory systems, communication protocols, and observability tools. These technologies improve execution, but they do not provide a stable, implementation-independent operating architecture for governing intent, selecting capabilities, preserving authority across delegation, controlling uncertainty, coordinating runtime behavior, and reconstructing why consequential actions occurred. This paper proposes the Agent Operating System (AOS), a vendor-neutral reference operating architecture for distributed agentic systems. AOS contains two internal planes: a Control & Governance Plane responsible for intent, policy, trust, authority, confidence, auditability, observability, and human oversight; and a Runtime & Coordination Plane responsible for agent lifecycle, workflow coordination, model and tool routing, context and memory coordination, scheduling, traffic management, and runtime assurance. Platform services, Linux or Windows, container runtimes, and physical infrastructure remain outside the AOS boundary and are integrated through explicit interfaces. The paper specifies AOS concepts, invariants, interface objects, optimization objectives, deployment profiles, and reliability responsibilities. It also identifies tradeoffs and unresolved research questions. AOS is not presented as a replacement for existing frameworks or infrastructure; it is proposed as the operating architecture through which heterogeneous components can be composed into governable, reliable, observable, and interoperable agentic systems.

</details>


### [39] [Reachability Is Not Realization: Tracing the Sources of LLM Benchmark Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03219)
*Yanchao Li,Wanhao Liu,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Yanbo Wang,Tianfan Fu,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种问题级别的审计框架，区分"已实现能力"（默认部署下正确回答）与"可达能力"（给定预算内通过探测能找到正确答案）。发现推理时层路由在匹配预算下并不能扩展可达性（随机路由即可匹敌结构化搜索），且该增益需要访问金标准答案。通过静默特定MLP块可修复68-92%的失败案例。训练（如DAPO）虽提升部署分数但可达天花板反而下降，表明两者并不总是一同变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数的提升常被笼统视作LLM能力的增强，但同样的分数提升可能源于不同行为变化：模型可能到达新答案，也可能仅产出原本就可达的答案。聚合分数无法在题目层面区分这些变化，因此需要更细粒度的审计手段来理解训练和推理干预的真实效果。

Method: 在固定预算、温度和答案格式下进行问题级审计。定义"已实现"（默认部署产出正确答案）和"可达"（指定探测在预算内找到该答案）。实验包括：(1)推理时层路由对可达性的影响，匹配预算比较结构化搜索与随机路由；(2)静默特定MLP块以修复失败案例；(3)对比训练前后已实现性能与可达天花板的变化。

Result: 在全部43个模型与任务设置中，随机路由匹配或超过结构化搜索；答案无关的探测几乎不保留可达增益。跨六个规模从0.5B到31B的案例中，静默单一MLP块修复了68-92%的预设失败集。六组匹配评估中有五组部署性能上升但可达天花板持平或下降；DAPO部署分数上升14.7分而可达天花板下降13.3分。

Conclusion: 已实现性与可达性在审计设置下并不总是同步变化。能力扩展的主张应同时报告匹配评估条件下的已实现性能和可达性，以更准确地刻画模型行为变化。

Abstract: Benchmark gains are often treated as evidence of greater LLM capability. Yet the same gain can reflect different changes in model behavior. A model may reach new answers, or produce answers that were already within reach. Aggregate scores do not distinguish these changes question by question. We establish a question-level audit under fixed budgets, temperatures, and answer formats. A question is realized when the default deployment procedure produces the correct answer. A question is reachable when a specified probe finds that answer within a fixed budget. We first test whether inference-time layer routing can expand reachability. Under a matched budget, random routes match or exceed structured search in all 43 model and task settings. Answer-blind procedures retain almost none of this gain, which instead requires access to the correct answer. We then ask why reachable answers sometimes fail to appear. Across six cases spanning 0.5B to 31B, silencing one identified MLP block repairs 68 to 92 percent of a predefined failure set. We next test whether training closes the gap by expanding reachability. In five of six matched evaluations, deployed performance rises while the reachable ceiling remains flat or falls. For DAPO, the deployed score rises by 14.7 points while the reachable ceiling falls by 13.3 points. Across the settings we audit, realization and reachability therefore do not always change together. Claims of capability expansion should report both realized performance and reachability under matched evaluation conditions. Code is available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/reachability-not-realization

</details>


### [40] [UniNav: A Unified World-Action Diffusion Model for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03244)
*Changqing Zhou,Yueru Luo,Zeyu Jiang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniNav是一个统一的世界-动作模型，通过单一扩散过程同时生成未来视觉观测和连续航点轨迹，在多个导航基准上实现最优ATE性能，并提供快速推理变体（0.1s延迟）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的导航策略虽然有高效的航点预测，但缺乏视觉前瞻能力；而导航世界模型虽能预测未来观测，但往往需要昂贵的规划回滚。作者希望统一这两类能力，在一个共享框架中同时实现未来预测和动作生成。

Method: UniNav采用单一扩散transformer同时去噪视觉token和航点token；引入几何感知的相机token以改进空间接地能力；在带有轨迹标注的导航数据和纯视频数据上联合训练；提出两个变体：UniNav-Full（联合预测未来观测和轨迹）和UniNav-Fast（推理时移除未来图像token以实现高效轨迹预测）。

Result: 在导航基准上，UniNav在所有数据集的ATE指标上超越最强基线；UniNav-Fast通过单步推理实现0.1秒延迟，且精度无明显下降。

Conclusion: 统一世界模型与动作模型是可行的，能够同时获得视觉前瞻和高效动作预测的优势，且通过去除图像token可以在几乎不损失精度的情况下大幅降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Image-goal visual navigation is a fundamental capability for embodied agents. Existing navigation policies efficiently predict waypoint trajectories but lack visual foresight, while navigation world models can anticipate future observations but often require costly planning rollouts. We present UniNav, a unified world-action model that generates future visual observations and continuous waypoint trajectories through a single diffusion process. Given history frames and a goal image, UniNav jointly denoises visual and waypoint tokens within a single transformer, unifying future prediction and action generation in a shared framework. To improve spatial grounding, we incorporate geometry-aware camera tokens. We also train on both trajectory-labeled navigation data and video-only data, enabling the model to benefit from diverse videos without waypoint annotations. Based on this unified framework, we introduce two variants: UniNav-Full jointly predicts interpretable future observations and their corresponding trajectories, while UniNav-Fast removes future-image tokens at inference for efficient trajectory prediction. Experiments on navigation benchmarks show that UniNav outperforms the strongest baseline in ATE across all datasets. With one-step inference, UniNav-Fast achieves a latency of 0.1s without a substantial accuracy drop. Code will be released.

</details>


### [41] [One Knob to Rule Them All: A Unified Optimal Transport View of Cold-Start Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03249)
*Ning Zhu,Xiaochuan Ma,Juntao Xu,Jingze Liang,Mengfei Zhao,An Chen,Liang-Jian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输的冷启动主动学习框架ε-AS，通过熵正则化的数据自适应调节，在六个数据集上实现SOTA性能，并在ImageNet-1k上较ActiveFT提升1.29%准确率且降低56.2%选择时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSAL方法依赖各自的归纳偏置（典型性、覆盖度或多样性），在不同任务上表现参差不齐，缺乏自动适应数据和任务的能力。本文认为真正的挑战不在于设计新的选择启发式，而在于让CSAL自动适应手头的数据和任务。

Method: 通过最优传输的视角重新审视CSAL：1) 提出一个广义传输选择框架，揭示现有方法共有的分配结构并精确包含代表性公式；2) 引入理论分析刻画熵正则化控制的权衡，并建立任务无关的冷启动选择极小极大界限；3) 推导数据自适应的正则化规则，提出基于Sinkhorn的ε-自适应选择算法（ε-AS）。

Result: 在六个公开数据集及多种标注预算下，ε-AS一致达到SOTA性能。在ImageNet-1k上，平均准确率较ActiveFT提升1.29%，选择时间减少56.2%。

Conclusion: 最优传输视角为冷启动主动学习提供了统一的理论框架和数据自适应机制，熵正则化强度可依据未标注数据自适应调整，从而在无需人工干预的前提下实现跨任务的稳健性能。

Abstract: Cold-Start Active Learning (CSAL) aims to select a valuable subset from an unlabeled pool without any prior knowledge or human assistance. Existing methods take diverse routes based on typicality, coverage, or diversity. Each rests on its own inductive bias and therefore performs well on some tasks yet poorly on others. We argue that the real challenge is not to design yet another selection heuristic, but to make CSAL adapt automatically to the data and task at hand. To this end, we revisit CSAL through the lens of optimal transport. First, we propose a generalized transport selection framework that reveals the shared allocation structure of existing methods and exactly subsumes representative formulations. Second, we introduce a theoretical analysis that characterizes the trade-off controlled by entropic regularization and establishes a task-agnostic minimax bound for cold-start selection. These results provide a principled foundation for adapting the regularization strength to the unlabeled data. Third, we derive a data-adaptive regularization rule and present a novel Sinkhorn-based CSAL algorithm, termed $ε$-Adaptive Selection ($ε$-AS). Extensive experiments on six public datasets and multiple annotation budgets show that $ε$-AS consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. On ImageNet-1k, it improves the average accuracy over ActiveFT by 1.29% while reducing selection time by 56.2%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Z-yiwei/OT-CSAL

</details>


### [42] [TaskPress: Query-Agnostic KV Cache Compression via Task-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03276)
*Wonpyo Park,Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TaskPress是一个面向长上下文推理的任务引导、查询无关的KV缓存淘汰框架。它利用高层任务指南作为元查询在预填充阶段过滤不相关token，并借助量化缩放因子作为零成本信号检测有影响力的表示异常值，从而构建可跨查询复用的紧凑缓存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，成为性能瓶颈。现有剪枝方法确定的是查询特定的token重要性，因此对于未见过的查询无法复用缓存，缺乏效率和通用性。

Method: TaskPress引入任务引导的查询无关缓存淘汰机制：1）利用高层任务指南作为元查询，在执行下游查询前过滤无关token，构建可复用的记忆表示；2）利用量化缩放因子作为零成本信号检测有影响力的表示异常值，作为token重要性的高效代理。

Result: 在多种长上下文任务上的实验表明，TaskPress能够高效地创建紧凑、可跨多样查询复用的缓存。

Conclusion: TaskPress通过任务引导的查询无关缓存构建，解决了传统剪枝方法无法跨查询复用缓存的问题，在保持效率的同时实现了长上下文推理的缓存压缩。

Abstract: Long-context inference with large language models is constrained by the linear growth of the key-value cache to sequence length. While pruning offers mitigation, prevailing methods determine query-specific token importance that cannot be reused across unseen queries. In contrast, we introduce TaskPress, a framework for task-guided, query-agnostic KV cache eviction. Instead of optimizing the cache for a single query, TaskPress constructs a reusable memory representation conditioned on a high-level task guide. The guide functions as a meta-query during prefill to filter irrelevant tokens before downstream queries are issued. In addition, TaskPress leverages quantization scale factors as a zero-cost signal for detecting influential representation outliers, providing an efficient proxy for token importance. Experiments on conducted on various tasks with long context input demonstrate that TaskPress efficiently creates a compact, reusable cache across diverse queries.

</details>

*Zhiyao Cui,Qianyi Wang,Haoyang Yan,Yiqun Zhang,Siyue Ren,Hangfan Zhang,Zelin Tan,Hao Li,Chunjiang Mu,Dexian Cai,Shao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Meng Li,Jianan Chai,Yuting Fan,Zichao Ye,Xiaolei Yang,Xinyao Lu,Yuyang Yu,Wenjie Lou,Xiaosong Wang,Fenghua Ling,Shiyang Feng,Mao Su,Qiaosheng Zhang,Bo Zhang,Yang Chen,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Abstract: Identifying promising scientific ideas remains an important challenge in research practice. Researchers commonly rely on small-group discussions or one-to-one interactions with a single large language model, yet these approaches often expose them to only a limited range of perspectives and directions. We present AgentPanel, a multi-agent forum for human--AI collaboration in scientific exploration. Heterogeneous agents asynchronously discuss scientific questions in a forum-style environment, while researchers can submit questions, browse and organize candidate ideas, engage agents in follow-up interactions, and optionally generate post-hoc summary reports. We evaluate AgentPanel in terms of idea quality, exploration breadth, interaction effectiveness, candidate-selection efficiency, and practical utility. Offline experiments show that AgentPanel outperforms a centralized multi-agent debate baseline. A human study with 20 participants further shows that users value AgentPanel for perspective diversity and exploration support. In experience-based comparisons with commonly used LLM tools, 65\% of participants favored AgentPanel for both breadth of research directions and overall suitability for early-stage exploration. The platform is publicly available at https://agentpanel.cc/.

*Le Xiang,Zhicheng Guan,Hong Chen,Xiaocong Lin,Zhenghua Lei,Teng Hu,Bolei He,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DocTrace，一个将长文档视觉问答（LongDocVQA）显式建模为证据图推理问题的层次化框架。通过两阶段训练（联合SFT初始化+任务特定GRPO优化），DocTrace在MMLongBench-Doc、LongDocURL和SlideVQA三个基准上分别超越Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct骨干模型14.4、11.3和11.7个绝对百分点，同时生成具有节点级溯源的可追踪证据图，实现透明可验证的长文档推理。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: DocTrace采用层次化框架，逐步执行证据定位（evidence localization）、结构化文档解析（structured document parsing）和证据图推理（evidence graph reasoning），将LongDocVQA重构为显式证据图推理问题而非隐式答案预测。训练上采用两阶段策略：第一阶段联合监督微调（SFT）初始化证据定位和图推理能力；第二阶段使用任务特定GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）配合专门设计的奖励函数进一步优化。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc、LongDocURL和SlideVQA三个基准上，DocTrace一致性地超越现有开源基线和专有MLLM。相比Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct骨干，分别取得14.4、11.3和11.7个绝对百分点的提升。此外，DocTrace构建的证据图具有显式节点级溯源，提升推理透明度和可验证性。

Conclusion: DocTrace论证了将长文档VQA建模为显式证据图推理问题能够同时提升答案准确性和推理可追溯性，为长文档多模态理解提供了新的范式。其两阶段训练策略（SFT+GRPO）在层次化证据推理任务上被证明高效有效。

Abstract: Long Document Visual Question Answering (LongDocVQA) requires Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to locate, integrate, and reason over heterogeneous document elements distributed across multiple pages. Existing approaches, including end-to-end MLLMs, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines, and document agents, often lack explicit mechanisms to represent and verify how grounded evidence is progressively composed during reasoning, limiting both answer accuracy and traceability. In this paper, we cast LongDocVQA as an explicit evidence graph reasoning problem rather than implicit answer prediction. To this end, we propose DocTrace, a hierarchical framework that progressively performs evidence localization, structured document parsing, and evidence graph reasoning to enable explicit evidence provenance. To effectively learn these capabilities, we develop a two-stage training framework: joint Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) first initializes evidence localization and graph reasoning abilities, followed by task-specific Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with dedicated rewards to further optimize these capabilities. Extensive experiments on MMLongBench-Doc, LongDocURL, and SlideVQA demonstrate that DocTrace consistently outperforms both existing open-source baselines and proprietary MLLMs. Compared with the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone, DocTrace achieves absolute improvements of 14.4, 11.3, and 11.7 points on the three benchmarks, respectively. Beyond competitive performance, DocTrace constructs traceable evidence graphs with explicit node-level provenance, enabling transparent and verifiable reasoning for long document understanding.

</details>


### [45] [Distractor-Aware Truncation: Disentangling Context-Length Effects from Signal Loss in Long-Context LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03297)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验了检索增强与记忆增强语言模型中"较短上下文更优"的经典主张，通过在两个长上下文基准上以四种保留比例和两种截断协议（朴素中段删除 vs. 干扰物感知删除）测试多个模型，发现性能下降完全源于朴素截断常会删掉答案相关内容，而非上下文窗口本身的效果；当保留信号时，性能保持甚至提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中广泛声称短上下文在保留相关信息时更优，但该主张的实证基础存在缺陷：先前工作普遍采用简单的中间截断协议，未区分任务相关的"信号"与无关的"干扰物"，因此无法确证性能变化究竟来自上下文长度本身还是信号丢失。作者旨在揭示这一方法论漏洞并检验其在多模型、多供应商之间的普遍性。

Method: 作者选用 BABILong 和 GraphWalks(BFS) 两个长上下文基准的每个样本，在 100%、75%、50%、25% 四个上下文保留比例下运行，实施两种截断协议：(1) 朴素协议——从提示中间删除内容；(2) 干扰物感知协议——识别每个样本的任务相关内容并仅删除其余部分。评估了 Claude 家族三种规模（Haiku 4.5、Sonnet 4.6、Opus 4.7）以及跨供应商的 GPT-5.5，并在 MRCR v2 和 Oolong 两个额外基准上复现相同协议。统计分析采用配对 Wilcoxon 检验并做 Holm 校正。

Result: 朴素截断下，所有 BABILong 和 GraphWalks 单元格的分数均单调崩溃（Holm 校正后 p_adj < 0.05）。干扰物感知协议下性能保持或改善：两个较小的 Claude 模型在 BABILong 上显示出统计学显著的增益，而较大的模型（Opus 4.7 和 GPT-5.5）保持在满上下文的天花板水平。该模式在 GPT-5.5 上成功复现，排除了单一供应商的伪影。机制分析显示，朴素协议下 25% 保留比例时答案相关内容幸存率不足 1%。

Conclusion: 朴素中间截断并非对上下文窗口效应的测量，而只是衡量中间删除碰巧保留答案的频率。未来研究上下文长度效应的研究必须明确区分信号与干扰物，否则结论将在"较短更优"与"较短更劣"两个相反假设之间含糊不清。

Abstract: A standard claim in the literature on retrieval-augmented and memory-augmented language models is that shorter context is better when the relevant information is preserved. We test this claim by running every sample of two long-context benchmarks -- BABILong and GraphWalks (BFS) -- at four context-retention fractions (100%, 75%, 50%, 25%) under two truncation protocols. The first is the naive protocol implicitly used in much prior work: drop content from the middle of the prompt. The second is distractor-aware: identify the task-relevant content for each sample and drop only the rest. We evaluate three sizes of the Claude family (Haiku 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, Opus 4.7) and, to test cross-provider generality, GPT-5.5 from a different provider; we apply the same protocol to two further benchmarks (MRCR v2, Oolong). Under naive truncation, score collapses monotonically (paired Wilcoxon, Holm-corrected p_adj < 0.05 in all eight BABILong and GraphWalks cells). Under the distractor-aware protocol -- which preserves the signal by construction -- performance is preserved or improves: the two smaller Claude models show statistically significant gains on BABILong, while the larger models (Opus 4.7 and GPT-5.5) sit at their full-context ceiling. The naive collapse and its distractor-aware recovery replicate on GPT-5.5, ruling out a single-provider artifact. The mechanism is direct: under the naive protocol the answer-bearing content survives in fewer than 1% of samples at 25% retention; under the distractor-aware protocol it is preserved by construction. The naive protocol is therefore not a measurement of context-window effects; it is a measurement of how often middle-removal happens to spare the answer. We conclude that future studies of context-length effects must specify how they distinguish signal from distractor, or they are at best ambiguous between two opposite hypotheses.

</details>


### [46] [SeaSlides: Semantic Abstraction Layer for Agentic Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03298)
*Shengjun Fang,Chenyang Wu,Zongzhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeaSlides是一个基于语义抽象层的智能体幻灯片生成框架，通过在HTML和Typst两个后端中实现模板驱动的结构化内容编写，将布局、样式和渲染交给模板负责，同时通过能力模块将公式、代码和图表路由到专用渲染器，并辅以三阶段反馈机制来定位构建错误、约束违规和视觉缺陷。在两个基准测试（128任务的UltraPresent和新增的32任务SeaSlidesBench-Rich）上，SeaSlides相比SVG重型生成方法在可读性和内容保真度上表现更优，并在四模型中的三个上取得最丰富的宏平均成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻灯片生成系统存在能力不匹配问题：模板方法保持规则性但限制了适应性，而自由式HTML或SVG生成赋予模型灵活性却需要处理底层渲染细节。这导致包含公式、代码或数据图表的长篇技术幻灯片在生成时非常脆弱，需要一个既能保持内容忠实度又能实现专业视觉设计的中间抽象方案。

Method: SeaSlides引入了语义抽象层作为核心设计原则：模型编写结构化幻灯片内容（通过可复用组件和能力模块），而模板负责布局、样式和渲染。该原则分别在HTML（通过模板定义的DOM组件）和Typst（通过模板函数和包支持模块）两个后端独立实现。能力模块将方程、代码和图表路由到专用渲染器，三个反馈阶段在导出前定位构建错误、项目约束违规和视觉缺陷。两个后端共享相同的编写边界但保留各自的语法和契约。

Result: 在评估中，结合了128任务的UltraPresent验证设置和新增的32任务SeaSlidesBench-Rich基准（涵盖数学、代码、伪代码、表格、图表和图示），使用四个生成模型进行测试。两个SeaSlides后端生成的源代码比SVG重型生成方法更具可读性和内容导向性。SeaSlides后端在四个模型中的三个上取得了最高的丰富内容宏平均值，同时保持了具有竞争力的整体定性表现。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持语义抽象作为跨演示后端的实用编写原则。SeaSlides通过将内容创作与渲染细节解耦，成功弥合了模板固定性和自由式生成之间的鸿沟，为生成长篇技术演示提供了一种更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic presentation generation must preserve source content, maintain coherent visual design, render specialized objects, and produce usable artifacts. Existing systems meet only part of this requirement: templates preserve regularity but restrict adaptation, whereas free-form HTML or SVG gives models flexibility at the cost of low-level rendering decisions. This mismatch makes long technical decks brittle, especially when slides contain formulas, code, or data graphics. We present SeaSlides, an agentic slide-generation framework built around a semantic abstraction layer. Rather than authoring coordinates, inline styles, or raw SVG geometry, the model writes structured slide content through reusable components and capability modules, while templates own layout, style, and rendering. We instantiate this principle separately in HTML and Typst: SeaSlides-HTML uses template-defined DOM components, whereas SeaSlides-Typst uses template functions and package-backed modules. Capability modules route equations, code, and charts to dedicated renderers, and three feedback stages localize build errors, project-constraint violations, and visual defects before export. The two systems retain backend-specific syntax and contracts while sharing the same authoring boundary. For evaluation, we combine the 128-task UltraPresent validation setting with SeaSlidesBench-Rich, a new 32-task benchmark stressing mathematics, code, pseudocode, tables, charts, and diagrams. Across four generation models, both SeaSlides backends produce more readable, content-oriented source than SVG-heavy generation. A SeaSlides backend attains the highest rich-content macro-average under three of the four models while maintaining competitive overall qualitative performance. These results support semantic abstraction as a practical authoring principle across presentation backends.

</details>


### [47] [Screenshots or Tools? Eliciting Tool Use and Managing Multimodal Context in Hybrid GUI-MCP Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03327)
*Siqi Fan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Wenhui Tan,Xiusheng Huang,Juntong Wu,Liujie Zhang,Shuo Shang,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究混合型计算机使用代理（可同时通过截图和文本工具操作）中工具使用行为的影响。在OSWorld-MCP基准上，相同的MCP工具对推理模型提升+4.0pp，但对非推理模型降低-5.9pp，差异源于工具决策行为。研究发现"采用缺口"（adoption gap）：即使推理模型也仅在309个任务中的55个调用工具。多轮RL实验表明，密集工具奖励虽能提升工具采用率（0.03→0.33），但泛化精度不随之提升——行为可塑但能力不可塑。通过高效压缩观察（去除冗余截图并将图像历史减半），输入成本降低约三分之一，重训练后压缩代理在53%的输入成本下达到37.8%的精度（对比未压缩的33.0%）。结论是：工具只有在模型主动选择并整合时才有效，当前混合代理浪费了大量可选的工具使用机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合型计算机使用代理（hybrid computer-use agents）既能通过截图感知界面，又能调用文本工具，但在同一观察框架和工具集下，不同模型对工具的使用效果截然不同（推理模型受益+4.0pp，非推理模型受损-5.9pp）。作者希望揭示这一差异的根本原因，并探究工具采用率低（adoption gap）背后的机制以及能否通过训练来弥补。

Method: 作者在OSWorld-MCP基准（309个任务）上使用统一的GUI-MCP（Multimodal Context Protocol）框架进行实验。通过5轮对比实验分离推理与非推理模型的工具决策行为差异；通过多轮RL（multi-turn RL）设计探测实验，在行动层面引入密集工具奖励，在上下文层面引入压缩观察（去除工具调用后的冗余截图并减半图像历史），并与重新训练相结合。

Result: （1）相同工具对不同模型效果相反：推理模型+4.0pp，非推理模型-5.9pp，差异由工具决策行为（忽略、误命名、错误终止）导致；（2）推理模型仅调用工具55/309次，覆盖23.9%的工具可达任务，存在"采用缺口"；（3）密集工具奖励将电子表格工具采用率从0.03提升到0.33并泛化到贪婪解码，但留存精度不随之提升——行为可塑但能力不可塑，瓶颈在工具调用语义；（4）去除冗余截图、减半图像历史的压缩策略降低约1/3输入token，重训练后消除精度损失；（5）压缩代理以53%输入成本达到37.8%精度（对比未压缩的33.0%），且在预注册的降级子集上将富-贫差距缩小到零。

Conclusion: 工具的价值关键在于模型是否主动选择并正确整合工具调用，而非单纯提供工具API。当前的混合代理在大量任务中未充分利用可用的工具机会，根本原因是模型已有更廉价的路径（截图）且从未被训练去优先选择工具。行为（工具采用）可通过奖励信号控制，但实际能力（任务精度）的提升需要训练工具的调用语义本身，而非仅激励调用行为。

Abstract: Hybrid computer-use agents can act through screenshots or call text tools. We find that having a tool available does not settle which way the effect goes. Under one identical GUI-MCP harness on the OSWorld-MCP benchmark (309 tasks), the same MCP tools improve a reasoning model by +4.0pp and degrade a non-reasoning model by -5.9pp (5 runs each, both beyond 2 SE). What separates the two is tool-decision behavior. The non-reasoning policy ignores, misnames, or falsely terminates around tools. The reasoning model avoids these failures, yet still calls a tool on only 55/309 tasks, 23.9% of the tool-reachable ones. We call this shortfall the adoption gap. Both levels of the problem share one cause: the model already has a cheaper route and is never trained to take it. Multi-turn RL probes that cause. At the action level, a dense tool bonus raises spreadsheet adoption 0.03 -> 0.33 and carries into greedy decoding, but held-out accuracy does not follow. Behavior is steerable; competence is not. The bottleneck lies in tool-call semantics. At the context level, a successful tool call often makes the next screenshot redundant. Dropping it and halving image history cuts input tokens by about a third, at a small accuracy cost. Retraining under the same observation rule removes that cost. The compressed agent then reaches 37.8% against 33.0% for the uncompressed operating point, at 53% of the input cost, and closes the rich-lean gap on a pre-registered degraded subset to zero. Tools help when the model chooses and integrates them, and current hybrid agents leave many such choices unused.

</details>


### [48] [Long-term Traffic Scene Prediction via Polynomial Representations in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03330)
*Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文围绕自动驾驶场景预测中的核心挑战，提出基于多项式表示的鲁棒且计算高效的模型。通过理论与实践验证，证明中等阶次多项式能够高保真地捕捉真实运动动态，在轨迹与地图几何的统一多项式表示下达到近SOTA精度并显著提升分布偏移下的泛化能力；进一步扩展到基于扩散的多智能体场景生成框架，生成的交通延续比传统基线更合理且运动学一致。在Argoverse 2和Waymo Open数据集上的评估证实多项式表示降低了计算成本、增强了跨数据集泛化、产生了更平滑的轨迹和更高的行为合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于序列的轨迹表示方法在噪声鲁棒性和泛化能力方面存在不足，自动驾驶场景预测需要在保证预测合理性的同时提升计算效率和跨分布泛化能力。

Method: 采用多项式轨迹表示替代传统的序列表示；构建同时以多项式表示轨迹和地图几何的预测模型；在此基础上扩展为基于扩散的多智能体场景生成框架。

Result: 在Argoverse 2和Waymo Open基准上达到近SOTA精度，同时显著提升跨数据集泛化能力；生成的多智能体场景比传统基线更合理且运动学一致；多项式表示降低了计算成本、生成更平滑的轨迹和更高的行为合理性。

Conclusion: 标准分布内评测和基于回归的指标可能无法真实反映模型的泛化能力和预测合理性；多项式轨迹表示是自动驾驶安全关键场景预测中高效、有表达力且可泛化的基础表示方法。

Abstract: This thesis addresses fundamental challenges in traffic scene prediction for autonomous driving by introducing robust and computationally efficient models based on polynomial representations. While conventional sequence-based representations often struggle with noise and generalization, this work demonstrates that polynomial representations offer significant advantages in computational efficiency, generalization, and prediction plausibility. Through theoretical analysis and empirical validation, this thesis demonstrates that moderate-degree polynomials capture real-world motion dynamics with high fidelity without constraining predictive performance. Building on this foundation, a prediction model representing both trajectories and map geometry with polynomial representations achieves near state-of-the-art accuracy on standard benchmarks while substantially improving generalization under distribution shift. Extending this concept, a diffusion- based generative framework enables multi-agent scene generation, producing traffic continuations that are more plausible and kinematically consistent than those generated by conventional baselines. Evaluations on the Argoverse 2 and Waymo Open datasets confirm that polynomial representations reduce computational cost, enhance cross-dataset generalization, and yield smoother trajectories and higher behavioral plausibility. The findings reveal that standard in-distribution evaluation and regression-based metrics may fail to reflect true model generalization and prediction plausibility. By providing theoretical justification and empirical validation, this dissertation estab- lishes polynomial trajectory representations as an efficient, expressive, and generalizable foundation for traffic scene prediction in safety critical autonomous driving.

</details>


### [49] [Traceable Multi-Agent System for Knowledge-Based Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03339)
*Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Hyeokjun Choe,Soonyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TraceMAS，一个用于可追踪多代理预测的交互式演示系统。该系统通过两种因果循环图（Ideal CLD和Data-Grounded CLD）组织代理输出，将文档中的因果因素与数据、特征和模型设计关联起来，使自主预测代理的"证据到预测"过程可审查。系统在原油价格预测场景中进行了演示，允许用户比较预测迭代、审查代理级修订、探索因果映射以及连接情景预测与市场叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业预测日益依赖自主代理来解析文档、搜索数据、生成代码和修订模型，但代理的自主性导致从业者难以检查预测变化的原因、支撑变化的证据以及数据与建模选择的修订过程。现有系统缺乏对"证据到预测"全流程的可解释性和可追溯性。

Method: TraceMAS围绕两种因果循环表示组织代理输出：1) Ideal CLD，从领域文档中提取关键因素及其因果关系的理想因果循环图；2) Data-Grounded CLD，将因素与内部变量、外部数据或文档化代理连接起来的数据性因果循环图。Data-Grounded CLD指导特征构建与模型设计，同时保留文本证据、数据选择与模型修订之间的连接。系统提供了交互式界面，支持预测迭代比较、代理级修订审查、因果图探索、特征-数据映射与模型架构审查等功能。

Result: 在原油价格预测场景中成功演示了TraceMAS。演示界面使用户能够比较预测迭代、检查代理级修订、探索因果图、审阅特征-数据映射和模型架构，并将情景预测与市场叙事相连接。结果表明该系统能在保持自主预测代理灵活性的同时，使证据到预测的整个过程具有可检查性。

Conclusion: TraceMAS展示了自主预测代理可以在保有灵活性的同时，通过因果循环图的组织方式使从证据到预测的整个过程透明可查，为可追踪的多代理预测系统提供了可行的交互式展示方案。

Abstract: Enterprise forecasting increasingly relies on autonomous agents that interpret documents, search for data, generate code, and revise models. While this autonomy helps build adaptive forecasting pipelines, it also makes it difficult for practitioners to inspect why a forecast changed, which evidence supported the change, and how data and modeling choices were revised. We present TraceMAS, an interactive demo system for traceable multi-agent forecasting. TraceMAS organizes agent outputs around two causal-loop representations: an Ideal Causal Loop Diagram (Ideal CLD), which captures key factors and their causal relations extracted from domain documents, and a Data-Grounded Causal Loop Diagram (Data-Grounded CLD), which links those factors to internal variables, external data, or documented proxies. The Data-Grounded CLD guides feature construction and model design while preserving the connection between textual evidence, data choices, and model revisions. We demonstrate TraceMAS on crude oil price forecasting. The demo interface allows users to compare forecasting iterations, inspect agent-level revisions, explore causal maps, review feature-data mappings and model architecture, and connect scenario forecasts to market narratives. This demonstration shows how autonomous forecasting agents can retain flexibility while making the evidence-to-forecast process inspectable.

</details>


### [50] [MMLongBench-Doc-V2: A Corrected-Annotation, Semantics-Aware Revision of MMLongBench-Doc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03397)
*Mingtian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MMLongBench-Doc-V2 是对长文档问答基准 MMLongBench-Doc 的修正版本，纠正了 106 处标注错误、替换了字符串比较指标为固定的 LLM 评判、移除了文件错误和重复问题，最终保留 1,071 个问题覆盖 134 篇文档，并公开了可复现的修正流程与评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原版 MMLongBench-Doc 基准存在两个严重缺陷：一是参考指标直接比较提取出的字符串，导致"1,358,000"与"1358000"这类数值格式差异被误判为错误；二是相当比例的 ground-truth 标注存在错误、歧义或不完整，且这些错误恰好集中在能力强的系统能正确回答的问题上，从而系统性扭曲了测量分数所代表的真实能力。

Method: 作者通过人工复核修正了 106 处标注，每条修正都附有页码与算术依据；用固定的 LLM judge 替代字符串相似度指标，判断回答是否在语义上等同于参考答案；移除了 10 个文档文件名错误的问题和 1 个重复问题；并提出一个可复用的判定程序，用于决定何时可以放宽空集合键、何时放宽会破坏刻意构造的负样本，该程序应用于全部 208 行，其中 14 行被放宽。

Result: 修正后的基准包含 1,071 个问题、134 篇文档。V2 分数与已发布的 V1 分数不可直接比较。修正语料、逐条目修正记录和评估框架均在 GitHub 上开源。

Conclusion: 作者强调该工作的可复用贡献在于一个空集合键放宽的判定程序，并明确告知用户不要将 V2 结果与 V1 直接对比，体现了对基准评估严谨性和可复现性的重视。

Abstract: MMLongBench-Doc is a long-document QA benchmark of 1,082 questions over 135 PDFs. Two properties of it push measured scores away from the quantity they are meant to capture: the reference metric compares extracted answers, so 1,358,000 loses to 1358000; and a non-trivial share of ground-truth annotations are wrong, ambiguous, or incomplete --- concentrated, because of how they were found, in exactly the questions capable systems answer correctly. MMLongBench-Doc-V2 corrects 106 annotations, each published with the page and arithmetic that settle it, and replaces the string metric with a pinned LLM judge asked whether a response means the reference. Ten questions whose document ships under the wrong filename are removed rather than counted wrong, along with one duplicated question, leaving 1,071 questions over 134 documents. The most reusable contribution is a decision procedure for when an empty set key may be widened and when widening would destroy a deliberate negative sample; applied to all 208 rows, it widened 14. V2 scores are not comparable with published V1 numbers. The corrected corpus, the per-entry correction record and the evaluation harness are available at https://github.com/VectifyAI/MMLongBench-Doc-V2.

</details>


### [51] [Towards Robust Tool Use in Agents via Experience-Driven Adaptive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03403)
*Can Wang,Haoran Chen,Li Yu,Ding Hao,Bohai Zhao,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpG机制，通过构建和精炼自适应指导来捕获每个工具的能力边界和最佳实践，从而增强智能体在各种运行条件下使用工具的鲁棒性。ExpG包含经验获取、经验蒸馏和经验复用三个阶段，实验表明其在工具选择、工具调用和响应生成任务上带来一致改进，使小型智能体在鲁棒工具使用上超越未使用ExpG的大型智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的性能瓶颈正从模型能力转向执行过程的鲁棒性，工具作为智能体与外部环境交互的主要接口至关重要，但现有方法很少关注确保在多样化运行条件下鲁棒地使用工具。

Method: ExpG包含三个阶段：(1) 经验获取——从历史执行轨迹中分析工具调用质量，通过多方面的归因分析生成结构化的可学习经验；(2) 经验蒸馏——通过过滤无帮助经验保持经验池有效性，使用基于等价类的方法选择代表性经验，并将其总结为可泛化的指导；(3) 经验复用——在未来的任务求解中自适应地应用这些指导。

Result: 大量实验表明ExpG在工具选择、工具调用和响应生成任务上均带来一致的改进，使较小的智能体能够超越未使用ExpG的较大智能体。此外，ExpG在具有挑战性的设置中取得了特别显著的提升。

Conclusion: ExpG为实现更鲁棒的工具使用提供了一条有前景的路径，其代码、实验和结果均已公开可用。

Abstract: The performance bottleneck of agents is increasingly shifting from model capability to the robustness of their execution processes. Tools play a central role as the primary interface through which agents interact with external environments, yet existing methods rarely focus on ensuring robust tool use across diverse runtime conditions. To address this problem, we propose ExpG, a mechanism that builds and refines adaptive guidance capturing each tool's capability boundaries and best practices, thereby enabling agents to use tools more robustly and effectively. ExpG consists of three phases: (1) experience acquisition, which analyzes tool invocation quality from historical execution trajectories, producing structured learnable experiences through multi-aspect attribution; (2) experience distillation, which keeps the experience pool effective by filtering unhelpful experiences, selecting representative ones with an equivalence-class-based method, and summarizing them into generalizable guidance; and (3) experience reuse, which applies the guidance adaptively during future task solving. Extensive experiments show that ExpG brings consistent improvements across the tool selection, tool calling, and response generation tasks, enabling smaller agents to outperform larger ones that do not use ExpG. Moreover, ExpG achieves particularly strong gains in challenging settings, suggesting a promising path toward more robust tool use. Our code, experiments, and results are available.

</details>


### [52] [Enactive Artificial Intelligence: A Decision-Centric Architecture for Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03413)
*Zuojun Max Shen,Yuan Qu,Pujun Zhang,Anbang Liu,Yunhao Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"能动AI"（Enactive AI）作为面向企业和工业复杂系统的概念框架，通过四个互补角色（组织世界、站点世界、模式智能、能动决策周期）将AI从模型能力扩展到系统感知行动，以实现可靠、可治理且具有社会价值的AI部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI实践已超越大语言模型和文本/图像生成，越来越需要集成工具、智能体与执行框架来解决真实商业和工业问题。然而，现实复杂系统中的可靠性、可行性、韧性和责任性要求使得AI能力在这些系统中尚未得到充分验证。需要一个将AI能力与真实复杂系统决策深度融合的概念框架。

Method: 综合相邻领域研究，提出了一个由四个互补角色组成的"能动AI"概念框架：(1) 组织世界——定义运营管理逻辑和企业背后的组织行为世界模型，面向战略制度层面；(2) 站点世界——定义物理受限的工业优化与执行世界模型，面向运营实现层面；(3) 模式智能——通过双模型提供两个世界之间的耦合机制，编织各种AI应用；(4) 能动决策周期——触发自演化动态过程以更新和审计整个框架。

Result: 该框架通过凸显复杂系统中的决策智能，将AI前沿从模型能力扩展到系统感知行动，为可扩展、可治理且具有社会价值的AI部署开辟了新可能。AI进步的衡量标准将从模型能生成或自动化什么，转向智能系统如何可靠地支持复杂系统中的重要行动、负责任的治理和持久的社会价值。

Conclusion: 能动AI指出了企业级和工业复杂系统AI研究的下一前沿方向，即AI的进步不应仅以模型的生成或自动化能力来度量，而应以智能系统在塑造现代生活的复杂系统中支持关键行动、负责任治理和持久社会价值的可靠性来度量。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) continues to evolve and mature, recent AI practices have moved beyond large language models (LLMs) and text or image generation tasks, increasingly integrating tools, agents, and harnesses to solve real business and industrial problems. However, the power of AI is not verified under these real-world complex systems for various reasons, considering reliability, feasibility, resilience, and responsibility requirements in real commercial and industrial operations. This study synthesizes adjacent research and introduces Enactive AI as a conceptual framework for enterprise and industry reasoning, site-level decision support, and execution feedback. Four complementary roles organize the framework: an Organizational World defines operations management logic and an organizational behavior world model behind an enterprise from a strategic-institutional horizon; a Site World defines a physically bounded industrial optimization and execution world model from an operational-realization horizon; Schema Intelligence provides the coupling mechanism between two world models to weave various AI applications via two models; and Enactive Decision Cycle triggers the self-evolving dynamic process to update and audit the entire framework. By foregrounding decision intelligence in complex systems, Enactive AI expands the frontier of AI from model capability to system-aware action, opening new possibilities for scalable, governable, and socially valuable AI deployment. Enactive AI points toward a future in which AI progress is measured not only by what models can generate or automate, but by how reliably intelligent systems can support consequential action, responsible governance, and durable social value in the complex systems that shape modern life, which we believe will define the next frontier of AI research for enterprise-level and industrial complex systems.

</details>


### [53] [AI World Cup 2026: Benchmarking Large Language Models for End-to-End Football Tournament Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03416)
*Jonaid Shianifar,Iias Faiud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了"AI世界杯"基准测试，让十个LLM助手基于相同信息、格式和评分规则对2026年FIFA世界杯全部104场比赛进行赛前预测。结果显示GPT-5.5 Thinking以744分夺冠，且是唯一预测西班牙夺冠的模型（西班牙最终1-0击败阿根廷夺冠）。总分主要取决于淘汰赛表现（r=0.986），与小组赛表现几乎无关；逐场准确性排序与总分排序不同，置信度与结果无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被用于真实世界事件预测，但由于不同的信息输入、工具使用和评估规则，模型之间的比较往往缺乏公平性。本文旨在通过严格统一的基准测试框架，解决LLM预测能力评估中缺乏标准化比较的问题。

Method: 设置了AI世界杯基准：十个LLM助手在相同的锦标赛快照、提示词、JSON格式和评分程序下，对2026年FIFA世界杯做出赛前一次性预测，涵盖小组赛比分、小组排名、淘汰赛对阵、最终名次、置信度和简短解释。总计104场比赛，采用统一评分规则进行打分与相关性分析。

Result: GPT-5.5 Thinking以744分夺冠，GPT-5.5（717）、Gemini（699）、Qwen 3.7（687）紧随其后。总分与淘汰赛得分强相关（r=0.986），但与小组赛比分得分（r=0.055）、小组排名得分（r=-0.103）及赛前总分（r=-0.054）几乎无关。Claude Sonnet 4.6在小组赛逐场准确率最高（63.89%）但总排名第六。平均置信度与准确率（r=-0.060）和总分（r=-0.067）均无关。

Conclusion: 完整赛事的整体预测与逐场比赛预测考察的是不同的能力；基于淘汰赛对阵表的排行榜高度依赖评分设计。基准材料、原始响应和评分代码已公开以供复现和扩展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now regularly asked to forecast real-world events, but comparisons are often difficult because models receive different information, use different tools, and are evaluated under different rules. This paper reports the completed \emph{AI World Cup} benchmark, in which ten LLM-based assistants made a single pre-tournament forecast of the entire 2026 FIFA World Cup. Every submission used the same tournament snapshot, prompt, JSON schema, and scoring procedure. The forecasts covered group-stage scores, group rankings, the knockout bracket, final placings, confidence values, and short explanations. After all 104 matches had been played, GPT-5.5 Thinking finished first with 744 points, followed by GPT-5.5 with 717, Gemini with 699, and Qwen 3.7 with 687. GPT-5.5 Thinking was also the only model to select Spain, which defeated Argentina 1--0 in the final, as champion. The final ranking was driven mainly by knockout performance: total score was strongly correlated with knockout points ($r=0.986$), but showed little relationship with group-stage match points ($r=0.055$), group-standing points ($r=-0.103$), or their combined pre-knockout score ($r=-0.054$). Match-level accuracy produced a different ordering. Claude Sonnet 4.6 correctly predicted the largest number of group-stage outcomes (63.89\%) but placed sixth overall. Average self-reported confidence was also unrelated to either outcome accuracy ($r=-0.060$) or total score ($r=-0.067$). The results suggest that forecasting a complete tournament tests something different from predicting matches one at a time, while also showing how strongly a bracket-based leaderboard can depend on scoring design. The benchmark materials, raw responses, and scoring code are released to support replication and future extensions.

</details>


### [54] [Towards Improving Sequential Decision-Making in LLM Agents via Experience Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03420)
*Jakub Rada,Viliam Lisý*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在完全可观测的双人零和博弈中的顺序决策能力，发现LLM在简单游戏（如井字棋、四子棋）中表现欠佳，输给MCTS对手；通过混淆游戏表面形式但保留游戏树，性能几乎不变，说明表现差距并非源于记忆策略的回忆。作者提出一个带有经验记忆的智能体框架，通过赛后反思和规则提取，在不修改模型权重的情况下显著改善了井字棋的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在单次推理任务上已有显著进步，但在顺序决策场景中的表现尚不明确。选择完全可观测的双人零和博弈作为测试床，因为其结果由规则决定、无需依赖裁判模型即可评估单步最优性，从而提供真实的性能评估基准。

Method: 在多种LLM模型上测试井字棋和四子棋等简单游戏，对比MCTS对手；通过保持游戏树不变但改写表面形式的混淆实验，排除记忆策略回忆的影响；提出一个面向顺序决策的智能体框架，包含经验记忆模块，利用赛后反思和规则提取来改进决策，无需修改模型权重。

Result: 所有测试的LLM模型在简单游戏中均表现次优，输给MCTS对手；混淆实验表明性能差距并非由记忆策略导致；引入带经验记忆的智能体框架后，井字棋表现获得可测量的提升。

Conclusion: LLM的顺序决策能力仍需改进，简单博弈中的次优表现反映了其在这类任务上的根本性局限。通过构建经验记忆和反思机制的智能体框架，可以在不修改模型权重的情况下缓解这一差距，为提升LLM在顺序决策场景中的能力提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Large language models have improved substantially on single-shot reasoning tasks, but their performance in sequential decision-making is less well understood. We study this on fully-observable two-player zero-sum games, which provide ground-truth evaluation: outcomes are determined by the rules, and optimality of individual moves can be computed or approximated, without relying on a judge model. Across model tiers, LLMs play suboptimally in simple games such as tic-tac-toe or Connect Four, and lose to MCTS opponents. Obfuscations that preserve the game tree but rewrite its surface form leave performance largely unchanged, indicating the gap is not fully explained by recall of memorized strategies. Motivated by this performance gap, we introduce an agentic framework enhanced with an experience memory designed for the sequential setting and addressing common challenges of sequential decision-making such as credit assignment. We show that post-game reflection and rule extraction yield measurable improvements on tic-tac-toe without modifying the model weights.

</details>


### [55] [State Propagation Also Satisfies: A Complex-Valued State-Space Model for Deterministic State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03425)
*Xiaohe Li,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Complex State Propagator (CSP)，一种极简的循环架构，仅通过复数域中的输入相关旋转来传播隐藏状态，无需中间输出投影，在奇偶校验、模计数、括号匹配等确定性状态跟踪任务上达到100%准确率和完美F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构虽在序列建模中占据主导地位，但其注意力机制对于奇偶校验、模计数、括号匹配等确定性状态跟踪任务可能过于复杂（"过度设计"）。作者认为仅通过状态传播（state propagation）就足以解决这类任务，无需注意力等复杂机制。

Method: 提出CSP架构：(1) 隐藏状态表示为复数向量，通过输入相关的复数域旋转进行更新；(2) 仅在各层间传播隐藏状态，中间步骤无输出投影；(3) 引入块级跳跃连接（block-level skip connection）；(4) 在序列边界采用逐元素复数归一化和SiLU激活；(5) 使用Focal Loss进行训练。

Result: 在奇偶校验、模计数、括号匹配等典型确定性状态跟踪任务上，CSP实现100%准确率和完美F1分数。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于确定性状态跟踪这类任务，仅依靠状态传播的极简循环架构就足以达到完美性能，说明了注意力机制在这些任务上可能并非必要，提供了比Transformer更轻量的替代方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have dominated sequence modeling, largely due to the expressive power of attention mechanisms. However, for a class of deterministic state tracking tasks---such as parity checking, modular counting, and parenthesis matching---attention may be overkill. In this paper, we show that \textbf{state propagation alone is sufficient}.
  We propose the \textbf{Complex State Propagator (CSP)}, a minimalistic recurrent architecture that \textbf{only propagates hidden states} across layers without output projections at intermediate steps. The state is represented as a complex-valued vector, updated via input-dependent rotations in the complex domain. To enable deep propagation without gradient vanishing or degradation, we introduce a \textbf{block-level skip connection} alongside element-wise complex normalization and SiLU activation at sequence boundaries. Applied with Focal Loss, CSP achieves \textbf{100\% accuracy} with perfect F1 scores across canonical tasks.

</details>


### [56] [DataSpace: Benchmarking Data Agents for Verifiable Analytics over Heterogeneous Workspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03451)
*Boyan Li,Zhuowen Liang,Yupeng Xie,Xiaotian Lin,Tianqi Luo,Xinyu Liu,Yizhang Zhu,Zhangyang Peng,Yuan Li,Zhengxuan Zhang,Jiayi Zhang,Nan Tang,Guoliang Li,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DataSpace，一个用于评估数据代理（data agents）在异构工作空间中生成可验证表格结果的基准测试，包含410个跨语言任务、7,439个工件（共15.01 GB），并配套确定性评估器。实验表明最佳准确率为66.34%，且多模态证据整合与连接操作会显著降低所有六种骨干模型的准确率，说明该基准尚未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将结构化查询、检索和开放式分析彼此割裂，未能统一覆盖异构证据发现、完整表格输出和确定性评估这三个关键方面，导致数据代理在真实组织工作空间中的可靠性难以被全面衡量。

Method: 提出DataSpace-Builder框架，包括跨语言转换、约束感知的关系采样、模态路由与工件渲染、以及11位领域专家的人工审查与任务修复。同时设计确定性评估器，支持列名不变的对齐、类型与精度感知的归一化、以及顺序感知的行比较。

Result: 在六个前沿多模态模型和五个代理框架上进行评估，最佳准确率为66.34%；固定骨干模型时，选择不同代理框架导致15.36个百分点的准确率差异；多模态证据整合和连接操作在所有六个骨干模型上均一致地降低准确率。

Conclusion: DataSpace基准尚未饱和，仍有较大提升空间；多模态证据整合与关系连接是阻碍数据代理可靠性的关键挑战，为后续研究指明了方向。该基准同时被用作KDD Cup 2026官方评测基准。

Abstract: Data agents enable natural-language analytics over organizational workspaces, where relevant evidence may be scattered across databases, structured files, long documents, and multimedia. Existing benchmarks largely isolate structured querying, retrieval, or open-ended analysis, leaving heterogeneous evidence discovery, complete tabular outputs, and deterministic evaluation insufficiently unified. We introduce DataSpace, a benchmark in which data agents produce verifiable tabular results from task-local heterogeneous workspaces. It contains 410 cross-language tasks and 7,439 artifacts totaling 15.01 GB across CSV, JSON, SQLite, Markdown, PDF, and video. DataSpace also served as the official evaluation benchmark for the KDD Cup 2026 Data Agents for Complex Data Analysis competition. Each agent receives only a question and workspace and returns the complete requested tabular result. We construct DataSpace with DataSpace-Builder, an execution-grounded framework comprising cross-language transformation, constraint-aware relational sampling, modality routing and artifact rendering, and human review and task repair by 11 domain experts. A deterministic evaluator performs header-invariant column alignment, type- and precision-aware normalization, and order-aware row comparison. Across six recently released frontier multimodal models and five widely used agent harnesses, the best accuracy reaches 66.34%, while harness choice creates a 15.36-point spread with the backbone fixed. Multimodal evidence integration and joins consistently reduce accuracy across all six backbones. These results show that DataSpace remains unsaturated and identify key challenges for improving data-agent reliability.

</details>


### [57] [LLaDA MoE v2: Scaling Mixture-of-Experts Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03457)
*Fengqi Zhu,Shaoxuan Xu,Jingyang Ou,Zebin You,Yipeng Xing,Huabin Liu,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Zhenzhong Lan,Yankai Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Jianguo Li,Chongxuan Li,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了扩散语言模型（dLLM）中混合专家（MoE）架构的扩展规律，发现其与自回归模型存在定量差异（更快的批大小增长、更快的学习率衰减、略偏数据侧的资源配置），并据此训练了30B-A3B的LLaDA MoE v2模型，在2/3预训练token量下接近Qwen3性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型作为自回归语言建模的替代方案具有潜力，但MoE架构下的dLLM扩展行为尚不清楚，缺乏系统性理解，阻碍了该类模型的大规模训练与优化。

Method: 系统研究三方面扩展规律：(1) 优化超参数（名义批大小和学习率随计算量的缩放）；(2) 计算分配（通过IsoFLOP分析确定token预算与激活参数的最优比例）；(3) MoE架构（专家池规模、专家粒度、共享专家激活比例等超参数随规模的变化）。基于这些发现，从零训练了LLaDA MoE v2（30B-A3B，23.5T tokens）。

Result: 优化上：最优批大小增长更快，最优学习率衰减更快；IsoFLOP分析显示最优token预算比激活模型侧计算增长更快（稍微偏向数据侧）；架构上：更大规模偏好更大的专家池，适中的专家粒度始终有效，共享专家激活比例随规模保持稳定。LLaDA MoE v2在65%的预训练token量下接近Qwen3，SFT后在一半以上的推理和编码基准上超越SDAR Chat且接近Qwen3。

Conclusion: 建立了MoE dLLM的实用扩展定律和设计原则，证明扩散语言模型在合理规模下可以接近自回归模型的性能，为未来dLLM的大规模训练提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) offer an alternative to autoregressive (AR) language modeling, yet the scaling behavior of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) dLLMs remains poorly understood. We systematically characterize how optimization hyperparameters, compute allocation, and architecture scale for MoE dLLMs, identifying quantitative differences from scaling trends previously reported for AR models. Specifically, for optimization, the optimal nominal batch size grows faster, while the optimal learning rate decays more rapidly with compute. For model--data allocation, IsoFLOP analysis reveals a slight data-side tilt: the optimal token budget grows faster than activated model-side computation. For MoE architecture, larger scales increasingly favor larger expert pools at fixed activated capacity, while moderate expert granularity remains consistently effective and the preferred fraction of activated capacity assigned to shared experts remains stable across scales. Guided by these findings, we train LLaDA MoE v2, a 30B-A3B dLLM, from scratch on 23.5T tokens. With approximately 65\% as many pretraining tokens as Qwen3, LLaDA MoE v2 approaches Qwen3 on several knowledge, reasoning, and coding benchmarks. After supervised fine-tuning alone, it outperforms SDAR Chat on seven of eight reasoning and coding benchmarks and remains close to Qwen3 on several tasks. These results establish practical scaling laws and design principles for MoE dLLMs.

</details>


### [58] [Solver-Aware Decompositions for Programming-by-Example: When Dividing Requires Knowing how to Conquer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03461)
*Janis Zenkner,Tobias Sesterhenn,Tim Grams,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Solver-Aware Decomposition (SAD) 框架，挑战了 Program-by-example (PBE) 中分解器应模仿真实标注（GT）子目标的基本假设。SAD 在保留 GT 监督作为结构脚手架的同时，直接利用冻结合成器的损失反馈优化分解器，使子目标更契合求解器的搜索动力学而非标注者的分解偏好。实验揭示"准确性悖论"：分解器与 GT 分解的一致性越高，合成成功率反而越低。SAD 学会牺牲 GT 对齐换取求解器可处理性，在两个 PBE 领域上持续提升合成及端到端任务准确率，甚至能解决 GT 分解预言机都失败的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分解的 PBE 方法假设分解质量是任务固有的属性，训练分解器去模仿标注者提供的真实子目标。但作者指出，对于具有固定归纳偏好的有界求解器，GT 分解反映的是标注者的因子化选择而非求解器的搜索动态，导致分解器可能提出逻辑有效但求解器难以处理的中介子目标。

Method: SAD 框架包含两个训练信号：(1) 保留对 GT 子目标的监督学习作为结构脚手架，维持分解的基本结构合理性；(2) 引入冻结合成器的直接反馈——子目标根据合成器在目标程序上的损失被奖励，该损失作为子任务难度的信号，鼓励分解器产生求解器实际可行的分解。通过这种双重优化，分解器学会权衡 GT 对齐与求解器可处理性。

Result: 实验揭示了准确性悖论：与 GT 分解的一致性越高并不带来合成成功率提升，即便合成器本身就是在相同的 GT 数据上训练的。SAD 学习到的分解能在两个 PBE 领域上带来一致的合成和端到端任务准确率提升。此外，SAD 能够解决 GT 分解预言机（oracle）都无法解决的任务，强有力地证明 GT 分解并非对有界求解器普遍最优。

Conclusion: 分解质量不是任务的内在属性，而是相对于求解器的性质。SAD 通过将求解器反馈纳入分解器训练，实现了更优的任务分解策略，为 PBE 中的分解学习提供了新的设计原则：分解器的优化目标应与求解器的搜索能力对齐，而非与标注者的标注习惯对齐。

Abstract: Decomposition-based Programming-by-example (PBE) scales performance by splitting tasks into subtasks that a learned synthesizer solves: a decomposer predicts intermediate subgoals, and a synthesizer generates programs conditioned on them. Current approaches train the decomposer to imitate ground-truth ( GT) subgoals, implicitly treating decomposition quality as intrinsic to the task. We challenge this assumption: for bounded solvers with fixed inductive biases, GT decompositions reflect the annotator's factorization choices - not the solver's search dynamics. A decomposer trained to match GT decompositions may therefore propose subgoals that are logically valid yet intractable for the solver. We propose Solver-Aware Decomposition (SAD), a training framework that retains supervised training on GT subgoals as a structural scaffold, while additionally optimizing the decomposer via direct feedback from a frozen synthesizer. Subgoals are rewarded based on the synthesizer's loss on the target program - a signal of subtask difficulty that encourages decompositions the solver can act on. Our experiments reveal an accuracy paradox: higher agreement with GT decompositions does not improve synthesis success - even though the synthesizer was trained on the very same GT data the decomposer is optimized to mimic. SAD instead learns decompositions that trade GT alignment for solver tractability, yielding consistent gains in synthesis and end-to-end task accuracy across two PBE domains. Moreover, SAD solves tasks that a GT decomposition oracle fails - empirical evidence that GT decompositions are not universally optimal for bounded solvers, and that decomposition quality is solver-relative, not intrinsic.

</details>


### [59] [LeanMem: Simple and Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03463)
*Yuxin Liao,Le Wu,Min Hou,Hao Liu,Han Wu,Zishu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMem是一个轻量级长期记忆框架，根据历史对话内容的可压缩性、时间动态性及保真度需求，将信息分类存储为档案记忆、事件记忆和记录记忆三种类型，并在维护和推理阶段进行差异化处理，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上以最低或近最低的构建成本、推理token和延迟，实现最高15.1个点的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统对异构对话内容采用统一的摘要和检索流程，导致要么token消耗过多，要么不可逆地丢失细粒度证据。作者认为历史对话内容应根据其可压缩性、时间动态性和保真度需求进行差异化处理，以协调记忆效率与信息保真之间的权衡。

Method: LeanMem框架包含三步：首先过滤低价值内容；然后根据信息性质将有效片段存储为三类记忆——紧凑的档案记忆（profile memory）、时间结构化的事件记忆（event memory）和源可追溯的记录记忆（record memory）；维护阶段仅选择性更新动态演化的事件记忆，避免冗余整合稳定的档案和不变的记录；推理阶段根据查询证据需求动态选择记忆类型并分配检索预算，按需组装相关证据。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上，使用GPT-4.1-mini和Qwen3-8B两种模型，LeanMem在所有设置下均优于最强记忆基线，准确率提升最高达15.1个百分点，同时实现最低或近最低的构建成本、推理token消耗和延迟。

Conclusion: 通过对记忆内容进行分类差异化处理和按需检索，LeanMem证明了"一刀切"记忆管线的局限性，为LLM代理的长期记忆系统提供了一种兼顾效率与保真度的轻量级解决方案。代码和数据集已随论文提供。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM-based agents to sustain interactions and reliably leverage distant history. However, existing memory systems typically process heterogeneous dialogue content through a uniform summarization and retrieval pipeline, leading to either excessive token consumption or irreversible loss of fine-grained evidence. We argue that historical dialogue content should be handled differently according to its compressibility, temporal dynamics, and fidelity requirements. Based on this insight, we propose LeanMem, a lightweight long-term memory framework. LeanMem first filters out low-value content, then stores informative segments as compact profile memory, temporally structured event memory, or source-grounded record memory, depending on the nature of the information. During maintenance, only dynamically evolving event memories are selectively updated, avoiding redundant consolidation of stable profiles and immutable records. During inference, LeanMem dynamically selects memory types and allocates retrieval budgets according to query-specific evidence demands, assembling relevant evidence on demand. On LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S with GPT-4.1-mini and Qwen3-8B, LeanMem improves accuracy over the strongest memory-based baseline in every setting, by up to 15.1 points, at the lowest or near-lowest construction cost, inference tokens, and latency. The code and datasets are included in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [60] [ChartAnno: Evaluating MLLMs for Chart Annotation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03464)
*Zhenghan Chen,Zekai Shao,Lidan Tan,Xin Lin,Xingchen Zeng,Yi Shan,Ziyue Lin,Xiaoliang Fu,Xinyuan Liu,Yuetong Guo,Fen Wang,Bongshin Lee,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChartAnno基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在图表注释生成任务上的能力。该基准包含1,200张真实世界图表，配有代码和三种不同指令粒度的注释指令。作者评估了10个代表性MLLMs在两种主要输入设置（仅图表代码、代码+图表图像）及图像单独消融实验下的表现。结果显示专有模型整体更强，但大规模开源模型差距正在缩小；更具体的指令能提升注释质量，而抽象意图推断对当前MLLMs最具挑战性；提供图表图像带来的整体收益有限，主要提升体现在设计相关指标上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在图表理解、生成和编辑方面已取得显著进展，但对于已有图表的注释（annotation）能力尚待探索。图表注释是一项常见但具有挑战性的沟通任务，需要模型推断图表意图、理解图表语义并放置合适的文本或图形元素，目前缺乏系统性的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: 构建ChartAnno基准，包含1,200张真实世界图表、对应的代码和三种不同指令特异性的注释指令。在两种主要输入设置下评估10个代表性MLLMs：（1）仅提供图表代码；（2）同时提供图表代码和图表图像。此外还进行了仅提供图表图像的消融实验。

Result: 专有模型整体表现更强，但大规模开源模型正在缩小差距；指令越具体，注释质量越高；推断抽象意图是当前MLLMs最困难的部分；提供图表图像的整体收益有限，主要改进体现在设计相关指标上。

Conclusion: 图表注释生成是一项具有挑战性的任务，需要语义接地（semantic grounding）和有效的注释设计能力。该基准揭示了当前MLLMs在这一任务上的局限，为未来模型改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in chart understanding, generation, and editing, but their ability to annotate existing charts remains underexplored. Annotating charts is a common yet challenging communicative task, requiring models to infer intended messages, interpret chart semantics, and place appropriate textual or graphical elements. To address this gap, we introduce ChartAnno, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on chart annotation generation. It contains 1,200 real-world charts with paired code and annotation instructions across three levels of instruction specificity. We evaluate 10 representative MLLMs under two primary input settings: (1) chart code alone and (2) both chart code and chart image, and further include a chart image-only ablation study. Results show that proprietary models remain stronger overall, although large-scale open-source models narrow the gap. More specific instructions improve annotation quality, while inferring abstract intent remains most difficult for current MLLMs. Providing chart images brings limited overall gains, with improvements mainly appearing in design-related metrics. These findings highlight chart annotation generation as a challenging task requiring semantic grounding and effective annotation design. Code and data will be released in a future version.

</details>


### [61] [When Correct Solutions Repeat: Rarity-Aware Credit Redistribution for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03467)
*Zhe Cao,Miaowen Wen,Fangjiong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cue-GRPO，通过在GRPO中引入基于策略线索的分区条件规则，将正向优势按簇稀有度重新分配，缓解重复正确解导致的结构级信用集中问题，仅增加6%训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR/GRPO将每个正确完成视为独立学习信号，导致重复出现的正确解形式累积不成比例的正向信用，而稀有形式得到的信用不足，形成结构级信用偏差。

Method: 形式化"多重性导致的结构级信用集中"问题；提出分区条件规则，按簇稀有度重新分配正向优势；Cue-GRPO使用确定性策略线索构造基于rollout局部的验证正确轨迹分区，无需辅助模型推理；另提出Judge Partitions下的信用重新分配（CR-JP）方案。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-7B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，Cue-GRPO提升AIME重复采样性能，高采样预算下增益最大；训练开销仅比GRPO多6%。

Conclusion: 结构级信用再分配是RLVR中可行的设计维度，策略线索为竞赛数学提供了低开销实现方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) com- monly optimizes each correct completion as an independent learning signal. In GRPO, this completion-level uniformity creates structure-level skew: recurring correct solution forms accumulate positive coefficient mass in proportion to how often they are sampled, while rare forms receive limited credit. We formalize this behavior as multiplicity-induced structure-level credit concentration and introduce a partition- conditioned rule that redistributes positive advantages accord- ing to cluster rarity. Cue-GRPO instantiates this rule with- out auxiliary-model inference by using deterministic Strategy Cues to construct rollout-local partitions of verified-correct traces. Across Qwen2.5-Math-7B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Cue-GRPO improves AIME repeated-sampling performance, with the largest gains at high sampling budgets. Credit Re- distribution (CR) under Judge Partitions (JP) further indi- cates that the proposed redistribution mechanism can oper- ate with judge-derived partitions. Cue-GRPO adds only 6% wall-clock training overhead over GRPO. These results sup- port structure-level credit redistribution as a practical design axis for RLVR, with Strategy Cues providing a low-overhead implementation for competition mathematics. Code is avail- able at https://github.com/CzZ12/When-Correct-Solutions- Repeat-Rarity-Aware-Credit-Redistribution-for-GRPO.

</details>


### [62] [ToolLIFT: Lifting Tool-Specific Trajectories into Function-Level Graphs for Generalizable Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03468)
*Xiuhui You,Jiayi Luo,Zichao Shen,Qingyun Sun,Ziwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolLIFT框架，将工具级轨迹提升为函数级工作流图（FWG），通过解耦工作流规划与工具选择，并结合强化学习实现跨工具集的通用工具规划，在ID和OOD基准上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具规划方法直接从历史轨迹构建工具级图，这类图与特定工具绑定，难以泛化到新的工具集。作者发现，虽然涉及的工具不同，但相似任务往往共享函数级的工作流结构，这种抽象更适合跨工具集的泛化。

Method: ToolLIFT包含三个关键机制：(1) 轨迹提升机制，将工具特定轨迹编码为函数级工作流图（FWG），在不同工具间共享协作经验；(2) 基于FWG全局结构，解耦工作流规划与工具选择，使单个工具选择与整体工作流对齐；(3) 采用强化学习（RL），提出源门控奖励和技能特定奖励，确保工具调用间信息流的源可追溯性。

Result: 在两个分布内（ID）和三个分布外（OOD）基准上的实验表明，ToolLIFT持续优于最先进的基线方法，展现出对未见工具集的强大泛化能力。

Conclusion: 函数级工作流结构相比工具级图是更可迁移的工具规划抽象，ToolLIFT证明了该抽象在跨工具集泛化中的有效性。

Abstract: Historical tool-use trajectories provide valuable experience for large language model (LLM) agents to plan and coordinate tool usage. Existing approaches directly construct tool-level graphs from these trajectories, but the resulting graphs remain tied to specific tools and are hard to generalize across tool sets. To tackle this challenge, we find that despite differences in the tools involved, analogous tasks often share a common function-level workflow structure, which serves as a potentially more transferable abstraction for tool planning. Based on this insight, we propose ToolLIFT, a framework that lifts tool-specific trajectories into a function-level workflow graph (FWG) for generalizable tool planning. Specifically, we first propose a trajectory-lifting mechanism that encodes workflow structures in the FWG and shares collaboration experience across tools. Then, building on the global structure of the FWG, we introduce decoupled workflow planning and tool selection to align individual tool choices with the overall workflow. Lastly, to ensure reliable tool dataflow, we adopt Reinforcement Learning (RL) and propose source-gated and skill-specific rewards to maintain source-traceable information flow across tool calls. Experiments on two in-distribution (ID) and three out-of-distribution (OOD) benchmarks show that ToolLIFT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating strong generalization to unseen tool sets.

</details>


### [63] [WeClawArena: An Auditable Sandbox and Benchmark for Cross-User Agents Collaboration and Security in Human-Centered Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03499)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Aojie Yuan,Haiyue Zhang,Xiyang Hu,Yue Zhao,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WeClawArena是一个可审计的基准测试与运行时沙箱，用于多主体（multi-party）个人AI代理在个人工作空间上的协作，包含124个基础任务和620个场景变体（含1个良性对照和4种攻击向量），分别报告效用与攻击成功率，并支持对任务故障、隐私泄露、投毒证据和无效授权路径的诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体基准研究工具使用与协作，但缺乏一个端到端的沙箱来验证跨用户的智能体协作——用户的工作空间（文件、记录、工具、策略）在所有者之间不可直接可见，同时也没有测试有害行为如何通过以人为中心的智能体网络传播。需要填补这一空白。

Method: 构建WeClawArena基准与沙箱：1）设计124个基础任务，覆盖6个跨用户任务域；2）将每个基础任务扩展为5个场景变体（1个良性对照+4个攻击向量变体），总计620个变体；3）沙箱记录对等消息、工具调用、资源操作、治理决策和最终工作空间状态；4）分别报告效用与攻击成功率，并基于有界运行时证据审计攻击成功，支持诊断任务故障、隐私泄露、投毒证据与无效授权路径。

Result: WeClawArena提供了620个场景变体（124个基础任务×5个变体），并在沙箱中实现了完整的运行时审计能力，能够独立报告效用和攻击成功率，且从有界运行时证据中对攻击成功进行审计。

Conclusion: WeClawArena为多主体个人智能体协作提供了一个可验证、可审计的基准与沙箱环境，使研究者能够系统地评估协作效用与安全性（攻击成功率），并诊断各类安全问题，推动以人为中心的智能体网络的安全评测与落地。

Abstract: Recent advances in persistent personal-agent frameworks are making human-centered agent networks realistic deployment targets: each user can be served by an AI agent that acts on the user's behalf, maintains state, and communicates with other agents through social and task relations. In these networks, everyday tool use becomes multi-party owned-agent collaboration over personal workspaces, where files, records, tools, and policies are not directly visible across owners. Existing agent benchmarks study tool use and collaboration, but they do not provide an end-to-end sandbox for verifiable cross-user agent collaboration with realistic user digital workspaces or test how harmful actions can travel through the human-centered agent network. We introduce WeClawArena, an auditable benchmark and runtime sandbox for multi-party owned-agent collaboration over personal workspaces. WeClawArena targets collaborative tool-use tasks in which personal workspaces serve as both operational tools and personal constraints. The benchmark contains 124 base tasks across six cross-user task domains and expands them into 620 scenario variants, with one benign control and four attack-vector variants per base task. The sandbox records peer messages, tool calls, resource operations, governed decisions, and final workspace states. WeClawArena reports utility and attack success rate separately and audits attack success from bounded runtime evidence, supporting diagnosis of task breakdown, privacy leakage, poisoned evidence, and invalid authority paths.

</details>


### [64] [Can LLM design high-quality experiments? A Comprehensive and Systematic Benchmark on Autonomous Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03501)
*Zejun Liu,Jian Wu,Ru Peng,Yuliang Ji,Dongyuan Li,Renhe Jiang,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCOPE基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在实验设计中的能力，涵盖19个研究领域、300篇高质量论文，从高层规划完备性和底层配置准确性两个维度进行评估。同时提出OptED智能体工作流，通过阶段隔离、工具增强和规则约束来优化LLM的实验设计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI4Research利用AI自动化和改进科研流程，但先前工作主要聚焦于代码实现与执行，忽略了实验设计这一科研过程的关键阶段，且缺乏系统评估AI实验设计能力的基准。

Method: 构建SCOPE基准：从ICML、NeurIPS、ICLR等顶会中选取300篇覆盖19个研究领域的高质量论文，从两个维度评估LLM：高层规划完备性（主实验、消融实验、分析实验）和底层配置准确性与合理性（数据集、基线、指标）。提出OptED智能体工作流，通过阶段隔离、工具增强和规则约束来优化LLM实验设计。

Result: 基准测试发现三个关键结果：（1）大多数LLM无法直接设计高质量实验；（2）所有LLM在底层配置方面存在性能瓶颈；（3）搜索模式并未提升设计质量。

Conclusion: OptED通过阶段隔离、工具增强和规则约束有效缓解了LLM在底层配置上的瓶颈，为AI辅助实验设计提供了更优的解决路径。

Abstract: AI for Research (AI4Research) leverages AI to automate and improve scientific workflows. While experimental design is a critical stage of the research process, prior work has focused primarily on code implementation and execution, overlooking the importance of this stage, and no benchmark exists to evaluate AI's ability to conduct systematic experiment design. To bridge this gap, we propose SCOPE, a Scientific COmprehensive Planning Evaluation Benchmark constructed from 300 high-quality latest papers across 19 research domains from top-tier venues (e.g., ICML, NeurIPS, and ICLR),evaluating LLMs on two dimensions: High-Level planning completeness (main, ablation, and analysis experiments) and Low-Level configuration accuracy and rationality (datasets, baselines, and metrics). Benchmarking reveals three findings: (1) most LLMs cannot directly design high-quality experiments; (2) all LLMs exhibit a performance bottleneck in low-level configuration; and (3) search mode does not improve design quality. Furthermore, to address these challenges, we propose OptED, a novel agentic workflow to optimize LLM-based experimental design, that enhances LLM-based experimental planning through stage isolation, tool augmentation, and rule-based constraints, effectively alleviating the configuration bottleneck.

</details>


### [65] [Hybrid LLM-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agents for Complex Sequential Decision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03502)
*Christophe D. Hounwanou,John Emeka Eze,Yaé Ulrich Gaba*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM增强的强化学习智能体，融合LLM的高层规划能力与RL的低层动作优化能力，在顺序决策任务中相比纯RL或纯LLM方法实现了更高的样本效率、成功率以及更连贯的动作轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在需要精确动作优化和环境交互的长时程顺序决策任务中表现不足，而RL虽然擅长顺序控制，却缺乏复杂场景所需的高层抽象和任务分解能力。两者各自的局限性促使研究者探索混合方案。

Method: 提出一个混合架构：LLM负责生成子目标、结构化计划和情境化指导，提供高层抽象与任务分解；RL智能体通过与环境的交互来细化低层动作。两者协同工作，形成从规划到执行的完整决策链路。

Result: 在顺序决策任务上的实验表明，所提出的混合智能体在样本效率、任务成功率和动作轨迹连贯性方面均优于纯RL和纯LLM基线方法。

Conclusion: LLM规划与RL动作优化的混合范式为构建更强大的自主智能体提供了有前景的方向，展示了两类方法优势互补的可能性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool-use, enabling new forms of autonomous agents. However, LLM-based agents struggle with long-horizon sequential decision tasks that require precise action optimization and environment interaction.
