Unlike text-only agents, CUA failures arise from coupled visual perception, spatial grounding, low-level interaction, task reasoning, and environment dynamics, making debugging a distinctive multimodal causal localization problem. We introduce CUADebug, a framework for diagnosing and repairing CUA failures. CUADebug includes a CUA-specific error taxonomy, CUAErrorBench, a human-annotated OSWorld failure benchmark, and CUADebugger, a tool-augmented debugger. Instead of prompting over the full trajectory once, CUADebugger actively inspects suspicious steps with paired before/after screenshots and action traces, then submits a structured diagnosis containing the root-cause step, error type, grounded evidence, and corrective strategy for re-execution. Human annotations over 204 failed trajectories show that task reasoning and control is the largest failure family (110/204), followed by perception (36), grounding/interaction (25), external/system (13), and an others category of 20 OSWorld infeasible-task cases. On the main Claude-agent split, CUADebugger improves joint subtype-and-step diagnosis from 11.2% to 19.6% with Gemini 2.5 Pro and improves consistently across debugger backbones. In single re-execution package evaluation, RCA-based conditions achieve higher task completion than history-only continuation (28.47% with machine RCA and 29.90% with our method, versus 13.89%); in continual re-execution, our method improves success from 12.2% to 25.86%, while human-oracle guidance reaches 29.21%. These results show that CUA root-cause diagnosis can provide actionable repair signals rather than merely post-hoc explanations.

</details>


### [229] [A Unified Feature Model for Microservice Identification and Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02644)
*Ana Almeida,António Rito Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于微服务识别工具的特性模型（feature model），该模型基于对现有研究的广泛分析，用于统一描述、比较和设计微服务识别工具。通过系统映射和现有工具实例化分析，验证了该模型能捕获现有方法的主要变化点，并揭示了现有工具之间互补但碎片化的现状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微服务自动识别方法在数据收集、分析技术、分解算法、可视化和优化机制上存在多样性，但缺乏统一的通用概念框架，导致系统性的实验和跨方法比较受限。同时，研究表明不存在单一最优方法，需要整合多种方法才能充分探索设计方案的权衡。

Method: 1) 基于对现有研究的广泛分析，提出一个用于变体丰富的微服务识别工具的特性模型；2) 通过系统映射（systematic mapping）代表性文献来评估该特性模型；3) 分析该模型在现有微服务识别工具架构中的实例化情况。

Result: 两方面的发现：其一，所提出的特性模型成功捕获了现有方法的主要变化点，为分析、比较和设计微服务识别工具提供了统一基础；其二，虽然没有任何单个工具覆盖模型超过一小部分，但被分析的工具合在一起几乎覆盖了所有变化点，显示出工具间的互补性。

Conclusion: 设计空间已经被现有工具所填充，但分散在独立的工具中，没有任何单一工具能够比较或整合其他工具所实现的不同方案。这一互补性是核心结论，表明需要一种统一框架来整合碎片化的工具能力。

Abstract: Several approaches have been proposed for the automatic identification of microservices within monolithic systems. These methodologies differ in their data collection and analysis techniques, the decomposition algorithms applied, and the mechanisms used to visualize and refine candidate microservices. Despite this diversity, systematic experimentation and comparison across approaches remain limited by the absence of a common conceptual framework. Furthermore, current research indicates that no single optimal method exists; rather, integrating multiple approaches is necessary to fully explore the trade-offs inherent in any design solution.
  To address this gap, this paper proposes a feature model for variant-rich microservice identification tools, grounded in an extensive analysis of the state of the art. We evaluate this feature model through a systematic mapping of representative literature and by analyzing its instantiation within the architecture of an existing microservice identification tool. Our findings are two-fold. First, the proposed feature model successfully captures the primary variation points of existing methodologies, providing a unifying foundation for analyzing, comparing, and designing microservice identification tools. Second, while no individual tool covers more than a fraction of the model, the analyzed tools jointly span almost all of its variation points. This complementarity is the central result: the design space is already populated, but it is fragmented across standalone tools, none of which can compare or integrate the alternatives that the others implement.

</details>


### [230] [Verified Tool Calls Improve LLM Agent Reliability Under Non-Atomic Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02645)
*Isham Kalappurackal Mansoor,Abhishek Phadke,Pratip Rana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的、具备验证感知能力的工具包装器（tool wrapper），通过后置条件验证、验证-再重试逻辑和幂等键来增强LLM代理与外部工具交互的语义，解决真实世界中工具调用非原子性（超时、延迟可见性、部分状态更新）带来的可靠性问题，如重复操作和不必要的工具执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理框架假设工具调用是原子性的并返回二进制的成功/失败信号，但真实系统存在超时、延迟可见性和部分状态更新等非原子行为，导致重复操作、任务失败和不必要的工具执行等可靠性问题。

Method: 提出一个轻量级的验证感知工具包装器，包含三个核心组件：(1) 后置条件验证（postcondition verification），(2) 验证-再重试逻辑（verify-before-retry），(3) 幂等键（idempotency keys）。该方法在受控模拟环境中注入非原子故障，并在多个任务模板上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法显著减少了重复操作（duplicate actions），同时保持了可比较的任务成功率（task success rates）。

Conclusion: 增强工具交互语义是提升LLM代理可靠性的一个有前景的方向，无需修改底层语言模型即可实现。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents rely on external tools to perform multistage tasks. Existing agent frameworks typically assume that tool calls are atomic and return binary success or failure signals. However, real-world systems exhibit non-atomic behaviors such as timeouts after dispatch, delayed visibility, and partial state updates. These mismatches lead to reliability issues including duplicate actions, task success, and unnecessary tool executions. A lightweight, verification-aware tool wrapper is introduced that augments tool calls with postcondition verification, verify-before-retry logic, and idempotency keys. The approach is evaluated in a controlled simulated environment with injected non-atomic failures across multiple task templates. The results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces duplicate actions, while maintaining comparable task success rates. Overall, the findings suggest that strengthening tool interaction semantics is a promising direction for improving LLM agent reliability without requiring modifications to the underlying language model.

</details>


### [231] [Intent-Level Quantum Programming with Assertion-Guided Execution and Inspectable Intermediate Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02648)
*Ilesh Vora,Srikanth Thudumu,John Carlson,Harshil Kamdar,Rajesh Vasa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个量子领域特定语言（QDSL），通过意图-执行分离、一等IR内省和断言引导的模态推断三个机制，解决量子程序验证、可移植性和可检查性问题，并在基准电路上验证了其故障检测能力和跨框架兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序验证困难重重：电路通常以命令式门序列表达、可检查性有限；执行模态需手动选择；输出本质上是概率性的。此外，跨后端框架的移植面临内部约定差异的额外挑战，亟需一种系统性解决方案。

Method: 提出量子领域特定语言（QDSL），包含三个核心机制：(i) 意图-执行分离——算法构造（制备、叠加、纠缠、测量）以可检查对象表示，经预执行中间表示（IR）验证后才编译为后端特定电路；(ii) 一等IR内省——在执行前暴露电路宽度、导线映射、操作顺序和测量意图供开发者检查；(iii) 断言引导模态推断——引擎分析声明的验证属性，自动选择采样、态矢量或双执行模态。结果以结构化形式记录以支持可复现的回归测试。

Result: 在纠缠、基于oracle、结构化变换和变分示例等基准电路上评估原型。在Bell和3比特GHZ电路的故障注入实验中，基于总变差距离的检测器在所有评估的采样预算下识别注入故障（真阳性率1.0），仅在128次采样时观察到假阳性（假阳性率2.7-3.8%）。差分测试表明，经过端序规范化后，PennyLane和Qiskit编译目标在评估构造上数值一致。IR生成成本低于毫秒级。

Conclusion: 该QDSL通过意图-执行分离、IR内省和自动化模态推断，有效提升了量子程序的可验证性、可移植性和可调试性。故障检测的高真阳性率和跨框架的数值一致性验证了其作为量子程序开发验证基础设施的可行性，且IR生成开销极低。

Abstract: Quantum programs are difficult to validate: circuits are typically expressed as imperative gate sequences with limited inspectability, execution modalities must be selected manually, and outputs are inherently probabilistic. These challenges are compounded when programs must be portable across backend frameworks with differing internal conventions. We present a quantum domain-specific language (QDSL) that addresses these problems through three mechanisms: (i) intent-execution separation, where algorithmic constructs such as preparation, superposition, entanglement, and measurement are represented as inspectable objects compiled into backend-specific circuits only after pre-execution Intermediate Representation (IR) validation; (ii) first-class IR introspection, exposing circuit width, wire mapping, operation order, and measurement intent for developer inspection prior to execution; and (iii) assertion-guided modality inference, where the engine examines declared verification properties to automatically select sampling, statevector, or dual execution without user intervention. Results are logged in structured form to support reproducible regression testing. We evaluate our prototype on benchmark circuits spanning entanglement, oracle-based, structured-transform, and variational examples. In fault-injection experiments on Bell and 3-qubit GHZ circuits, a total-variation-distance detector identifies the injected faults across all evaluated shot budgets (True Positive Rate 1.0), with observed false positives (False Positive Rate 2.7-3.8%) only at 128 shots. Differential testing demonstrates numerical agreement across PennyLane and Qiskit compilation targets for the evaluated constructs after endianness canonicalization. IR generation costs are sub-millisecond.

</details>


### [232] [When Policies Change Probabilities: Modular Decision-Making for LLM Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02677)
*Rasvik Kudum,Max Corbett,Hitansh Paliwal,Romaisa Fatima,Thomas Jiralerspong,Sneheel Sarangi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM代码审查者是否将风险概率估计与基于代价的决策分离。通过在720个候选补丁上的15,792个响应测试发现，当代价政策从等代价变为10:1假接受时，报告的风险概率平均变化13.6-16.9个百分点，说明概率估计受到下游政策影响。模块化流程（独立评估风险+外部监控评分+代码中应用代价）相比校准审查者可提高概率准确性并在等代价下减少平均损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码审查者通常在一个提示中同时完成风险估计和审批决策，但概率应仅取决于证据，而行动应由代价决定。这种混淆可能导致概率估计被政策偏差污染，进而导致非最优决策。

Method: 使用720个候选补丁（360个仓库问题，每个包含一个通过和一个失败的测试）上的15,792个响应，对四个部署型审查者接口进行测试。在保持补丁和监控证据固定的条件下，将等代价政策替换为10:1假接受政策，比较报告概率的变化。同时构建一个模块化流水线：独立eliciting风险（不含政策信息）、融合独立监控分数、在代码层面应用代价。

Result: （1）所有四个审查者在高代价提示下返回的行动比拒绝所有补丁更差；（2）概率elicitation本身贡献了超额损失，将高代价规则应用于等代价下elicited的概率可减少所有系统的损失；（3）模块化流程相比校准的仅审查者评分提高了概率准确性，在等代价下每问题平均损失减少0.073，接受58-68%补丁；在10:1代价下不接受任何补丁并匹配拒绝所有。

Conclusion: 下游政策会改变其本应使用的概率，这支持将风险评估、外部证据和行动决策分离进行独立评估的设计原则。模块化流程体现了这种分离的优势。

Abstract: LLM code reviewers often estimate patch risk and make approval decisions in one prompt. A probability should depend on evidence; costs should determine the action taken from it. We test whether four deployed reviewer interfaces preserve this separation using 15,792 responses on 720 candidate patches, with one that passed and one that failed an archived test harness for each of 360 repository issues. In matched calls with the patch and monitor evidence fixed, replacing an equal-cost policy with a 10:1 false-accept policy changes reported failure probabilities by 13.6 to 16.9 percentage points on average. For every reviewer, the actions returned under the high-cost prompt are worse than rejecting all patches. Applying the same high-cost rule to probabilities elicited under equal costs reduces loss for all four systems, showing that probability elicitation itself contributes to the excess loss. We also evaluate a modular pipeline that elicits risk without policy information, combines an independent monitor score, and applies costs in code. Relative to calibrated reviewer-only scores, the pipeline improves average probability accuracy and, at equal costs, reduces mean loss by .073 per issue while accepting 58 to 68% of patches. At 10:1, it accepts none and matches reject-all. Downstream policy can therefore change the probability it is meant to use, motivating separate evaluation of risk, outside evidence, and action.

</details>


### [233] [AI Sandbox: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02679)
*Muhammad Waseem,Md Aidul Islam,Md Nasir Uddin Shuvo,Md Mahade Hasan,Kai-Kristian Kemell,Jussi Rasku,Mika Saari,Vilma Saari,Roope Pajasmaa,Markku Oivo,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在产学研合作背景下设计并实现的一个治理感知的多租户AI沙箱平台，该平台支持结构化实验、可重用评估证据生成，并通过参考架构分离多租户UI与后端控制平面，集成了用户注册管控、项目协作、AI服务管理、审批流程、审计日志和可追溯实验等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨行业与学术界的协作AI实验需要支持快速原型开发的同时保持受控访问、租户隔离和透明工作流。尽管AI沙箱受到越来越多的关注，但在如何设计实现集成实验能力与治理要求的平台方面仍缺乏实用指导。

Method: 基于与工业伙伴迭代细化的需求开展产学研合作，设计了治理感知的多租户AI沙箱。其参考架构将多租户用户界面与后端控制平面分离，并将执行和数据管理功能置于专用层。平台支持受治理的用户注册、以项目为中心的协作、AI服务受控访问、审批工作流、审计日志和可追溯实验。实验配置、上下文信息和治理决策以持久化记录形式存储，便于跨项目比较和复用。

Result: 成功开发并部署了治理感知的多租户AI沙箱平台，实现了实验证据的生成与跨项目复用，并在产学研协作环境中通过实践积累了部署和扩展此类平台的经验教训。

Conclusion: 该工作为在协作研究和工业环境中部署与扩展治理感知的AI沙箱平台提供了实用指导，填补了AI沙箱在治理集成设计方面缺乏实践经验的空白。

Abstract: Collaborative AI experimentation across industry and academia requires platforms that enable rapid prototyping while preserving controlled access, tenant separation, and transparent workflows. Despite growing interest in AI sandboxes, there is still limited practical guidance on how to design and implement platforms that integrate experimentation capabilities with governance requirements. This work presents the design and implementation of a governance-aware, multi-tenant AI sandbox for structured experimentation and the generation of reusable evaluation evidence across projects and stakeholder groups.
  The sandbox was developed within an industry-academia collaboration based on requirements that were iteratively refined with industrial partners. Its reference architecture separates the multi-tenant user interface from the backend control plane and places execution and data-management functions in dedicated layers. The platform supports governed user onboarding, project-centered collaboration, managed access to AI services, approval workflows, audit logging, and traceable experimentation. Experiment configurations, contextual information, and governance decisions are stored as persistent records, allowing evidence and outcomes to be compared and reused across projects.
  The development process provides practical lessons for deploying and extending governance-aware AI sandbox platforms in collaborative research and industrial environments.

</details>


### [234] [TraceCompiler: Skill-Guided Mining and Compilation of LLM Agent Traces into Mostly Deterministic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02680)
*Salma El Yadouni,Guanyi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceCompiler，一种基于技能引导的系统，可从嘈杂的智能体痕迹（traces）中挖掘聚类并编译成可执行的、近乎确定性的工作流。它仅在消费参数可唯一归因于先前生产者时建立跨工具依赖（"硬边"），并携带可审计证据；模糊关系标记为"疑似"且不施加排序约束。绑定被分类为常量、用户输入、复制输出、转换或残余LLM决策。在T1基准上，规则恢复生产者-消费者依赖的精确率为0.928、召回率为0.943；在AppWorld上，规则在确定性模拟器中重放轨迹，对563个token边达到0.993精确率。实验显示Venmo转账意图将34次API调用缩减为11次，并通过基准自身状态测试15/21；Spotify/Todoit意图因副作用不可逆且未充分确定而正确拒绝编译。作者明确声明未衡量离线编译成本，因此不主张净效率收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型语言模型智能体反复重新执行其已运行过的过程，产生混合了可复用结构与重试、探索、偶然排序和重复查询的痕迹。现有方法无法从这些嘈杂痕迹中可靠地提取可复用的确定性工作流，因此需要一种能自动将噪声痕迹编译为可执行工作流的系统，以消除冗余调用并沉淀技能。

Method: TraceCompiler采用技能引导方法：首先对噪声智能体痕迹进行聚类挖掘，然后编译为可执行的、基本确定性的工作流。核心规则是：仅当消费方的参数包含可唯一归因于先前生产者的值时，才允许跨工具依赖（硬边），每条硬边附带可审计证据元组；模糊关系被标记为"疑似"且不施加排序约束。绑定被分类为常量、用户输入、复制输出、转换或残余LLM决策五类。

Result: 在T1基准上，机械化规则在训练集15,775条def-use边上达到0.928精确率与0.943召回率，优于邻接法（0.711 F1）和频率阈值直接跟随法（0.712 F1）；技能盲运行在250条边上达0.992准确率。在AppWorld上，确定性模拟器中重放恢复掩码返回值，563条token边达0.993精确率。Venmo意图将34次API调用缩减为11次运行时调用，但在留一执行下仅通过15/21测试（失败折因所需分支从未观察而升级而非行动）；Spotify/Todoit意图被编译器正确拒绝编译。

Conclusion: TraceCompiler展示了从噪声智能体痕迹中提取可审计、确定性工作流的有效性，其依赖规则在精确率和召回率上均显著优于基线。系统能成功编译可复现意图（Venmo）并正确拒绝无法充分确定副作用的条件编译（Spotify/Todoit）。然而，作者明确承认未测量离线编译成本，因此不主张净效率收益，这是未来工作的重要方向。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents repeatedly rediscover procedures they have already executed, producing traces that mix reusable structure with retries, exploration, accidental ordering, and repeated lookups. We present TraceCompiler, a skill-guided system that mines clusters of noisy agent traces and compiles them into executable, mostly deterministic workflows. It admits an inter-tool dependency only when a consumer argument contains a value attributable uniquely to an earlier producer; every hard edge carries an auditable evidence tuple, and ambiguous relations are marked suspected and impose no ordering constraint. Bindings are classified as constants, user inputs, copied outputs, transforms, or residual LLM decisions. On T1, a mechanized form of the rule recovers producer-consumer dependencies at 0.928 precision and 0.943 recall over 15,775 def-use edges of its training split, against 0.711 F1 for adjacency and 0.712 for a frequency-thresholded directly-follows measure on identical data; the compiler skill run blind reaches 0.992 on 250 of those edges. On AppWorld we replay released trajectories in the deterministic simulator to recover masked return values and measure the rule against 563 token edges at 0.993 precision - a self-consistency check, since replay injects tokens by a related heuristic. We compile two recurring intents: a Venmo money-request intent reduces 34 observed API calls to 11 runtime calls and, under leave-one-out execution against the benchmark's own state tests, passes 15 of 21, the failing fold escalating rather than acting because its required branch was never observed; and a Spotify/Todoist intent the compiler correctly refuses to compile, because an irreversible side effect is under-determined. We measure call reduction but not offline compilation cost, so we claim no net efficiency result.

</details>


### [235] [Studying Developer Perceptions on the Potential of CI Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02682)
*Osamah H. Alaini,Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一项探索性调查研究，旨在理解GitHub开发者如何选择持续集成（CI）服务，区分"需求驱动"与"社会影响"两种动机，并探讨开发者对CI普适性认知、采用障碍及对自动化CI推荐系统的看法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已识别出CI采用的障碍，但缺乏关于开发者如何选择CI服务、以及采用决策是由项目真实需求还是社会影响驱动的实证证据。这一空白限制了CI推荐系统的发展和CI采用的优化。

Method: 采用探索性调查研究方法，计划联系约5000名活跃的GitHub开发者（包括CI用户和非用户），围绕三个核心问题开展调查：(1) CI采用与服务选择的驱动因素，区分需求驱动与社会影响动机；(2) 开发者对CI是否普遍必要或依赖具体情境的看法及采用障碍；(3) 开发者对自动化CI推荐系统的感知。

Result: 该研究尚处于调查设计阶段（探索性调查），未提供具体实证结果。预期产出包括开发者CI选择行为的分类学洞察、需求驱动与社会影响动机的区分证据，以及CI推荐系统设计的用户需求信息。

Conclusion: 研究结果将为CI推荐系统的开发提供信息支撑，并为在开源项目中简化CI采用流程的从业者提供实践指导。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) is central to modern software development, yet developers often struggle to choose the most suitable CI service. Prior work has identified barriers to CI adoption but offers little empirical evidence on how developers select CI services or whether adoption decisions are driven by genuine project needs versus social influence. This paper presents an exploratory survey study addressing that gap. We aim to contact about 5,000 active GitHub developers, including both CI users and non-users. The study investigates: (1) what drives CI adoption and service selection, distinguishing need-driven from socially influenced motivations; (2) whether developers consider CI universally necessary or context-dependent and what barriers hinder adoption; and (3) developers' perceptions of automated CI recommendation systems. Our findings will inform researchers developing CI recommendation systems and practitioners aiming to streamline CI adoption in open-source projects.

</details>


### [236] [BulkPR-Bench: Benchmarking Queue-Level Governance of Interacting Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02685)
*Zetong Xiong,Qiao Zhao,Jun Zhang,Xueying Lyu,Zhi Li,Yixiang Tu,Xiaowen Yang,Yunjie Zhang,Yufeng Wang,Zhe Zhang,Kaize Yu,Hanwen Du,Zhongkai Sun,Zhuoxin Liu,Zekun Lin,Jianwen Yang,Ruining Chen,Ying Zhang,Tingxuan Pan,Ke Chen,Shubin Han,Chuanhao Sun,Yehua Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BulkPR-Bench，一个可执行的基准测试，用于评估编码agent在处理相互关联的PR队列时，能否恢复PR之间的因果/关联关系并以可执行顺序返回安全合并子集。在18个真实仓库快照上的581个新编写的候选PR上测试，最佳模型的Relational Delivery Score仅达66.6%，而多数模型无法完整交付队列，表明现有模型在整体队列治理上仍有很大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码agent基准通常处理单一结果或固定变更序列，无法应对PR队列中交互依赖的场景。当排队的PR相互影响时，最大化安全交付需要联合决策合并哪些变更以及合并顺序，而非逐个独立处理。这需要新的基准来评估agent在滚动发布协议下处理交互PR的能力。

Method: 构建BulkPR-Bench：在18个真实仓库的冻结快照上编写581个新的候选PR，通过注册的状态逐步仓库执行（包括隐藏安全检查）验证金标准关系图，使用精确oracle计算最大安全子集。提出两种评估指标：Relational Delivery Score (RDS) 从实际合并轨迹中按关系组评分安全交付与正确拒绝；Global Safety-Gated Yield (Global-SGY) 从整体队列计划角度严格衡量交付。采用批大小K=32的缓冲主协议进行测试。

Result: 在六个模型中，前三名RDS为66.6%、62.0%和57.9%，而最强顺序基线为53.1%。324次模型运行中只有8次精确完成整个队列。关键关系召回率范围在35.2%到57.7%之间，即使提供金标准关系的诊断运行也显示仍有大量改进空间。

Conclusion: 当前模型在关系组层面的收益尚未转化为可靠的整体队列治理能力。虽然模型能在一定程度上识别并处理PR之间的关系，但在滚动发布协议下精确完成整个PR队列的能力仍然不足，说明该领域有显著的headroom需要进一步研究和改进。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks increasingly cover long-horizon, end-to-end, and interactive development, but typically retain one requested outcome or a fixed change sequence. Sequential policies can process a pull-request (PR) queue one candidate at a time, but when queued PRs interact, maximizing safe delivery can require jointly deciding which changes to merge and in what order. We introduce BulkPR-Bench, an executable benchmark in which an agent must recover consequential PR relations and return a large safe subset in executable order under a rolling-release protocol. The suite contains 581 newly authored candidate PRs on frozen snapshots of 18 real repositories. Registered state-by-state repository execution, including hidden safety checks, validates the gold relation graph; an exact oracle then computes the largest safe subset. Our primary metric, Relational Delivery Score (RDS), scores safe delivery and correct rejection over relation groups from the realized merge trace; Global Safety-Gated Yield (Global-SGY) separately measures strict delivery of the realized whole-queue plan. Under the buffered primary protocol with batch size $K=32$, the three highest RDS estimates among the six models are 66.6%, 62.0%, and 57.9%, compared with 53.1% for the strongest sequential baseline. Only 8 of 324 model runs complete a queue exactly. Critical-relation recall ranges from 35.2% to 57.7%, and diagnostic runs supplied with the gold relations show substantial remaining headroom. Gains on relation groups therefore do not yet translate into dependable whole-queue governance.

</details>


### [237] [PRWeaver: Evaluating LLM-Based Code Auditors against Long-Horizon Malicious Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02693)
*Yuekun Wang,Mingfei Cheng,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PRWeaver是一个包含208个经执行验证的攻击样本的基准测试，来自十个真实仓库，每个攻击样本在四种匹配的审查渲染下实例化（共832个渲染）。研究发现，跨PR的良性-恶意变更交织和连贯载体融合能显著降低LLM代码审计器的检测率（分别降低5-13和10-18个百分点），而提交边界分解几乎不影响检测。在整窗口审查下，检测率降至16-22%，表明仅访问仓库历史不足以抵御精心构造的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的代码审计器越来越多地被集成到PR工作流中，但它们对抗跨仓库演化分布的攻击性变更的可靠性仍然缺乏理解。现有研究未系统评估攻击者在PR审查上下文中的对抗策略，特别是通过提交分解、变更交织和载体融合等手段逃避审计的问题。

Method: 作者构建了PRWeaver基准，包含从十个真实仓库中提取的208个经过执行验证的攻击样本，每个攻击在四种匹配的审查渲染下实例化。评估了三个PR审计代理在六种审计模型系统上的性能，并系统比较了提交边界分解、跨PR变更交织（N=16）以及连贯载体融合等对抗策略对检测率的影响。

Result: 在所有系统中，提交分解对检测率的影响最多仅5个百分点，表明提交边界本身不能解释规避成功。跨PR交织（N=16）和连贯载体融合分别将检测率降低5-13和10-18个百分点。在整窗口审查（N=24）下，检测率降至16-22%，而逐PR审查下为50-60%。

Conclusion: 结果显示，仅访问仓库历史是不够的：当良性变更和恶意变更共同占据审计者的活动审查上下文，或当声明的目的看似合理地解释了携带攻击的差异时，隐藏最为有效。这揭示了LLM代码审计器在对抗性PR场景下的关键脆弱性。

Abstract: LLM-based code auditors are increasingly integrated into pull-request (PR) workflows, yet their reliability against adversarial changes distributed across repository evolution remains poorly understood. We introduce PRWeaver, a benchmark of 208 execution-validated attacks from ten real-world repositories, each instantiated under four matched review renderings (832 renderings in total). We evaluate three PR-auditing agents across six auditor-model systems. Across all systems, decomposing an attack changes detection by at most five percentage points, showing that commit boundaries alone do not explain evasion. In contrast, per-PR interleaving at $N=16$ and coherent carrier fusion reduce detection by 5-13 and 10-18 points, respectively. Under whole-window review at $N=24$, detection falls to 16-22%, compared with 50-60% under per-PR review. These results show that access to repository history is insufficient: concealment becomes most effective when benign and malicious changes jointly occupy the auditor's active review context or when the stated purpose plausibly accounts for the attack-bearing diff.

</details>


### [238] [Don't Regenerate, Debug: A Domain-Specific Agent for Repairing Near-Miss Hardware Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02712)
*Yansong Sun,Shenxiu Wu,Siyuan Chen,Runlin Hou,Junhao Qiu,Junming Cao,Shudi Shao,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向硬件加速器（GPU/NPU）内核生成的领域专用调试智能体，主张从"重新生成"转向"自动调试"：通过检索模式与诊断插桩缓解知识稀缺、通过反作弊检测与全覆盖评估确保完整性、通过收敛守卫与有界迭代控制成本。实验表明调试策略显著优于重新生成策略，且单位成功消耗的token降低92.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的内核生成系统通过强化学习与演化搜索构建pipeline，生成大量候选内核后丢弃绝大多数失败样本，未能将失败转化为可复用的知识。许多被丢弃的候选是能编译运行但未能通过数值验证的near-miss算子，其中蕴含真实的领域知识和大量的LLM推理、交叉编译及硬件执行成本。作者认为应转变范式：与其重新生成，不如调试，因为调试的搜索空间更小、反馈信号更密集。

Method: 提出一个领域专用调试智能体，解决三个核心挑战：（1）通过检索模式与诊断插桩缓解知识稀缺；（2）通过反作弊检测与全覆盖评估确保完整性；（3）通过收敛守卫与有界迭代控制成本。调试智能体在生成pipeline的失败输出上迭代修复，而非重新从头生成。

Result: Debug Pass@1达到66.7%，显著优于Regenerate Avg Pass@1的25.9%和Regenerate Pass@3的40.7%；每次成功消耗的token减少92.8%。组件消融实验显示知识库是恢复能力的主要驱动因素；完整性门控拒绝了工作流自身已接受的成功候选中12.5%-33.3%的结果。

Conclusion: 调试在内核生成中扮演两个互补角色：一方面通过恢复反复重新生成都无法产生的算子来扩展能力边界；另一方面降低每个交付算子的成本。该范式为LLM驱动的内核生成提供了一种更高效、更具知识复用性的替代路径。

Abstract: Kernel generation for hardware accelerators such as GPUs and NPUs has become a proving ground for large language models (LLMs), and state-of-the-art systems raise correctness through pipelines that couple LLMs with agentic reinforcement learning and evolutionary search. Such pipelines generate, compile, and execute large numbers of candidate kernels, discarding most of them and forgoing the opportunity to distill failures into reusable knowledge. Many discarded candidates are near-miss operators that compile and run but fail numerical validation; each embodies genuine domain knowledge and a nontrivial investment in LLM inference, cross-compilation, and hardware execution. We argue for a paradigm shift: rather than regenerate, debug. Debugging is far more constrained than generating from scratch: the search space is small and feedback is dense. We present a domain-specific debug agent that addresses three core challenges in autonomous repair: mitigating knowledge scarcity through retrieved patterns and diagnostic instrumentation, ensuring integrity through anti-cheat detection and full-coverage evaluation, and controlling cost via convergence guards and bounded iteration. Debugging serves two complementary roles: it extends the capability frontier by recovering operators that repeated regeneration fails to produce, and it lowers cost per deliverable operator. Debug Pass@1 achieves 66.7% versus Regenerate Avg Pass@1's 25.9% and Regenerate Pass@3's 40.7%, while consuming 92.8% fewer tokens per success than three-trial regeneration. Component ablations show that the knowledge base drives recovery, while integrity gates reject 12.5-33.3% of the successes the workflow itself accepted.

</details>


### [239] [HyperFL: Query-Adaptive Representation Learning for Software Fault Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02967)
*Shuai Shao,Yiming Zeng,Yu Zhao,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HyperFL是一个查询自适应的软件缺陷定位表示学习框架，通过轻量级超网络为查询编码器生成查询特定的LoRA参数，实现动态查询适配，同时在多个嵌入骨干上持续提升检索性能，在函数级MRR@10上相对提升13.3%，Hit@1相对提升16.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于检索的缺陷定位方法将所有issue报告用固定的查询表示进行编码，然而真实世界中的issue报告在长度、结构和调试信息上差异巨大，固定表示无法充分捕捉这种多样性，限制了缺陷定位的性能。

Method: 提出HyperFL框架，使用轻量级超网络（hypernetwork）为查询编码器生成查询特定的LoRA（低秩适配）参数，从而使查询编码能够根据具体issue内容动态调整，同时保持代码编码器固定可复用。

Result: 在真实世界issue定位基准上，HyperFL在多个嵌入骨干网络上持续提升检索性能，相对SweRank方法在函数级MRR@10上提升13.3%，Hit@1上提升16.7%。进一步分析表明HyperFL能够针对不同issue特征学习不同的适配模式。

Conclusion: 查询自适应的表示学习对于软件issue定位是有效的，HyperFL通过为不同issue学习差异化适配模式，显著优于固定查询表示方法，验证了查询自适应策略的优越性。

Abstract: Software fault localization identifies the code locations responsible for reported issues and is a fundamental step toward automated debugging and program repair. Recent retrieval-based approaches formulate fault localization as a dense retrieval task by learning a shared embedding space between issue reports and source code. However, these methods encode all issue reports using a fixed query representation, despite the substantial diversity of real-world issue reports in length, structure, and debugging information. To address this limitation, we propose HyperFL, a query-adaptive representation learning framework for software fault localization. HyperFL employs a lightweight hypernetwork to generate query-specific LoRA parameters for the query encoder, enabling dynamic query adaptation while keeping the code encoder fixed and reusable. Experiments on a real-world issue localization benchmark demonstrate that HyperFL consistently improves retrieval performance across multiple embedding backbones, achieving up to 13.3% relative improvement in function-level MRR@10 and 16.7% relative improvement in Hit@1 over the state-of-the-art method SweRank. Further analysis shows that HyperFL learns distinct adaptation patterns for different issue characteristics, highlighting the effectiveness of query-adaptive representations for software issue localization.

</details>


### [240] [ConFL: Explainable Concurrent Fault Localization via Hierarchy-Guided LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02974)
*Shuai Shao,Dingbang Wang,Yiming Zeng,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConFL是一个可解释的并发故障定位框架，通过结构化并发知识增强LLM推理，在真实世界并发bug上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅通过bug报告定位并发bug非常困难，因为信息不完整、程序实体提及具有误导性、跨线程交互复杂，导致现有基于LLM的方法推理不稳定且可解释性有限。

Method: ConFL从源代码构建并发知识库(CKB)，执行LLM引导的分层检索，渐进地从组件层缩小到交互层并发上下文；使用交互级DSL显式编码共享资源上的跨线程交互，避免深层调用链遍历。

Result: 在八个大型Java项目的真实并发bug上，ConFL显著优于SOTA的IR和LLM基线，MRR达0.503，MAP达0.486，且对噪声bug报告、未见bug和不同LLM骨干均保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结构化并发知识能够有效增强LLM在并发故障定位中的推理能力和可解释性，为并发bug定位提供了一种可靠且可泛化的新方法。

Abstract: Localizing concurrent bugs from bug reports alone is challenging due to incomplete information, misleading program-entity mentions, and complex cross-thread interactions, causing existing LLM-based approaches to suffer from unstable reasoning and limited explainability. We propose ConFL, an explainable concurrent fault localization framework that augments LLM reasoning with structured concurrency knowledge. ConFL constructs a Concurrent Knowledge Base (CKB) from source code and performs LLM-guided hierarchical retrieval to progressively narrow the search space from components to interaction-level concurrency contexts. An interaction-level DSL explicitly encodes cross-thread interactions over shared resources, enabling focused reasoning without traversing deep call chains. Experiments on real-world concurrent bugs from eight large-scale Java projects show that ConFL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art IR-based and LLM-based baselines, achieving an MRR of 0.503 and a MAP of 0.486, while remaining robust to noisy bug reports, unseen bugs, and different LLM backbones.

</details>


### [241] [The Ground Is Shifting: A Reflection on the Foundations of Software Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03007)
*Thomas Bock,Audris Mockus,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出软件测量领域中，从手工数据收集转向版本控制工具跟踪数据的过程中，一直隐含"数据由专业开发人员生成"的假设。随着AI代理也开始使用这些工具生成跟踪数据，该假设被频繁违反。作者提出一个系统性的AI辅助复制计划，利用现代方法重新检验关键研究成果，确保软件测量仍具意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件测量长期以来依赖手工收集数据，转而使用版本控制等工具跟踪数据后，一直假定这些工具被专业开发人员用于构建真正的软件系统。然而，AI代理的参与使得数据生成方式发生变化，破坏了这一根本假设，可能使大量以往研究结论失效，因此亟需解决。

Method: 提出一个系统的AI辅助复制程序（replication program），该程序利用现代AI技术重新审视和验证软件测量领域的关键已有发现，目标是开发在当代数据环境下仍能产生一致结果的新方法论。

Result: 尚未给出具体实验结果，本文为前瞻性观点/立场文章，提出研究方向和框架，强调需要检测数据中基础假设被违反的情况，并发展适应新环境的新方法。

Conclusion: 为了保持软件测量研究的现实意义，必须立即行动：检测当代数据中基础假设的违反情况，并通过系统性的AI辅助复制计划开发在变化环境下依然有效的新方法论。

Abstract: For most of the past six decades, software measurement relied on labor-intensive manual collection of proprietary data, which hampered progress. The shift to repurposing traces from version control and related tools dramatically expanded data availability$\unicode{x2014}$especially with the rise of open-source software$\unicode{x2014}$but hinged on an often unstated assumption: that these tools are used by professional developers to build genuine software systems. However, as trace-generating tools, data types and scale, and empirical methods have all evolved, it has become clear that changes in data generation and analytical approaches affect many prior findings about software development, maintenance, and evolution. With AI agents now actively using these same tools, the resulting traces frequently violate the original assumption of human origin. To preserve the relevance of software measurement research, immediate action is needed: We must detect when foundational assumptions are violated in contemporary data and develop new methodologies that remain valid under changed circumstances. To this end, we propose a systematic AI-assisted replication program that revisits key findings using modern techniques, aiming for methods that yield consistent results on current data to keep software measurement meaningful.

</details>


### [242] [LLM Serving in the Wild: An Empirical Study of Frameworks, Methods, and System Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03036)
*Forough Majidi,Mohammad Mehdi Morovati,Foutse Khomh,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源软件系统中LLM服务框架的采用情况进行了大规模实证研究，分析了vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、LMDeploy和FlashInfer五种框架的单独及组合采用模式，发现vLLM最受欢迎，并行计算、内存管理和网络剪枝是最常用的服务方法类别，多框架组合使用有限但能互补服务栈能力，且框架采用因模型类别、模态、规模、领域和部署环境而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已深度集成到软件系统和AI服务中，高效LLM服务成为软件工程的关键问题。尽管已有大量关于LLM推理、优化和服务技术及框架的研究，但这些技术在实践中的采用情况鲜为人知，缺乏来自开源系统的实证视角。

Method: 对开源软件系统进行大规模实证研究，识别并分析五个LLM专用服务框架（vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、LMDeploy、FlashInfer），考察框架和技术的单独及组合采用方式、不同LLM类别间的采用差异，以及仓库在意图、重点、用例和架构设计上的区别。

Result: vLLM在流行度和采用率上最具可见性；并行计算、内存管理和网络剪枝是最常用的服务方法类别；多框架使用有限，开发者通常依赖单一服务框架，但组合框架可连接服务栈的互补能力；框架采用因模型家族、模态、模型大小、领域专业化和部署设置而异；仓库级分析表明LLM服务框架支持包括RL推理、多模态生成与理解、微服务和云基础设施在内的应用与架构。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了LLM服务框架在实践中的大规模实证特征刻画，为研究人员、框架维护者和LLM系统从业者提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into software systems and AI services, making efficient LLM serving a concern for software engineering. Serving LLMs is challenging because inference requires computation, memory, GPU resources, and execution while maintaining latency and throughput. Although prior research has proposed LLM inference, optimization, and serving techniques and frameworks, little is known about how they are adopted in practice. In this study, we investigate the use of LLM serving frameworks and serving methods in open-source software systems. We identify and analyze five LLM-specific frameworks: vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, LMDeploy, and FlashInfer. We examine how these frameworks and techniques are adopted individually and in combination, how adoption varies across categories of LLMs, and how repositories differ in intent, focus, use case, and architectural design. Our results show that vLLM is the most visible framework in popularity and adoption, while parallel computation, memory management, and network pruning are the most frequently used serving-method categories. Multi-framework usage is limited, suggesting that developers rely on a single serving framework; however, combined frameworks connect complementary capabilities across the serving stack. Framework adoption varies across model families, modalities, model sizes, domain specializations, and deployment settings. Repository-level analysis shows that LLM serving frameworks support applications and architectures, including Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based reasoning, multimodal generation and understanding, microservices, and cloud infrastructure. Overall, this study provides a large-scale empirical characterization of LLM serving framework adoption in practice and offers insights for researchers, framework maintainers, and practitioners working on LLM systems.

</details>


### [243] [Integration Barriers in Open-Source SSI Frameworks: An Exploratory Developer Experience Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03039)
*Breno Cerqueira Reis Nakamura,Arlindo Flavio da Conceição*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文通过对9位开发者的探索性探测研究，调查了开源SSI工具链（Walt.id、Traction和MetaMask）的开发者体验，揭示了被动操作（如凭证接收）相对成熟而主动构建任务（尤其是模式定制）存在显著架构摩擦，并提出三项架构转变建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SSI承诺去中心化数字身份，但开放源码SSI工具链的集成复杂性和工具链不成熟阻碍了广泛采用。现有研究缺乏对开发者实际集成体验的系统性理解，需要探索早期集成者在使用开源SSI工具时遇到的具体障碍。

Method: 采用探索性探测研究方法，招募9位具备去中心化身份概念的开发者（代表早期集成者），使用Walt.id、Traction和MetaMask三种开源SSI工具完成核心凭证生命周期任务。通过开放式开发者报告进行定性主题分析，并结合任务级难度评分来识别重复出现的集成障碍。

Result: 研究发现存在关键抽象鸿沟：被动操作（如凭证接收）相对成熟，但主动构建任务（尤其是模式自定义）暴露出显著的架构摩擦。障碍源于不充分的API抽象、脆弱的環境配置、以及无法跟上生态系统快速演进的技术文档。这些问题是当前框架中的结构性设计决策所致。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了当前SSI开源生态系统的结构性集成障碍，并提出三项开发者工具链的架构转变方向：基于Web的沙盒环境、AI辅助的模式生成器、以及可执行文档策略，以弥合抽象鸿沟并促进行业广泛采用。

Abstract: Self-Sovereign Identity (SSI) promises to decentralize digital identity, but widespread adoption remains hindered by integration complexity and tooling immaturity. This paper investigates the Developer Experience (DX) of open-source SSI tooling through an exploratory probe study. Nine developers with prior knowledge of decentralized identity concepts, representing early integrators building SSI applications, attempted core credential lifecycle tasks using Walt.id, Traction, and MetaMask. Our goal was to surface recurring integration barriers through qualitative thematic analysis of open-ended developer reports, complemented by task-level difficulty ratings. Our findings reveal a critical abstraction gap: while passive operations like credential receipt are relatively mature, active construction tasks, particularly schema customization, expose significant architectural friction. We identify that these barriers stem from inadequate API abstractions, brittle environment configurations, and documentation that fails to track the ecosystem's rapid evolution. These issues reflect structural design decisions in current frameworks. This study characterizes the structural integration barriers in the current SSI open-source ecosystem. To address the identified gaps, we propose three architectural shifts for developer tooling: web-based sandboxes, AI-assisted schema generators, and executable documentation strategies.

</details>


### [244] [Efficient Grammar-Constrained Decoding via Parser Stack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03065)
*Yongmin Li,Yihong Dong,Jia Li,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了PSC，一种新的文法约束解码方法，通过将词汇表中所有token的接受条件合并为解析器栈上的单一分类器，使得每次解码步骤只需检查解析器栈一次即可计算完整词汇掩码，时间复杂度与词汇量大小无关，掩码计算速度比基线快高达700倍，端到端LLM吞吐量接近无约束解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文法约束解码（GCD）方法在线计算开销大，延迟通常随词汇量大小线性增长，严重限制了大型词表模型（如代码生成、JSON输出场景）的LLM吞吐量。

Method: PSC在预处理阶段将所有词汇token的接受条件合并为解析器栈上的单一分类器；在解码阶段，每次只需检查解析器栈一次即可得到完整的词汇掩码，时间复杂度与词汇量无关。论文还提供了预处理开销分析和盈亏平衡点分析，以帮助用户决定是否自行进行预处理。

Result: 在复杂编程语言文法上掩码计算比基线快高达700倍，在符合schema的JSON上快30倍；端到端LLM吞吐量接近无约束解码水平。

Conclusion: PSC通过消除词汇量对掩码计算时间的影响，大幅提升了文法约束解码的效率，使GCD不再成为LLM吞吐量的瓶颈，并提供了实用的预处理决策指导。

Abstract: LLMs are widely used to generate structured output like source code or JSON. Grammar-constrained decoding (GCD) can guarantee the syntactic validity of the generated output, by masking out tokens that violate rules specified by a context-free grammar. However, the online computational overhead of existing GCD methods, with latency typically scaling linearly with vocabulary size, limits the throughput of LLMs, especially for models with large vocabularies. To address this issue, we propose PSC, a novel grammar-constrained decoding method. By combining acceptance conditions of all vocabulary tokens into a single classifier of the parser stack during preprocessing, PSC can compute the complete vocabulary mask by checking the parser stack exactly once per decoding step, with time complexity independent of the vocabulary size. Experiments show that PSC computes masks up to 700$\times$ faster than baselines on complex programming language grammars, and up to 30$\times$ faster for schema-conformant JSON; end-to-end LLM throughput with PSC approaches that of unconstrained decoding. We analyze the preprocessing overhead for preprocessing providers and decoding users, and provide a break-even point analysis to help users decide whether to do preprocessing by themselves.

</details>


### [245] [Cross-Ecosystem Bug Classification in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03173)
*Mir Mohammad Yousuf,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Bisma Majid*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对12,910个Qiskit问题及11个其他量子软件仓库的4,613个问题进行了跨生态系统的比较研究，发现经典软件缺陷占主导（67%），量子特定缺陷占27-30%；基于规则的分类框架在缺陷类型层面表现优于四个监督式机器学习基线，并揭示了生态系统的特定趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件工程面临经典与量子组件交互带来的复杂且难以理解的缺陷模式，但目前缺乏对量子软件缺陷的系统性跨生态比较研究，这阻碍了测试、调试和质量保证工作的推进。

Method: 采用基于规则的分类框架，对来自Qiskit（12,910个问题）和其他11个量子仓库（4,613个问题，含Cirq和PyQuil）的缺陷按类型、类别、严重程度、质量属性和量子特定子类型进行分析；通过与四个监督式机器学习基线进行基准比较，并进行2017-2025年纵向分析。

Result: 经典缺陷在所有生态系统中占67%，量子特定缺陷占27-30%；Qiskit呈现更多兼容性缺陷，其他生态系统语法和量子特定缺陷率更高；门和电路问题主导量子特定缺陷；基于规则的方法在缺陷类型层面优于机器学习基线，尤其对细粒度量子子类型；纵向分析显示量子特定缺陷随时间保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了量子软件缺陷分布的首次跨生态系统比较，验证了可解释、可自动化的规则框架在指导测试、调试和质量保证方面的实用性。

Abstract: Quantum software engineering faces unique challenges due to the interaction of classical and quantum components, which produce complex and often poorly understood bug patterns. Characterizing these bugs is essential for advancing testing, debugging, and quality assurance in quantum ecosystems. This paper presents a comparative study of 12,910 issues from Qiskit and 4,613 issues from 11 additional repositories, including Cirq and PyQuil. Using a rule-based classification framework, we analyze bugs by type, category, severity, quality attributes, and quantum-specific subtypes. Results show that classical bugs consistently dominate (67%) across ecosystems, while quantum-specific bugs account for 27-30%. Ecosystem-specific trends emerge: Qiskit repositories exhibit more compatibility related bugs, whereas other ecosystems show higher syntax and quantum-specific bug rates. Across both ecosystems, gate and circuit issues dominate quantum-specific bugs, though non-Qiskit projects reveal broader diversity, including algorithmic, resource, and hybrid-interface issues. Statistical validation confirms that the framework generalizes at the bug-type level while detecting significant variations at finer levels. Benchmarking against four supervised machine-learning baselines further shows that the rule-based framework consistently outperforms data-driven models, particularly for fine-grained quantum-specific subtypes, while longitudinal analysis (2017-2025) indicates that quantum- specific bugs remain relatively stable over time rather than exhibiting a steady increase. This study provides the first cross- ecosystem comparison of bug distributions in quantum software, demonstrating the utility of an interpretable, automation-ready, rule-based framework for guiding testing, debugging, and quality assurance.

</details>


### [246] [Fail-Fast, Restart-Smart: Early Failure Prediction and Restart for SWE Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03222)
*Chenyu Wang,Yunbo Lyu,Junda He,Zhou Yang,Chenxing Zhong,Yaniv Harel,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了FailFast-RestartSmart两阶段控制器：一个0.6B的轻量级fail-to-pass监控器在轨迹前缀即可预测失败，警报后以同策略重新启动轨迹，并可选叠加中断时的仓库diff。在SWE-bench Verified上节省14.6%-20.4%执行token，并在25%假阳性率下将Qwen3.6-27B的解决率从66.6%提升至71.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE代理解决仓库级问题时会积累越来越长的上下文，导致执行成本不断增加。失败的轨迹往往更长且包含冗余探索或循环，说明部分失败在完成前就可被检测。然而过早终止可能打断本可成功的轨迹，而失败的轨迹中也可能包含有用的仓库编辑，因此需要一种既能提前终止又不会损失有用信息的控制策略。

Method: 提出两阶段控制器FailFast-RestartSmart。FailFast是一个仅0.6B参数的轻量级监控器，使用终端和密集的fail-to-pass监督信号训练，仅从可观察的轨迹前缀预测失败，无需策略logits或隐藏状态。一旦发出警报，RestartSmart启动一个无先前提示历史的全新同策略rollout，并将中断时的仓库diff作为可选叠加，由代理自行决定检查、应用或丢弃。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，仅用Qwen3.6-27B轨迹训练的监控器可迁移到其他三种策略（含闭源API模型），在5%假阳性率下节省14.6%-20.4%执行token；在Qwen3.6-27B上20.4%的节省超过per-step AgentStop适配的12.5%。在25%假阳性率下，RestartSmart将Qwen3.6-27B的解决率从66.6%提升到71.8%，而冷重启仅有66.8%。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持"提前终止+同策略顺序恢复"这一范式：轻量级监控器可跨策略泛化预测失败，同策略重启结合中断diff叠加既能节省成本又能提升最终解决率，是一种高效的代理控制方案。

Abstract: Software engineering (SWE) agents resolve repository-level issues through long trajectories that grow increasingly expensive as context accumulates. Failed runs tend to be longer and exhibit redundant exploration or looping, suggesting that some failures may be detectable before completion. Early termination, however, risks interrupting trajectories that would otherwise succeed; conversely, an unsuccessful trajectory may still contain useful repository edits. We present FailFast-RestartSmart, a two-stage controller for a single active trajectory. FailFast is a lightweight 0.6B monitor trained with terminal and dense fail-to-pass supervision to predict failure from observable prefixes without policy logits or hidden states. Upon an alarm, RestartSmart launches a fresh same-policy rollout without prior prompt history and offers the interrupted repository diff as an optional overlay that the agent may inspect, apply, or discard. On SWE-bench Verified, a monitor trained solely on Qwen3.6-27B trajectories transfers to three other policies, including a closed-API model, and saves 14.6%-20.4% of execution tokens at a target 5% false-positive rate; on Qwen3.6-27B, its 20.4% saving exceeds the 12.5% achieved by our per-step AgentStop adaptation. At a target 25% false-positive rate, RestartSmart raises Qwen3.6-27B resolution from 66.6% to 71.8%, whereas cold restart reaches only 66.8%. Together, these results support early termination with sequential same-policy recovery.

</details>


### [247] [NotDec: WebAssembly Decompilation With Inter-Procedural Type Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03286)
*Jikai Wang,Ningyu He,Tianming Liu,Junhai Wang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NotDec是一个先进的WebAssembly反编译框架，通过扩展类型检查算法、结合Retypd跨过程类型恢复算法和Memory SSA结构化控制流分析，实现了高可读性、语义一致的C代码生成，在重构成功率和类型恢复精度上大幅超越Ghidra等基线工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly已广泛部署于浏览器、容器、IoT设备和Serverless平台，成为通用底层字节码标准，但现有反编译工具存在输出冗长、可读性差、类型恢复能力有限的问题，难以满足安全审计需求。

Method: NotDec包含三项核心技术：(1) 扩展WebAssembly类型检查算法，将字节码提升为SSA-based IR；(2) 应用跨过程类型恢复算法Retypd，并结合指针与数值区分方法恢复复杂数据结构；(3) 利用Memory SSA配合语义保持的结构化控制流分析，生成可读且语义一致的C代码。

Result: NotDec在全部5,241个Juliet样本和所有Howard数据集程序上实现100%重构成功率，远超Ghidra的45.95%；在真实世界程序的结构体成员访问类型恢复精度上达到85.33%，远超Ghidra的9.24%。完整跨过程版本在大二进制上存在可扩展性挑战，但过程内变体NotDec_F表现出卓越效率，内存消耗不到Ghidra的一半，在未优化二进制上执行时间节省最高达97%。

Conclusion: NotDec在反编译正确性、类型恢复精度和效率方面全面超越现有工具，为WebAssembly安全审计提供了可靠的反编译方案，同时揭示了跨过程分析在大规模二进制上仍需优化改进的方向。

Abstract: With WebAssembly widely supported in browsers, containers, IoT devices, and serverless platforms and increasingly adopted as a universal low-level bytecode standard, auditing its hidden vulnerabilities and malicious intentions has become critical. Decompiling existing WebAssembly modules can help security researchers and end users understand binary behavior, but current tools suffer from verbose result, poor readability, and limited type recovery.
  We present NotDec, an advanced WebAssembly decompilation framework. NotDec extends the WebAssembly type checking algorithm to lift bytecode into an SSA-based IR, applies the inter-procedural type recovery algorithm Retypd with pointer and numeric value differentiation methods to recover complex data structures, and leverages Memory SSA alongside semantics-preserving structured control-flow analysis to emit readable, semantically consistent C code.
  NotDec achieves 100% recompilation success rate on all 5,241 Juliet samples and all Howard dataset programs, significantly outperforming baselines including Ghidra (45.95% success rate). On type recovery accuracy, NotDec recovers 85.33% of struct member accesses in real-world programs, vastly exceeding Ghidra's 9.24%. While the full inter-procedural version faces scalability challenges on large binaries, the intra-procedural variant NotDec_F demonstrates superior efficiency, consuming less than half of Ghidra's memory and up to 97% less execution time on unoptimized binaries.

</details>


### [248] [Evaluating LLM Trade-offs for Enterprise Automation: Lessons from Workflow Generation in a Production Enterprise Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03311)
*Xavier Wrenn,Radoslav Raykov,Aleksandar Angelov,Hirokuni Kitahara,Yuji Watanabe,Anca Sailer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种大语言模型在企业平台中AI驱动工作流生成的性能，从单体生成管线（结构成功率31.5-82.8%）改进为分段式生成管线（74.1-97.8%），使较小模型达到生产可用水平，并分析了成本、延迟与模型选择的生产权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业合规管理需要快速适应不断演变的监管框架和紧迫的修复SLA。传统的静态编排器在混合云环境中常常失效，因为事件驱动的评估要求自动化代码在数秒内适应运行时上下文。需要探索AI驱动的动态工作流生成方案。

Method: 在真实企业生产平台上评估六种大语言模型，基准涵盖29个真实IT自动化场景、两种生成管线架构（单体和分段式）以及每个prompt-model-pipeline配置8次独立运行（共2,784次运行）。分段式管线将工作流构建分解为变量搭建、基础块组装和嵌套块生成三个步骤。

Result: 初始单体管线结构成功率为31.5-82.8%；分段式管线将结构成功率提升至74.1-97.8%。mistral-small在分段式管线中达到95.7%结构成功率且每条工作流成本仅USD 0.01，达到生产可用性。mistral-medium-2505和gpt-oss-120b达到最高结构成功率（96.1%和97.8%），但前者比mistral-small有19倍成本溢价。成本范围为每条工作流USD 0.008-0.20，交互使用延迟低于50秒。

Conclusion: 分段式分解使较小模型达到生产可用水平，消除了对昂贵的顶级模型的依赖。部署经验强调需要将结构有效性与语义正确性（对用户意图的逻辑满足）相分离，并为云工程中模型无关、可扩展的自动化提供解决方案。

Abstract: Enterprise compliance management requires rapid adaptation to evolving regulatory frameworks (e.g., DORA, AI RMF, FedRAMP) and tight remediation SLAs. Traditional static orchestrators often fail in hybrid cloud environments where event-driven assessments demand that automation code adapt to runtime context in seconds. This paper presents lessons learned from evaluating six large language models for AI-driven workflow generation in a production enterprise platform, benchmarked across 29 real-world IT automation scenarios, two generation pipeline architectures, and eight independent runs per prompt-model-pipeline configuration (2,784 runs total).
  Our initial pipeline used monolithic workflow generation, achieving 31.5-82.8% structural success rates (JSON schema validity and correct UI rendering), with most models struggling on complex JSON generation. We developed a redesigned piecewise pipeline that decomposes workflow construction into variable scaffolding, base block assembly, and nested block generation, raising structural success to 74.1-97.8% across all models.
  We analyze production tradeoffs including cost (USD 0.008-0.20 per workflow), latency (under 50s for interactive use), and model selection. Piecewise decomposition enables smaller models (e.g., mistral-small at 95.7% structural success and USD 0.01 per workflow) to reach production viability, removing dependency on expensive frontier models. While mistral-medium-2505 and gpt-oss-120b achieved the highest structural success (96.1% and 97.8%), mistral-medium-2505 carries a 19x cost premium versus mistral-small. Our deployment lessons highlight the need to separate structural validity from semantic correctness (logical fulfillment of user intent) and provide a solution for model-agnostic, scalable automation in cloud engineering.

</details>


### [249] [Making AI Visible, Not Vanished: How AI Policies Reshape Developer Experience on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03329)
*Yunqi Chen,Thomas Zimmermann,Bianca Trinkenreich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对开源软件（OSS）中的AI治理政策进行大规模实证研究，基于29,624个GitHub仓库识别出385个采用AI政策的项目，提出TRACE框架（涵盖透明度、责任、归属、约束、执行五个维度），将政策分为五种治理类型，并使用倾向性评分匹配和纵向双重差分分析评估其影响。研究发现AI治理主要起规范而非禁止作用，政策采纳带来维护者参与度提升、AI使用披露增加、评审互动更丰富、代码质量改善，且强调透明度和责任的政策比单纯的限制性政策效果更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正迅速改变开源软件开发的格局，许多项目开始引入AI辅助贡献的治理政策，但学术界对这些政策的差异及其对开发者体验的影响知之甚少，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 对29,624个GitHub仓库进行大规模分析，识别出385个采纳AI政策的项目；提出TRACE框架（透明度、责任、归属、约束、执行五个维度）对政策进行分类；采用倾向性评分匹配和纵向双重差分（difference-in-differences）方法估计政策效果。

Result: AI治理主要规范而非禁止AI辅助开发；政策采纳带来了维护者参与度提升、AI使用披露增加、更丰富的评审互动和更好的代码质量，同时AI辅助贡献持续增长；强调透明度和责任的治理策略比单纯限制性方法产生更强的社区和质量效果。

Conclusion: 不同的AI治理策略会显著影响开发者体验，研究结果为开源社区设计有效的AI政策提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Generative AI is rapidly reshaping Open Source Software (OSS) software development,prompting projects to introduce policies governing AI-assisted contributions. However, little is known about how these policies differ or whether they influence developer experience. We present the first large-scale empirical study of AI governance policies in OSS. Analyzing 29,624 GitHub repositories, we identify 385 projects that adopted AI policies and derive TRACE, a framework capturing five governance dimensions: Transparency, Responsibility, Attribution, Constraints, and Enforcement. We further classify policies into five governance families and estimate their effects using propensity-score matching and longitudinal difference-in-differences analysis. Our results show that AI governance primarily regulates rather than prohibits AI-assisted development. Policy adoption brings maintainer engagement, increased AI disclosure, richer review interactions, and improved code quality while AI-assisted contributions continue to grow. Governance design matters: policies emphasizing transparency and responsibility produced stronger community and quality outcomes than restrictive approaches alone. Our findings show how different AI governance strategies shape developer experience and provide evidence to help OSS communities design effective AI policies.

</details>


### [250] [Assessing Behavioral Validation in UI Component Test Suites Using Inferred Metamorphic Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03337)
*Yu Pei,Cunming Zhang,Jeongju Sohn,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于蜕变关系（MR）的框架，将推断的MR作为经验行为参考而非完整规范，用于评估UI组件测试套件。框架从组件源码、文档和测试中推断组件特定MR，通过混合确定性与语义分析将测试与MR对齐，并计算关系级MR覆盖率。评估显示现有测试套件在三个LLM配置下MR覆盖率仅为42.5%-47.6%，且低于MR Touch；大多数未覆盖关系属于弱断言测试用例。MR覆盖与基于执行的覆盖率互补，能揭示语句/分支覆盖反映不出的行为缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的UI组件库评估依赖语句覆盖率和分支覆盖率等执行型指标，但这些指标无法反映测试是否验证了组件API和文档所隐含的行为关系，缺乏对测试行为验证充分性的度量视角。

Method: 提出基于MR的评估框架：(1) 利用UI特定分类体系从组件源码、文档和测试中推断组件特定蜕变关系；(2) 通过混合确定性分析和语义分析将测试与推断的关系对齐；(3) 计算关系级MR覆盖指标（MR Cover和MR Touch）。

Result: 三个LLM配置下MR Cover介于42.5%-47.6%，显著低于MR Touch；多数未覆盖关系为弱断言用例（行为被执行但缺少显式行为验证）。MR覆盖与执行覆盖率互补，能揭示语句/分支覆盖率未反映的行为缺口。大多数问题描述可映射到推断的MR关系类型；弱断言关系常暴露缺失的验证证据；MR标签在MR相关故障检测中呈现趋势性表现。

Conclusion: MR覆盖率提供了互补的关系级视角，用于评估现代UI组件测试中的行为验证充分性，弥补了传统执行型指标的不足。

Abstract: UI component libraries are commonly assessed using execution-based metrics such as statement and branch coverage, yet these metrics provide limited insight into whether tests verify the behavioral relations implied by component APIs and documentation. This paper presents an MR-based framework that uses inferred metamorphic relations (MRs) as an empirical behavioral reference, rather than a complete specification, for assessing UI component test suites. Given a component's source, documentation, and tests, the framework infers component-specific MRs using a UI-specific taxonomy, aligns tests with the inferred relations through hybrid deterministic and semantic analysis, and computes relation-level MR coverage metrics. We manually validate both the inferred MR space and the test--MR alignment. Our evaluation shows that existing test suites exercise substantially more behavioral relations than they explicitly validate: MR Cover remains between 42.5% and 47.6% across three LLM configurations and consistently below MR Touch. Most uncovered relations are weak-oracle cases, where behaviors are exercised but lack explicit behavioral validation. MR coverage also complements execution-based coverage by revealing behavioral gaps not reflected by statement or branch coverage alone. We further assess practical relevance through issue-description mapping, oracle strengthening, and MR-relevant injected faults. Most reported issue descriptions can be mapped to inferred MR relation types; weak-oracle relations often expose missing validation evidence; and MR labels show a trend in MR-relevant fault detection. Overall, MR coverage provides a complementary relation-level perspective for assessing behavioral validation in modern UI component testing.

</details>


### [251] [Route-Align-Verify for Functional Correctness in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03341)
*Erxue Zhou,Jingxiang Meng,Aofan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAV框架，通过Route（任务感知的提示路由）、Align（对齐的LoRA适配）和Verify（基于执行的候选选择）三个协同阶段，在不修改骨干模型架构的前提下显著提升代码生成的功能正确性，在MBPP基准上达到0.8911（Sanitized）和0.8520（Full）的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型虽在代码生成上取得显著进展，但面对异构编程任务时，单一提示策略和单次直接生成的输出往往不足以实现强大的功能正确性，需要更系统化的策略来优化提示、适配和输出选择。

Method: RAV框架由三个阶段组成：1) Route阶段——在生成前应用任务感知的提示路由；2) Align阶段——通过对齐的LoRA适配来减少微调提示与推理提示之间的不匹配；3) Verify阶段——通过针对可见公共测试用例执行多个候选方案来筛选最终输出。

Result: 在MBPP Sanitized上达到0.8911，在MBPP Full上达到0.8520，相比基座模型分别提升6.35和9.92个百分点；消融实验表明任务感知路由和对齐适配在与基于执行的验证相结合时效果显著增强；鲁棒性与污染分析支持了改进的可靠性。

Conclusion: 代码生成的功能正确性可以通过联合优化任务提示方式、模型适配方式和最终输出选择方式得到有意义的提升，而无需修改骨干模型架构。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have substantially improved code generation, yet achieving strong functional correctness remains difficult, especially for heterogeneous programming tasks where a single prompting strategy and a single directly generated output are often insufficient. In this paper, we present RAV, a lightweight and modular framework that improves code generation with a fixed backbone model through three coordinated stages: Route, which applies task-aware prompt routing before generation; Align, which reduces the mismatch between fine-tuning prompts and inference-time prompts through aligned LoRA adaptation; and Verify, which selects the final output by executing multiple candidates against visible public tests.
  We evaluate RAV on the MBPP benchmark under both the sanitized and full settings. The complete RAV pipeline achieves the best performance among all evaluated configurations, reaching 0.8911 on MBPP Sanitized and 0.8520 on MBPP Full. Compared with the base model, these results represent improvements of 6.35 and 9.92 percentage points, respectively. Component-wise ablation experiments further show that task-aware routing and aligned adaptation become substantially more effective when combined with execution-based verification. Additional robustness and contamination analyses support the reliability of the observed improvements. Overall, the results indicate that functional correctness in code generation can be meaningfully improved without modifying the backbone architecture, by jointly optimizing how tasks are prompted, how the model is adapted, and how final outputs are selected.

</details>


### [252] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03392)
*Hao Zhou,Haichuan Hu,Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了自进化编码代理（self-evolving coding agents）这一新兴领域，定义其概念、提出面向对象的分类体系，并从"何时进化"和"何种软件证据驱动进化"两个正交视角进行补充分析，最后讨论了该领域面临的挑战（如反馈可靠性、基准过拟合、安全性、可维护性、成本与泛化），为设计更自适应、可靠且具备软件意识的代理系统奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有编码代理在部署后保持静态，但软件开发本身是一个动态、反馈密集的过程（仓库不断演化、依赖变化、测试失败、修复尝试积累可复用经验）。这种矛盾推动了对自进化编码代理的研究，但该领域缺乏系统的概念界定和框架梳理。

Method: 作者首先对自进化编码代理进行定义，并将其与常规编码代理及通用自进化代理区分；随后构建了一个以对象为中心的（object-centered）分类法来刻画系统中"什么在进化"，并用两个正交视角补充：进化的发生时机（when）以及驱动进化的软件特定证据（what evidence）。最后收集并整理了该领域的相关文献。

Result: 研究发现可执行反馈、仓库级上下文和编码轨迹使软件工程成为代理自进化的天然领域，但同时也引入了反馈可靠性、基准过拟合、安全性、可维护性、成本和泛化等新挑战。作者通过围绕这些维度组织现有工作，厘清了自进化编码代理的概念边界。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在澄清自进化编码代理的概念边界，为设计更自适应、可靠且具备软件意识的智能体系统提供基础，并公开了相关文献资源库。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly embedded in software engineering workflows as coding agents that can inspect repositories, invoke tools, execute tests, debug failures, and generate patches. Yet most existing agents remain largely static after deployment, even though software development is a dynamic, feedback-rich process in which repositories evolve, dependencies change, tests fail, and repair attempts leave reusable experience. This tension has motivated a growing body of work on self-evolving coding agents, where the agent improves its future behavior by updating its framework, memory, skills, tools, models, or collaboration structures from prior coding interactions. In this survey, we provide a systematic synthesis of this emerging area. We first define self-evolving coding agents and distinguish them from conventional coding agents and general self-evolving agents. We then develop an object-centered taxonomy that characterizes what evolves in these systems, and complement it with two orthogonal perspectives: when evolution occurs and what software-specific evidence drives it. Across the literature, we find that executable feedback, repository-level context, and coding trajectories give software engineering a distinctive role as a natural domain for agent self-evolution, but also introduce new challenges in feedback reliability, benchmark overfitting, safety, maintainability, cost, and generalization. By organizing existing work around these dimensions, this survey aims to clarify the conceptual boundaries of self-evolving coding agents and provide a foundation for designing more adaptive, reliable, and software-aware agentic systems. The papers we collect can be found at https://github.com/zhouhao1024/Awesome-Self-Evolving-Coding-Agents.

</details>


### [253] [When AI Joins the Team! A Model of How AI Adoption Relates To Social Patterns in Software Engineering Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03462)
*Giusy Annunziata,Rudrajit Choudhuri,Anita Sarma,Gemma Catolino,Filomena Ferrucci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于Transactive Memory Systems（TMS）理论，通过对152名使用AI工具软件专业人员的调查数据和多组PLS-SEM模型分析，发现AI采用与社区异味（community smells）之间的关系并非单一方向，而是取决于协作类型：在专业化工作中，AI通过促进知识共享同伴互动间接减少异味；在协调工作中，AI直接提升沟通质量，补充而非替代人际互动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发工具的日益普及正在改变软件团队的协作、知识共享与协调方式，但其对团队社会动态（特别是社区异味，即反映协调与沟通故障的社会技术反模式）的影响尚未得到充分探索。现有研究缺乏关于AI采用是增加还是减少社区异味以及其作用机制的实证证据。

Method: 以TMS理论为理论基础，验证了人机（Human-AI）和人人（Human-Human）交互在Specialization和Coordination两个TMS维度上的测量工具；基于文献构建社区异味构念，并通过专家调查和因子分析进行验证；在152名使用AI工具的软件专业人员调查数据上测试了五个PLS-SEM结构方程模型。

Result: AI采用与社区异味的关系并非单一方向，而是通过取决于工作类型的机制起作用：在专业化工作中，AI与更高的知识共享同伴互动相关，进而与更少的社区异味相关；在协调工作中，AI直接与更高的沟通质量相关，补充而非取代人际互动。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个经实证验证的、基于TMS理论的模型，表明AI与社区异味之间的关系取决于协作类型，并提供了一套可复用的测量工具及对研究与实践的循证启示。

Abstract: Context: The growing adoption of AI-assisted development tools is changing how software teams collaborate, share knowledge, and coordinate, yet its consequences for team social dynamics remain largely unexplored. Gap: It is unclear whether AI adoption is associated with an increase or reduction in community smells,socio-technical anti-patterns reflecting coordination and communication breakdowns,and through which mechanisms. Method: Grounded in Transactive Memory Systems (TMS) theory, we validate instruments for HumanAI and HumanHuman interaction along two TMS dimensions, Specialization and Coordination, and test five PLS-SEM models on survey data from 152 software professionals using AI tools. Community smell constructs were derived from the literature and validated through expert surveys and factor analysis. Results: AI adoption relates to community smells not in a single way, but through mechanisms depending on the work. In specialization work, AI is associated with higher knowledge-sharing peer interaction, which is in turn associated with fewer smells. In coordination work, AI is directly associated with higher communication quality, complementing rather than replacing human interaction. Contributions: We provide an empirically validated, TMS-grounded model showing that the AIcommunity-smell relationship is contingent on the type of collaboration, with a reusable instrument and evidence-based implications for research and practice.

</details>


### [254] [Leveraging System-Level Observations to Inform Bayesian Learning of Model Parameters for Quantitative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03489)
*Simos Gerasimou,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EPIK方法，通过先验知识获取与嵌入，结合贝叶斯学习和定量验证来分析软件系统的关键定量属性（如可靠性、响应时间），利用系统级可观测属性推导未知转移参数的分布，从而提高验证结果的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在贝叶斯学习与定量验证相结合的过程中，先验知识（PK）的准确性和鲁棒性直接影响验证结果。不准确或信息量不足的先验知识会导致错误的验证结果，而现有方法要求先验知识直接作用于形式化模型的转移参数，这在实际中难以获得。

Method: EPIK方法利用系统级可观测属性（与真实世界语义相关联）来获取先验知识，通过构建双重优化问题来推导未知转移参数的分布，然后将这些分布嵌入到定量验证中，用于验证新的或难以测量的属性。与现有方法不同，EPIK不要求先验知识直接提供给形式化模型的转移参数。

Result: 在多个真实世界案例研究的变体和多种EPIK实例化上进行了详细的实验评估，结果表明EPIK在有效性、灵活性和通用性方面均表现良好。

Conclusion: EPIK成功解决了先验知识在定量验证中难以准确获取和嵌入的问题，通过系统级可观测属性实现了更可靠的定量分析，为贝叶斯学习与定量验证的结合提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Combining Bayesian learning and quantitative verification is a powerful toolset for analysing key quantitative properties of software systems, like reliability and response time. However, the accuracy and robustness of verification results strongly depend on the prior knowledge (PK) underlying Bayesian inference. This knowledge reflects original beliefs about the probability of events and typically depends on domain expertise. Using inaccurate or uninformative PK can negatively affect quantitative analysis, yielding incorrect verification results. Our EPIK approach tackles this important challenge by eliciting and embedding PK in quantitative verification equipped with Bayesian estimators. Unlike existing approaches that require PK on formal model transition parameters, EPIK leverages system-level properties that are directly observable and are linked to real-world semantics. EPIK formulates a twofold optimisation problem to derive the distributions of unknown transition parameters and then embeds these distributions to verify new or difficult-to-measure (elusive) properties. The detailed experimental evaluation using multiple variants of real-world case studies and diverse EPIK instantiations shows its effectiveness, flexibility and generality.

</details>


### [255] [CodeAssay: A Multi-Metric Benchmark with Audited Ground Truth for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03535)
*Shahbaz Siddeeq,Muhammad Waseem,Umar Subhan Malhi,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeAssay是一个面向LLM代码生成评估的"分类法优先"基准，包含185个Python任务和10个软件工程类别。该基准通过审计的ground truth、分层测试（公开/隐藏/变异测试）、代码属性度量等多维度手段，发现审计修正了9.0%的正确性标签，并将模型间差距从11.9扩大到23.7个百分点，表明可靠性评估需要经过验证的ground truth、受保护的测试和多种可解释的度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成评估依赖基于测试的基准，但这类评估的有效性取决于参考答案和测试的可靠性；同时，基于测试的正确性只捕获了生成代码可观测属性的一部分。作者希望解决评估中ground truth不可靠、测试可能泄露、单一正确性指标不充分等问题。

Method: CodeAssay采用分类法优先的方法构建基准：(1) 185个Python任务覆盖10个软件工程类别；(2) 审计的ground truth；(3) 公开测试用于生成和修复，隐藏测试用于评分；(4) 基于变异的测试套件验证；(5) 选定的代码属性度量。实验涉及7个专有LLM和14个模型-提示配置。

Result: 审计后修正了1890个正确性标签中的170个（9.0%），模型间最好到最差差距从11.9%扩大到23.7%。完整和隐藏测试套件的变异得分为82.6%和74.8%。7个专有LLM的标准提示正确率范围为77.3%-98.9%，21个模型对中有12对差异显著。在120个所有配置都解决的任务中，没有模型在所有代码属性上表现最佳。安全聚焦提示没有显著改变正确性，也未一致减少静态分析发现，反而增加了所有模型的程序长度和圈复杂度。

Conclusion: 可靠评估LLM生成的代码需要经过验证的ground truth、受保护的测试以及多种明确解释的度量指标。CodeAssay为AI辅助软件开发中基于证据的模型评估提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly evaluated for code generation using test-based benchmarks. The validity of such evaluations depends on the reliability of their references and tests, while test-based correctness captures only part of the observable properties of generated code. We present CodeAssay, a taxonomy-first benchmark of 185 Python tasks across ten software-engineering categories. It combines audited ground truth, public tests for generation and repair, hidden tests for grading, mutation-based test-suite validation, and selected code-property measures. Regrading fixed model outputs after the audit changed 170 of 1,890 correctness labels (9.0%) and increased the measured best-to-worst model spread from 11.9 to 23.7 percentage points, although aggregate correctness remained nearly unchanged. The complete and hidden test suites achieved mutation scores of 82.6% and 74.8%, respectively. Across seven proprietary LLMs, standard-prompt correctness ranged from 77.3% to 98.9%, with significant differences in 12 of 21 model pairs. On the 120 tasks solved by all 14 model-prompt configurations, no model performed best across all selected code properties. A security-focused prompt produced no significant change in correctness or consistent reduction in the selected static-analysis findings, while increasing program length and cyclomatic complexity across all models. These findings show that reliable evaluation of LLM-generated code requires validated ground truth, protected tests, and multiple explicitly interpreted measures. CodeAssay provides a reproducible basis for evidence-based model evaluation in AI-augmented software development.

</details>


### [256] [EffiHolmes: Differential Profiling-Guided Repository Level Time Inefficiency Fix Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03558)
*Haowen Yang,Yun Peng,Zishuo Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EffiHolmes，一个基于LLM的仓库级时间低效修复定位框架，通过差分性能剖析识别热点、提取紧凑执行路径、并利用领域引导的LLM推理定位低效逻辑。同时引入了首个仓库级低效定位基准RepoEffi-Bench（含140个问题），实验表明EffiHolmes在多个基线对比中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型软件系统常因时间低效导致执行时间过长，但不同于功能缺陷，时间低效不会产生测试失败或堆栈线索，使得传统和基于LLM的缺陷定位方法均不适用。运行时剖析虽能提供替代证据，但在仓库级别存在三大挑战：单次剖析无法区分热点与执行噪声、现有剖析器难以从大量后台执行中提取相关执行路径、观察到的热点与实际修复位置之间存在语义鸿沟。

Method: EffiHolmes包含三个核心组件：1）差分剖析（differential profiling），在默认负载与缩放负载下对比运行，识别低效热点；2）提取连接热点与报告的低效函数之间的紧凑执行路径；3）采用领域引导的LLM推理，定位潜在的底层低效逻辑。此外还构建了RepoEffi-Bench基准，包含从流行Python仓库收集的140个高质量低效问题。

Result: 实验表明EffiHolmes一致优于最先进的基于检索、基于智能体和基于剖析的基线方法：使用GPT-5.1时文件级Acc@3提升4.29个百分点，使用qwen3-4b时函数级Acc@5提升15.00个百分点。该方法在不同模型容量下均保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EffiHolmes有效解决了仓库级时间低效修复定位问题，通过差分剖析与领域引导LLM推理相结合，弥合了热点与实际修复位置之间的语义鸿沟，在多个基线上取得显著提升，且在模型规模变化时保持稳定性。

Abstract: Large software systems often suffer from time inefficiencies that cause excessive execution time despite functional correctness. Localizing their fix locations is difficult because, unlike functional bugs, they produce neither test failures nor stack-trace clues, making traditional and recent LLM-based fault localization methods unsuitable. Runtime profiling provides alternative evidence but faces three challenges in repository-level settings: single-run profiling cannot reliably distinguish inefficiency hotspots from execution noise; existing profilers struggle to extract relevant execution paths from extensive background execution; and a semantic gap remains between observed hotspots and actual fix locations. We propose EffiHolmes, an LLM-based framework for repository-level time inefficiency fix localization. EffiHolmes uses differential profiling under default and scaled workloads to identify inefficiency hotspots, extracts compact execution paths connecting these hotspots to the reported inefficient function, and employs domain-guided LLM reasoning to locate the underlying inefficiency logic. We also introduce RepoEffi-Bench, the first benchmark for repository-level inefficiency localization, containing 140 high-quality issues collected from popular Python repositories. Experiments show that EffiHolmes consistently outperforms state-of-the-art retrieval-, agent-, and profiling-based baselines, improving file-level Acc@3 by 4.29 percentage points with GPT-5.1 and function-level Acc@5 by 15.00 percentage points with qwen3-4b. It also remains robust across model capacities.

</details>


### [257] [From Bug Reports to Browser-Executable Procedures: An LLM-Driven Agent for Web GUI Bug Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03598)
*Cunming Zhang,Yu Pei,Michail Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ReBug，一个上下文感知的代理系统，能够从自然语言Web GUI bug报告中重构、执行和验证浏览器层面的复现流程，通过驱动真实浏览器实现端到端的可视化执行与验证。在667个真实bug报告上，ReBug平均RSR达49.96%，任务完成率达74.96%，动作执行成功率达86.54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言bug报告中复现Web GUI bug对软件维护至关重要，但报告通常缺少前置条件（如依赖和输入文件）。现有bug复现技术主要针对代码单元或移动应用，缺乏对Web GUI的端到端可视化执行和验证能力。

Method: ReBug将复现分为两个阶段：准备阶段重构报告中缺失的前置条件并生成高层复现计划；执行阶段在浏览器中进行工具介导的交互，维护页面状态和动作历史的结构化摘要，并基于报告衍生的期望验证最终状态。

Result: 在来自四个开源Web应用的667个真实bug报告上评估，ReBug平均RSR为49.96%，平均任务完成率为74.96%，平均动作执行成功率为86.54%，优于两个基线方法。历史回放表明成功的复现流程在恢复的bug版本上通常能暴露原始的bug行为。

Conclusion: 显式的上下文重构和状态感知的浏览器执行能有效支持基于报告的浏览器复现，为Web GUI bug自动复现提供了可行的研究方向。

Abstract: Reproducing web GUI bugs from natural-language bug reports is critical for software maintenance, but remains difficult because reports often lack prerequisites such as dependencies and input files. Existing bug reproduction techniques mainly target code units or mobile applications and lack end-to-end visual execution and validation for web GUIs. We present ReBug, a context-aware agent system that reconstructs, executes, and validates browser-level reproduction procedures from web GUI bug reports by driving a real browser. ReBug separates reproduction into two stages. In the preparation stage, ReBug reconstructs missing prerequisites from the report and available artifacts, and it produces a high-level reproduction plan. In the execution stage, it performs tool-mediated interactions in the browser, maintains structured summaries of page state and action history, and validates the final state against expectations derived from the report. We evaluate ReBug on 667 real-world bug reports from four open-source web applications. On controlled current deployments, ReBug outperforms both baselines, achieving an average RSR of 49.96%, a mean task completion rate of 74.96%, and a mean action execution success rate of 86.54%. Our results show that explicit context reconstruction and state-aware browser execution effectively support report-derived browser reproduction, while historical replay shows that successful procedures often expose the original bug-present behavior on restored buggy versions.

</details>


### [258] [Pattern over Pixels: Measuring Pattern Completion Bias in Multimodal Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03691)
*Khai-Nguyen Nguyen,Oscar Chaparro,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次提出视觉模式补全偏差基准，系统揭示多模态大语言模型在网页截图转代码任务中会被重复UI模式强烈诱导，产生模式一致但视觉错误的输出。基于Design2Code数据集的30个网页构建1,440张评估截图，五个前沿MLLM的平均偏差率高达69.78%（卡片宽度）和80.22%（文本字号），而平均准确率仅21.17%和7.89%。研究还发现噪声、细微扰动用和边界位置会加剧偏差，且模型即使识别出异常元素仍会覆盖为模式一致答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在将网页截图翻译为前端代码时，重复的UI模式可能导致模型输出视觉不正确但模式一致的结果。现有工作缺乏对该系统性偏差的量化评估，因此需要专门的基准来测试这一失败模式。

Method: 设计了第一个视觉模式补全偏差基准：在重复UI模式中扰动一个局部元素，要求模型从截图和HTML上下文中恢复被遮蔽的宽度或字号值。从Design2Code数据集的30个网页出发，构建了1,440张评估截图，覆盖结构卡片和文本样式两类模式，并包含标准条件和噪声叠加条件。

Result: 评估五个前沿MLLM发现所有模型都强烈偏向重复基线：卡片宽度扰动的平均偏差率为69.78%，文本字号扰动的平均偏差率为80.22%，而平均准确率分别仅21.17%和7.89%。Codex-5.3表现最佳但在卡片上准确率从68.61%降至文本上的13.89%，Flash-3.0在文本上的偏差率高达96.11%。噪声、更细微扰动和边界位置进一步增加偏差率。推理分析显示推理努力越大偏差越低，但定性证据表明模型即使识别出异常元素仍会用模式一致答案覆盖。

Conclusion: 研究确认了多模态代码生成中的具体失败模式，且其严重性与视觉显著性密切相关。这揭示了当前MLLM在抗重复模式干扰方面存在系统性缺陷，为改进多模态代码生成模型的鲁棒性提供了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to translate webpage screenshots into front-end code, but repeated UI patterns may sway them toward visually incorrect yet pattern-consistent outputs. In this work, we test how repeated webpage patterns hurt MLLM accuracy on an objective screenshot-to-code fill-in-the-blank task. We introduce the first benchmark for visual pattern-completion bias, where one localized element in a repeated UI pattern is perturbed and the model must recover the masked width or font-size value from the screenshot and HTML context. Starting from 30 webpages curated from the Design2Code dataset, we build 1,440 evaluated screenshots spanning structural card and text-style patterns under standard and noise-overlaid conditions. We evaluate five frontier MLLMs and find that all are strongly biased toward the repeated baseline. Mean bias rate reaches 69.78% on card-width perturbations and 80.22% on text font-size perturbations, while mean accuracy is only 21.17% and 7.89%, respectively. Codex-5.3 performs best but still drops from 68.61% accuracy on cards to 13.89% on text, while Flash-3.0 reaches 96.11% bias on text. Noise, subtler perturbations, and boundary positions further increase bias rate. Reasoning analysis further shows that greater reasoning effort correlates with lower bias, yet qualitative evidence reveals that models can identify the anomalous element and still override it with the pattern-consistent answer. Our results identify a concrete failure mode in multimodal code generation and show that its severity is strongly associated with visual saliency

</details>


### [259] [We Must Have Missed This Comment: Detecting and Repairing Stale Function References in Linux Kernel Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03734)
*Kexin Sun,Yunbo Lyu,Xutong Ma,Hongyu Kuang,Ratnadira Widyasari,He Zhang,Xiaoxing Ma,Julia Lawall,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCite，一个三阶段方法，用于检测和修复Linux内核代码注释中因函数重构或删除而过时的函数引用。在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上检测出869个过期引用，约89%的修复建议有用，50个补丁已被上游接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核演化过程中，注释中引用的函数可能被重构或删除而注释未同步更新，形成外部诱导的过期引用（externally induced staleness）。已有代码-注释不一致检测工作主要聚焦于Javadoc注释与其直接注释函数之间的语义错位，无法适用于Linux内核中这种由外部变更导致的过期引用问题。

Method: ReCite采用三阶段方法：(1) 检测未解析的函数形式符号——注释中看似引用函数但在当前代码库中无法匹配到对应函数的符号；(2)通过Git历史追踪每个未解析符号的演化历史；(3)基于演化历史和当前代码上下文，使用大语言模型生成修复建议。此外还对所有未解析的函数形式符号进行了实证研究。

Result: 在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上，ReCite检测出869个过期引用并生成修复建议。人工评估200个抽样修复，其中178个（89.0%）提供了有用的修复指导，85个（42.5%）可直接应用。提交的75个补丁中有50个已被接受。

Conclusion: ReCite能够有效检测和修复Linux内核中注释中外部诱导的过期函数引用，其修复建议质量较高，且大量补丁获得上游认可，证明了该方法的实用价值。

Abstract: As the Linux kernel evolves, code comments may become outdated, as the functions they reference can be refactored or removed independently without corresponding updates to the comments. Such stale function references can mislead maintainers and thus hinder code comprehension. Prior work on detecting code-comment inconsistency mainly focused on addressing semantic misalignment between Javadoc comments and their directly annotated functions, making them inapplicable to this type of externally induced staleness in the Linux kernel. Therefore, we propose ReCite, a three-stage approach to identify and repair such stale references: (1) detecting unresolved function-form symbols -- symbols in comments that appear to reference functions but for which no matching function can be found in the current codebase, (2) tracing the evolution history of each unresolved symbol through the Git history, and (3) generating LLM-based repair suggestions grounded in the evolution history and current code context. On Linux kernel v6.18-rc1, ReCite detects 869 stale references with generated repair suggestions. A manual evaluation on 200 sampled repairs shows that 178 (89.0%) provide useful repair guidance, with 85 (42.5%) directly applicable. Of our 75 submitted patches, 50 have been accepted. We also empirically study all unresolved function-form symbols.

</details>


### [260] [Can LLMs Test Terminal User Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03743)
*Chao Peng,Ruida Hu,Ajitha Rajan,Tegawendé F Bissyandé,Jacques Klein,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了终端用户界面（TUI）应用的自动化测试问题。通过对197个真实TUI应用的调查发现现有测试严重不足，并构建了一个跨四种语言框架的headless基准测试平台，对比了四种前沿LLM与随机探索的测试效果。结果表明没有单一模型占优，随机探索在时间预算下是强基线，但LLM在每次交互效率和触及输入门控故障方面更优；自动推导启动输入是最大的实用改进点；行覆盖率不能很好地预测崩溃发现。作者发布了覆盖率工具tuicov和测试框架tuibot。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TUI应用结合了GUI的屏幕导向行为与终端部署方式，在开发者工具中日益普及，但缺乏专门的测试方法论。现有测试实践严重不足（仅12%的测试代码实际执行接口，45%的接口测试从不发送输入），因此需要系统性地研究TUI自动化测试的方法与工具。

Method: 作者首先调查了197个真实TUI应用以量化测试现状；随后构建了一个跨ratatui/Rust、bubbletea/Go、textual/Python、ink/TypeScript四种框架的headless基准测试套件，将每个应用打包为带插桩的Docker镜像，并记录行覆盖率、widget覆盖率、渲染终端状态及崩溃信息。在相等墙钟时间预算下，对比四种前沿LLM与随机探索策略。

Result: 实验发现：没有单一模型在所有指标上占优；随机探索在时间预算下是强基线，其崩溃发现优势源于更高的吞吐量；但按每次交互计算，LLM引导更高效且能独特地触及需要输入门控的故障；自动推导启动输入带来最大的实践收益，使原本无法启动的应用得以运行；行覆盖率对崩溃发现预测力弱，不能作为测试有效性的可靠代理。

Conclusion: TUI自动化测试是可行的但远未解决；诚实的基线（如随机探索）比模型选择更重要。该工作为TUI测试领域提供了首个系统的基准和方法论，并开源了tuicov覆盖率工具和tuibot测试框架，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Terminal User Interfaces (TUIs) combine the stateful, screen-oriented behaviour of GUIs with terminal deployment and are now common in developer tools. Yet they lack a dedicated testing methodology. We survey 197 real-world TUI applications: only 12% of test code exercises the interface, and 45% of those tests never send input, checking a static frame instead. We turn these applications into a headless benchmark spanning ratatui/Rust, bubbletea/Go, textual/Python, and ink/TypeScript, packaging each as an instrumented Docker image. We record line and widget coverage where reliable, rendered terminal states, and crashes. Under equal wall-clock budgets, we compare four frontier LLMs with random exploration. No model dominates.
