Random is a strong time-budgeted baseline, but its crash advantage comes from higher throughput: per interaction, LLM guidance is more efficient and uniquely reaches input-gated faults. Automatically deriving launch inputs yields the largest practical gain, enabling applications that otherwise never start. Line coverage poorly predicts crash discovery, weakening it as a proxy for test effectiveness. Automated TUI testing is feasible but far from solved, and honest baselines matter more than model choice. We release the coverage tool tuicov at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuicov and the testing framework tuibot at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuibot.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Knowledge-Geometry Decoupling: Refreshable Pretrained Transfer for Streaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02738)
*Zixuan Wang,Yuhong Chen,Yuxuan Zhu,Guidong Lei,Zhiluohan Guo,Yu Zhao,Kun Wang,Bangyang Hong,Kangle Wu,Yabo Ni,Anxiang Zeng,Cong Fu,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出知识几何解耦（KGD）框架，解决工业推荐系统中pretrain-then-transfer范式下的两个核心问题：从行为序列中学什么以及如何转移预训练知识。通过行为多Token预测（BMTP）只保留协同或语义相关的未来项作为监督信号，并通过可刷新编码器与任务学习器参数解耦、跨注意力只读访问及锚定校准残差（ACR）正交化写入，实现持续知识刷新且不干扰下游任务。在8个公开基准上比强基线提升4-12%，在Shopee首页搜索中使GMV提升1.75%、广告收入提升1.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统广泛采用预训练-微调范式的背景下，行为分布漂移带来两个关键挑战：一是传统的下一Token预测将相邻行为视为依赖关系，可能编码跨无关会话的虚假转换，导致行为知识不干净；二是预训练知识与任务特定几何结构对共享参数提出冲突的优化需求，限制了持续知识刷新和下游适配的有效性。

Method: KGD框架包含两大核心组件：1）行为多Token预测（BMTP）——只保留通过协同过滤或语义关联确认的未来行为作为监督，过滤虚假转换，获得更干净、更具迁移性的行为知识；2）参数解耦架构——可刷新编码器独占行为知识，任务学习器通过只读跨注意力读取编码器状态，并使用与预训练嵌入正交的锚定校准残差（ACR）写入任务特定几何结构，实现任务梯度不干扰预训练知识且知识持续刷新不使下游适配失效。

Result: KGD在8个公开基准上相对强预训练-迁移基线提升4-12%；在90天生产流上持续保持优势，而基线无增益；已在Shopee全面部署，首页搜索A/B测试中GMV/用户提升1.75%、广告收入提升1.53%。

Conclusion: KGD通过将"学到什么"与"如何迁移"两个问题解耦，有效应对行为分布漂移，实现了可泛化、可持续刷新的预训练推荐知识，并在大规模工业部署中验证了其实际商业价值。

Abstract: Industrial recommenders increasingly adopt the pretrain-then-transfer paradigm, yet behavioral distribution drift raises two questions: what to learn from behavior sequences, and how to transfer the learned knowledge while the pretrained model is continually refreshed. To resolve them, we propose Knowledge-Geometry Decoupling (KGD). For what to learn, conventional next-token prediction treats adjacency as dependency and may encode spurious transitions across unrelated sessions. We introduce Behavioral Multi-Token Prediction (BMTP) to retain only collaboratively or semantically related future items as supervision, yielding cleaner and more transferable behavioral knowledge. For how to transfer, pretrained knowledge and task-specific geometry impose conflicting optimization demands on shared parameters. To handle it, KGD assigns them to separate parameter sets: a refreshable encoder owns behavioral knowledge, while a task learner reads contextualized encoder states through read-only cross-attention and writes task-specific geometry through Anchored Calibration Residual (ACR) orthogonal to the pretrained embedding. The decoupled ownership enables continual knowledge refresh without task-gradient interference or invalidating downstream adaptation. KGD improves over strong pretrain-transfer baselines by 4-12% on eight public benchmarks and sustains its advantage over a 90-day production stream where baselines show no gains. KGD has been fully deployed in Shopee. In a live A/B test on Shopee Homepage Search, it increases GMV per user by 1.75% and advertising revenue by 1.53%, demonstrating its high practical value. We provide the core implementation of KGD at https://github.com/FuCongResearchSquad/KGD4REC.

</details>


### [262] [Search, Inspect, Fetch: Exploiting Boolean Retrieval for Deep-Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02751)
*Shuai Wang,Haodong Chen,Yu Yin,Shengyao Zhuang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SIEVE，一种基于字段化布尔检索（BQL）的搜索-检查-获取（search-inspect-fetch）接口，通过利用网页文档的结构化字段（标题、标题、章节、元数据）来约束检索和获取，在三个QA数据集上相比传统Search-Visit工作流提高了准确率并减少了20.7-50.6%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究代理采用搜索-访问（search-visit）工作流，检索并读取整个页面，但忽略了网络来源通过标题、标题、章节和元数据暴露的可寻址结构。这导致代理无法将检索直接约束到文档字段，并且往往将不相关的页面内容带入上下文，造成准确率下降和token浪费。

Method: SIEVE采用搜索-检查-获取三段式流程：首先通过字段化布尔检索（BQL）在文档字段上过滤候选结果，然后对已过滤的候选集进行排序，接着展示结构丰富的结果卡片供代理检查，最后只获取选定的章节（sections）。整个过程让代理能够直接约束检索到特定文档字段，并仅将相关章节加载到上下文中。

Result: 在三个QA数据集上，SIEVE比每个数据集上最准确的常规Search-Visit配置实现了更高的准确率，同时使用了20.7-50.6%更少的token。进一步分析表明，BQL过滤改善了所有测试的排序器，且准确率-上下文优势在多种检索器选择和代理骨干网络下保持一致。

Conclusion: 利用文档的结构化字段进行字段级检索和分章节获取是深度研究代理的有效改进方向，可以在提升准确率的同时显著降低上下文token消耗。

Abstract: Existing deep-research agents use a search-visit workflow that retrieves and reads whole pages, without considering the addressable structure that web sources expose through titles, headings, sections, and metadata. This prevents agents from directly constraining retrieval to document fields and often carries irrelevant page content into their context. We introduce SIEVE, a search-inspect-fetch interface driven by fielded Boolean retrieval (BQL). SIEVE filters candidates over document fields, ranks the admitted set, presents structure-rich result cards for inspection, and fetches only selected sections. Across three QA collections, SIEVE achieves higher accuracy than the most accurate conventional Search-Visit configuration on each collection while using 20.7-50.6% fewer tokens. Further analyses show that BQL filtering improves all tested rankers and that the accuracy-context advantage persists across retriever choices and agent backbones. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ielab/skim-search-agent.

</details>


### [263] [Field Aware Agent Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02880)
*Paimon Goulart,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了终身学习智能体在技能库不断增长时，保留技能的多字段结构化表示是否能提升技能检索性能。实验表明，将技能按字段（名称、描述、正文等）分开计算稀疏和稠密相似度，再用统一权重或小型MLP组合字段级分数，能够显著优于将技能拼接成单一扁平文档的传统方法。字段感知MLP在SkillRet和SRA-Bench两个基准上分别达到77.95和83.78的Recall@10，且技能库越大优势越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身学习智能体积累的技能库不断增长时，准确检索所需技能成为关键瓶颈。现有方法将每个技能拼接为扁平文档进行检索，忽略了技能本身是结构化、多字段的对象这一天然属性，可能导致检索信息丢失。

Method: 将每个技能拆分为独立的字段组件（名称、描述、正文等），对每个字段分别计算稀疏和稠密相似度，构建张量化的字段感知技能库表示；然后通过统一权重或小型可学习的MLP组合各字段级分数，实现混合检索。

Result: 在SkillRet基准上字段感知MLP达到77.95 Recall@10，在SRA-Bench上达到83.78 Recall@10，均优于拼接式学习的基线方法。保持字段分离能改善混合检索性能，且技能库规模越大，字段感知方法的优势越明显。

Conclusion: 技能本身的表示方式对检索性能有重要影响，仅保留技能文件中的既有结构就能显著提升检索效果，尤其在技能库规模增大、检索难度最高的场景下，字段感知的检索方法最为有效。

Abstract: As lifelong learning agents accumulate lifelong growing skill banks, retrieving the correct skill becomes an increasingly important bottleneck. Most current skill retrieval methods treat each skill as one flat document by concatenating fields such as the name, description, and body. However, skills are naturally structured, multi-field objects, where each field provides different information about when and how the skill should be used. In this work, we study whether preserving this structure improves skill retrieval. We represent each skill as its separate components, and compute sparse and dense similarities for each field independently, exposing a naturally tensorized, field-aware representation of the skill bank. We then combine these field-level scores either with uniform weights or with a small learned MLP. Across two different skill retrieval benchmarks, SkillRet and SRA-Bench, we find that keeping fields separate improves hybrid retrieval, and learning over the field-level scores gives the strongest and most consistent results. Our field-aware MLP reaches $77.95$ Recall@10 on SkillRet and $83.78$ Recall@10 on SRA-Bench, outperforming the corresponding concatenated learned baselines. We also find that the advantage grows as the skill bank becomes larger, suggesting that field-aware skill retrieval becomes especially useful in the setting where retrieval is most difficult. Our results show that skill representation itself matters, and that simply preserving the structure already present in skill files can substantially improve retrieval.

</details>


### [264] [Coverage Matters: MarginMerge for Compressing Multi-Vector Visual Document Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02969)
*Ailar Mahdizadeh,Aria Salari,Sohail Rajabi,Shahriar Mirabbasi,Panos Nasiopoulos,Alireza Morsali*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MarginMerge，一种针对冻结多向量视觉文档检索器（如ColPali和ColQwen）的压缩方法。它通过覆盖感知的锚点选择、补丁聚类和轻量共享网络合成每簇的代表向量，在索引阶段一次性压缩，保留标准MaxSim检索接口。在六个数据集上，以5%和10%向量保留率取得最优的查询无关平均性能，保留97%-99%的平均nDCG@5，同时减少90%-95%的存储向量，并相比几何合并减少约41%的排名翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量视觉文档检索器（ColPali、ColQwen）虽然检索性能强，但存储大量细粒度patch嵌入导致索引庞大、后期交互打分成本高昂。现有压缩方法按显著性独立选择patch，忽略了查询相关的覆盖性（即跨查询可能成为最强MaxSim匹配的多样文档区域），因此压缩效果不理想。作者认为有效的压缩应保留查询相关的覆盖性，这也是为何密集渲染页面比自然图像更易压缩的原因。

Method: MarginMerge包含三个关键步骤：(1) 选择覆盖感知的锚点（coverage-aware anchors），识别可能成为跨查询最强MaxSim匹配的多样文档区域；(2) 对文档patch进行聚类；(3) 使用轻量共享网络为每个聚类合成一个代表性向量。压缩在索引阶段一次性完成，检索阶段保持标准MaxSim接口不变，无需重新训练，可迁移到未见数据集和不同保留率。

Result: 在六个数据集上分别基于ColQwen2.5和ColPali测试，MarginMerge在5%和10%向量保留率下取得最高的匹配查询无关平均（matched query-agnostic average）。相比未压缩索引，保留97%-99%的平均nDCG@5，同时减少90%-95%的存储文档向量。在5%保留率下，相比几何合并方法，在所有六个ColQwen2.5数据集上平均减少约41%的排名翻转。

Conclusion: 查询相关的覆盖性是多向量检索器压缩的关键原则，优先保留可能成为MaxSim最强匹配的多样文档区域比独立按显著性选择patch更有效。MarginMerge在保持检索质量的同时大幅压缩索引规模，且无需重新训练即可泛化到未见数据，为高效的多向量视觉文档检索提供了实用的压缩方案。

Abstract: Multi-vector visual document retrievers such as ColPali and ColQwen achieve strong retrieval by storing fine-grained patch embeddings, but this produces large indexes and costly late-interaction scoring. We argue that effective compression should preserve query-relevant coverage, meaning the diverse document regions that may become the strongest MaxSim match across queries, rather than selecting patches independently by salience. This view also explains why dense rendered pages are easier to compress than natural images. We introduce MarginMerge, a compression method for frozen multi-vector retrievers. It selects coverage-aware anchors, clusters document patches, and uses a lightweight shared network to synthesize one representative per cluster. Compression is performed once during indexing, while retrieval keeps the standard MaxSim interface. Across six datasets on both ColQwen2.5 and ColPali, MarginMerge achieves the highest matched query-agnostic average at 5% and 10% vector retention. Compared with the uncompressed index using the same backbone, it preserves between 97% and 99% of average nDCG@5 while reducing stored document vectors by between 90% and 95%. At 5% retention, it also reduces ranking flips relative to geometric merging on all six ColQwen2.5 datasets by approximately 41% on average. The same model transfers to unseen datasets and retention ratios without retraining.

</details>


### [265] [Position Bias Undermines Preference Consistency in Listwise LLM-Based Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03091)
*Ethan Bito,Yongli Ren,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM(listwise reranker)在等价候选排列下的位置偏差问题，提出了一个评估框架，从成对偏好不稳定性、全局偏好不一致性和列表输出一致性三个层面衡量候选顺序敏感性，发现这些一致性指标与推荐有效性及边际位置暴露偏差可能脱节，表明减少暴露偏差不足以建立排序函数有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为listwise reranker在推荐系统中表现出潜力，但其在等价候选排列下的可靠性尚不明确。推荐候选本质上是无序集合，重排序器不应依赖于序列化它们的任意顺序。然而Decoder-only的LLM重排序器可能让输入顺序影响模型得分、成对偏好和排序结果。现有研究仅关注最终排序列表的变化，缺乏对排序过程本身是否受位置偏差影响的系统评估。

Method: 将等价候选排列下产生的排序视为诱导偏好系统的观测值，引入评估框架，包含三个层次的度量：成对偏好不稳定性(pairwise preference instability)、全局偏好不一致性(global preference inconsistency)和列表输出一致性(listwise output consistency)，从而在成对、全局和输出三个层面刻画候选顺序敏感性。

Result: 在多个LLM、数据集和列表长度上的实验表明，这些一致性度量高度一致，但可能与推荐有效性和边际位置暴露偏差出现分歧。提高相关性或使各位置暴露度平坦化，并不能必然恢复稳定的成对偏好、全局一致的偏好结构或一致的排序输出。

Conclusion: 减少边际暴露偏差不足以在LLM重排序中建立排序函数的有效性，需要更全面的框架来评估LLM重排序器在置换不变性方面的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as promising listwise rerankers for recommender systems, but their reliability under equivalent candidate permutations remains unclear. Since recommendation candidates form an unordered set, a reranker should not depend on the arbitrary order used to serialize them. However, decoder-only LLM rerankers can allow input order to affect model scores, pairwise preferences, and rankings. We study how position bias affects the ranking process induced by LLM-based rerankers. Instead of measuring only changes in final ranked lists, we treat rankings produced under equivalent candidate permutations as observations of an induced preference system. We introduce an evaluation framework measuring pairwise preference instability, global preference inconsistency, and listwise output consistency. This framework characterizes candidate-order sensitivity at the pairwise, global, and output levels. Experiments across multiple LLMs, datasets, and list lengths show that these consistency measures are closely aligned, but can diverge from recommendation effectiveness and marginal position-exposure bias. Improving relevance or flattening exposure across positions does not necessarily restore stable pairwise preferences, globally coherent preference structures, or consistent ranked outputs. These results show that reducing marginal exposure skew is insufficient to establish ranking-function validity in LLM-based reranking. Code is available at https://github.com/ejbito/InvariRank .

</details>


### [266] [Attacking and Defending Multi-Agent Collaborative Filtering Systems Through Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03272)
*Anjun Hu,Hanting Xie,Saranya Govindan,Jas Kandola,Kurt Cutajar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在AgentCF框架中系统研究了多智能体协同过滤（CF）系统的连接性如何调制其对抗攻防行为，沿候选数量与目录集中度两个维度刻画连接性，揭示了用户/物品智能体的角色不对称性、攻击效力的非单调时间动态以及不同攻击目标下的发散模式，并探索了流行病学启发的静态指标用于成本高效的鲁棒性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协同过滤系统通过自然语言交互协调自治的LLM驱动的用户与物品智能体，既继承了数据驱动方法的脆弱性，也具有多智能体交互特有的安全漏洞，但连接性如何调制这些系统的漏洞尚不清楚，理解这一点有助于开发更鲁棒的推荐管线。

Method: 将通用多智能体系统（MAS）文献中的攻击与防御方法适配到基于智能体的CF场景，在AgentCF框架中进行系统性评估。CF连接性沿两个轴向刻画：(i)候选数量（用户侧交互密度）与(ii)目录集中度（物品目录跨用户重叠程度）。此外，评估流行病学启发的静态指标在排序CF配置预期攻击结果方面的适用性。

Result: 证实了MAS攻击与防御在智能体CF领域中的部分可迁移性；揭示了用户与物品智能体之间的角色不对称性、攻击效力的非单调时间动态，以及传播与提取两类攻击目标间的发散模式；探索性实验表明流行病学静态指标具备潜在的成本高效鲁棒性评估价值。

Conclusion: 多智能体CF系统的连接性显著影响其攻防鲁棒性，理解连接性-脆弱性关系可支持更鲁棒推荐管线的开发，流行病学启发的静态指标为低成本鲁棒性评估提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent collaborative filtering (CF) systems coordinate autonomous LLM-powered user and item agents through natural-language interaction to refine preferences and generate recommendations. These systems inherit vulnerabilities from both their data-driven nature and their multi-agent interactions, which manifest in distinct ways. Understanding how connectivity modulates vulnerability in these systems could facilitate the development of more robust recommendation pipelines.
  In this work, we adapt attacks and defenses from the general multi-agent systems (MAS) literature to the agent-based CF setting, evaluating them under systematically varied connectivity in the AgentCF framework, where CF connectivity is characterized along two axes: (i) candidate count (the number of item candidates per turn per user, measuring user-side interaction density) and (ii) catalog concentration (the degree of item catalog overlap across users).
  Our contributions include: (1) Adaptation: we reproduce MAS-inspired attacks and defenses in the agentic CF domain, confirming partial transferability of original observations. (2) Characterization: we characterize how the two aspects of connectivity shape attack and defense outcomes, revealing role asymmetries between user and item agents, non-monotonic temporal dynamics in attack efficacy, and divergent patterns across dissemination and extraction attack goals. Additionally, as an exploratory extension, we assess the applicability of epidemic-inspired static metrics in ranking CF configurations by expected attack outcome, potentially enabling cost-efficient robustness assessment. Implementation is available at https://github.com/anjunhu/ConnACF

</details>


### [267] [LLM-Derived Priors for Thompson Sampling in Cold-Start Comment Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03382)
*Eugene Lee,Oseong Choi,Byungsoo Kang,Taeyeong Jang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）从用户生成文本评论中提取语义信号，转化为贝叶斯先验来热启动Thompson sampling多臂老虎机算法，从而缓解冷启动问题。在真实在线A/B/C测试中比较了均匀先验、性别先验和内容先验三种方案，发现LLM先验在稀疏反馈场景下收益最大，且不同先验设计产生不同的漏斗效应和人口统计异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂老虎机算法（特别是Thompson sampling）在在线推荐中广泛应用，但新加入的臂由于缺乏交互历史而面临冷启动问题。本文的候选臂是用户生成的文本评论，其语义内容可以在互动反馈之前就揭示标题的吸引力，因此可以利用LLM提取语义信号作为先验信息来缓解冷启动。

Method: 使用LLM从评论文本中提取语义信号，将其转换为信息性贝叶斯先验，对Thompson sampling进行热启动。针对不同性别-年龄段的响应模式差异，为每个细分群体分别维护和更新后验分布。在真实在线A/B/C实验中比较了均匀先验、基于人口统计亲和力的性别先验（Gender Prior）和基于标题特定身份线索的内容先验（Content Prior）三种设计。

Result: LLM-based先验在稀疏反馈场景下收益最大，最大增益出现在少量交互证据累积之后。先验设计产生明显的漏斗级效果差异；点击导向的对齐在性别先验上最强；治疗效果在不同人口统计细分群体间差异显著。

Conclusion: LLM派生先验可以作为文本丰富型bandit推荐的实际热启动机制，同时揭示了部署时的权衡取舍。

Abstract: Multi-armed bandit algorithms, especially Thompson sampling, are widely used in online recommendation. Despite their ability to adapt from online feedback, these methods often suffer from cold-start limitations when newly introduced arms have little or no interaction history. In our setting, the candidate arms are user-generated textual comments, whose semantic content can reveal a title's appeal before sufficient interaction feedback is available. We therefore use large language models (LLMs) to extract semantic signals from comment text and convert them into informative Bayesian priors that warm-start Thompson sampling under sparse early-stage feedback. To account for aggregate segment-level differences in response patterns, we maintain and update posteriors separately for each gender-age segment. In a real-world online A/B/C test, we compare a uniform prior with two LLM-based designs: a Gender Prior for demographic-affinity cues and a Content Prior for title-specific identity cues. The results show that LLM-based priors are most beneficial in sparse-feedback regimes -- with the largest gains emerging once a small amount of interaction evidence has accumulated -- and that prior design leads to distinct funnel-level effects. We further analyze prior-reward alignment and demographic heterogeneity, finding that click-oriented alignment is strongest for the Gender Prior and that treatment effects vary substantially across demographic segments. These findings suggest that LLM-derived priors can serve as a practical warm-start mechanism for text-rich bandit recommendation, while also revealing deployment trade-offs.

</details>


### [268] [Training Documents Reranker with Search Rubrics for Deep Research Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03527)
*Wenhan Liu,Yu Lu,Qiaolin Xia,Hui Xu,Tong Zhao,Jian Xi,Yutao Zhu,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi,Hao Wang,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RubricRanker，一种基于搜索导向rubrics的文档重排序器，通过层次化rubrics显式定义高质量文档集应满足的要求，并采用两阶段训练框架（rubrics引导的监督微调+基于rubrics的强化学习）来训练重排序模型，在四个深度研究基准和五个RAG基准上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统通过相关性匹配选择文档，但单独匹配良好的top-k文档组合成一个集合时，可能无法满足代理查询的复杂信息需求（如多样性、简洁性、权威性）。需要一种显式定义文档集质量要求的检索方法。

Method: 提出搜索导向rubrics，通过强大的LLM合成为层次化结构，显式定义每个代理查询的高质量文档集要求。基于这些rubrics训练RubricRanker文档重排序器，采用两阶段训练框架：第一阶段为rubrics引导的监督微调，第二阶段为基于rubrics的强化学习。

Result: RubricRanker在四个深度研究基准上比最强基线高出2.6个点，并在五个RAG基准上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式定义搜索rubrics并将集合级质量要求引入重排序训练，可以显著提升检索文档集对复杂代理查询的整体满足度，且该方法能够良好泛化到不同的检索增强生成场景。

Abstract: Retrieval systems help deep research agents generate high-quality answers by providing relevant documents. However, existing retrievers typically select documents through relevance matching, while individually well-matched top-$k$ documents may not form a \textit{set} that satisfies the complex information needs of an agent query (\eg, diverse, concise and authoritative documents). In this paper, we propose search-oriented rubrics that \textit{explicitly} define the requirements that high-quality document sets should satisfy for each agent query. Our search rubrics are organized into a hierarchical structure and synthesized using a powerful LLM. Based on these search rubrics, we further train a document reranker \textbf{RubricRanker} to select a high-quality subset from retrieved documents. We design a two-stage training framework that consists of rubrics-guided supervised fine-tuning and rubric-based reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RubricRanker outperforms the strongest baseline by 2.6 points on four deep research benchmarks and generalizes well to five RAG benchmarks.

</details>


### [269] [Conditionally Identifiable Latent-Environment Modeling for Out-of-Distribution Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03647)
*Qianqian Wang,Wenwu Gong,Yunshan Li,Zhenqing Wu,Ruili Wang,Lili Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CILER框架，用于条件可识别的风险感知推荐系统，通过用户条件指数族建模潜在环境、特征索引多项式建模偏好变化，实现了在特征、时间和地理分布偏移下对OOD推荐指标的全面改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OOD推荐易受潜在环境引起的偏好漂移影响，现有方法虽能从历史交互中推断潜在状态，但潜在环境的统计含义及其对偏好的影响机制未被充分确定，导致模型识别性（identifiability）不足。

Method: CILER采用两个核心组件：(1) 用户条件指数族（user-conditioned exponential family）建模潜在环境分布；(2) 特征索引多项式（feature-indexed polynomial）刻画环境如何改变用户偏好。预测过程通过对推断出的环境分布边缘化物品概率实现。在充足变异性、正确设定和编码器正则性条件下，识别环境敏感表示至声明的等价类，并给出超额部署日志风险的界。

Result: 在三个数据集上，CILER在共享支持内的特征、时间和地理偏移场景下，全面提升了全部十二个OOD排序指标。受控实验验证了充足变异性和模型设定对可识别性的影响。

Conclusion: CILER为OOD推荐提供了条件可识别的理论保证和实证验证，证明了充足变异性、正确模型设定与解码器正则性是实现可靠环境推断和风险控制的关键条件。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) recommendation is vulnerable to preference shifts induced by a latent environment. Existing methods can infer latent states from logged interactions, yet the statistical meaning of the latent environment and its effect on preference remain underdetermined. We formulate this task as conditionally identifiable risk-aware recommendation (CI-RR) and propose Conditionally Identifiable Latent-Environment Recommendation (CILER). CILER uses a user-conditioned exponential family to model the latent environment and a feature-indexed polynomial to specify how it changes preference. It predicts by marginalizing item probabilities over the inferred environment distribution. Under sufficient variation, correct specification, and decoder regularity, CILER identifies the environment-sensitive representation up to the stated equivalence class. We further bound excess deployment log-risk by environment-inference error. Controlled studies test the observable consequences of sufficient variation and model specification. Experiments on three datasets show that CILER improves all twelve OOD ranking metrics under feature, temporal, and geographical shifts within shared support.

</details>


### [270] [SITA: Semantic Interest Tokens for Target-Aware Compression in Long-Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03692)
*Rui Zhou,Bo Chen,Qinglin Jia,Jiezhou Ji,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SITA框架，通过语义标识符将压缩的用户兴趣组织成语义结构，实现目标感知的长期行为序列压缩建模，兼顾效率和目标自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长序列推荐方法存在两大方向：动态检索目标相关行为虽然实现目标感知但推理时需要目标依赖计算；压缩整个行为序列获得高效可扩展性但丢失了目标特定适应能力。关键挑战是如何在保持压缩用户表示的高效和可扩展性的同时实现目标感知建模。

Method: SITA通过并行语义量化学习语义标识符，将压缩兴趣组织成语义结构；基于目标物品的语义标识符，自适应聚合对应的结构化兴趣来构建目标特定的用户表示。

Result: 在公开数据集和大规模工业数据集上的大量实验表明，SITA在保持强可扩展性的同时持续优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: SITA展示了在实际推荐系统中的强大应用潜力，成功实现了目标感知建模与压缩表示效率的兼顾。

Abstract: As user behavior histories continue to grow on modern Internet platforms, effectively modeling long behavior sequences has become crucial for predicting user interests in candidate items. Existing methods have evolved along two directions. One line dynamically retrieves target-relevant behaviors from long histories, enabling target-aware modeling but requiring target-dependent computation during inference. The other line compresses entire behavior sequences into compact user representations, achieving high efficiency and scalability but sacrificing target-specific adaptation due to target-independent encoding. The key challenge is therefore to enable target-aware modeling while preserving the efficiency and scalability of compressed user representations. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{SITA}, a target-aware compression framework for long-sequence recommendation. SITA enables target-aware compression by organizing compressed interests into semantic structures through semantic identifiers learned via parallel semantic quantization. Conditioned on the semantic identifier of the target item, SITA adaptively aggregates the corresponding structured interests to construct the target-specific user representation. Extensive experiments on public datasets and a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate that SITA consistently outperforms representative baselines while maintaining strong scalability, highlighting its strong potential for real-world recommender systems.

</details>


### [271] [LegalPincite: Multi-level Legal Information Retrieval Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03756)
*Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Henrik Palmer Olsen,Giovanni Colavizza*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LegalPincite，一个基于欧盟法院判决的大规模法律IR数据集，解决了现有公共法律检索数据集缺乏段落级引文标注、查询存在数据泄漏以及语料库排除未引段落的问题，支持案例到案例、段落到案例和段落到段落三种粒度的检索任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律实践中常需要定位特定段落的精确引用（pincite），但现有公共法律IR数据集要么缺乏段落级引文标注，要么存在查询文本中的数据泄漏（引用信息直接暴露在查询中），且语料库仅包含被引用段落而排除了既非引用也非被引用的其他段落，导致检索环境不真实且过于简化，可能带来虚高的性能表现。

Method: 作者构建了一个基于欧盟法院（CJEU）判决的大规模法律IR数据集，包含三个关键设计：（i）掩码后的案件/段落查询，移除了其中的引用信息以避免数据泄漏；（ii）包含所有段落的完整语料库；（iii）案例级和段落级的地面真值引文标注，并经过部分人类专家验证。数据集支持多种粒度的检索（案例到案例、段落到案例、段落到段落）。

Result: 提供的数据集兼具规模性与真实性：（1）查询中的引用信息被剔除，消除了数据泄漏；（2）语料库涵盖全部段落而非仅引文相关段落，更贴近真实检索场景；（3）提供案例级和段落级标注，并经部分人工专家验证，可用于法律IR方法的开发与严格评估。

Conclusion: LegalPincite填补了法律IR领域缺乏真实、无泄漏、含段落级标注数据集的空白，为多粒度法律信息检索研究提供了标准化测试平台，有望更真实地评估和推动法律IR方法的发展。

Abstract: A common task in legal Information Retrieval (IR) is to find relevant legal sources from case-law collections. While legal practice often requires pinpoint citations (pincites) to specific case paragraphs, most existing public legal IR datasets lack paragraph-level citation annotations. Yet, publicly available datasets with such information contain data leakage in the query text and exclude paragraphs that are neither citing nor cited from the corpora, creating an unrealistic and oversimplified retrieval setting, potentially leading to inflated performance. To address these limitations, we contribute a large-scale legal IR dataset constructed from Court of Justice of the European Union (CJEU) judgments. The dataset contains: (i) masked case/paragraph queries, with removed citation information; (ii) a corpus that includes all paragraphs; and (iii) case- and paragraph-level ground-truth citations, with partial human expert validation. Our dataset supports both the development and rigorous evaluation of legal IR methods, at multiple query-document levels (case-to-case, paragraph-to-case, and paragraph-to-paragraph retrieval). Link to dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/theresiavr/legalpincite

</details>


### [272] [ATLAS: Learning to Recommend Across Unseen Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03899)
*Pervez Shaik,Prosenjit Biswas,Abhinav Thorat,Ravi Kolla,Niranjan Pedanekar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS框架，一种多源推荐域泛化方法，通过从异构源域学习域不变的共享用户-物品表示，实现无需目标域适配或语言模型预训练的零样本推荐。在五个Amazon源域训练并直接应用于十个未见目标域，ATLAS在多数目标域上超越SOTA基线，平均HitRate相对提升24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统具有强烈的域绑定特性：模型在一个交互环境上训练后，要推广到新的目录通常需要重新训练或进行目标域适配（如从电影推荐迁移到杂货或视频游戏推荐）。现有方法要么只迁移受限的推荐知识，要么需要目标域适配，要么依赖LLM的可迁移表征。作者希望探究：仅从多个异构源域中学到的推荐特定知识，能否在没有目标域适配或语言模型预训练的情况下泛化到完全未见的新域。

Method: ATLAS框架包含三个核心组件：(1) Gromov-Wasserstein对齐，用于保留跨域用户之间的相对关系结构；(2) 对抗性目标，使物品表示在不同域间不可区分；(3) 残差向量量化（RVQ）码本，将用户和物品嵌入压缩为离散潜空间，捕获层次化交互模式并抑制域特定变异。

Result: 在五个Amazon源域上训练并直接应用到十个未见目标域，ATLAS在大多数未见域上优于SOTA的序列式、图式、跨域、量化式和基于LLM的基线方法，平均HitRate相对提升24%。消融实验和表示分析验证了各组件有效性，并发现显著的源域多样性效应：增加源域异质性能大幅提升零样本迁移性能。

Conclusion: ATLAS建立了推荐域泛化作为零样本推荐的有前景范式，证明从多源异构域学习域不变表示可以泛化到完全未见的目标域，无需目标域适配或大规模语言模型预训练。

Abstract: Recommender systems remain domain-bound: a model trained on one interaction environment typically requires retraining or target-domain adaptation before it can operate on a new catalogue. A recommender trained on movies cannot be directly deployed to recommend groceries or video games. Existing approaches mitigate this by transferring restricted forms of recommendation knowledge, adapting to the target domain, or leveraging large language models (LLMs) for transferable representations. We instead ask whether recommendation-specific knowledge learned solely from multiple heterogeneous domains can generalize to entirely unseen domains without target-domain adaptation or language-model pretraining. We introduce ATLAS, a multi-source recommendation domain generalization framework that learns a shared, domain-invariant user-item representation from disjoint source domains, enabling zero-shot recommendation on unseen domains. ATLAS combines a Gromov-Wasserstein alignment that preserves how users relate to one another across domains, an adversarial objective that makes item representations indistinguishable across domains, and residual vector quantization (RVQ) codebooks that compress user and item embeddings into a discrete latent space, capturing hierarchical interaction patterns while suppressing domain-specific variation. Trained on five Amazon domains and applied directly to ten unseen domains, ATLAS outperforms state-of-the-art sequential, graph-based, cross-domain, quantization-based, and LLM-based baselines on most unseen domains, with an average relative gain in HitRate of 24%. Ablations and representation analyses validate each component, and we identify a pronounced source-domain diversity effect: increasing source heterogeneity substantially improves zero-shot transfer. ATLAS establishes recommendation domain generalization as a promising paradigm for zero-shot recommendation.

</details>
